<script>
/* Customer Planner (ported from Sorting app). Wrapped in an IIFE so its internal
   helpers never collide with Roster app globals. It still exposes
   window.loadCustomerTable / window.CustomerTab.onShow and patches window.showTab,
   so the existing Roster nav triggers it automatically. */
(function(){

(function(){
'use strict';

/* v24 cleanup: removed legacy delete-toast code and unused helper paths.
   Delete/edit for MOP continues to use CustomerID + ProductionDay + service scope. */

/* -- helpers first -- */
function el(id){ return document.getElementById(id); }
function esc(s){
  return String(s != null ? s : '')
    .replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
}
function lighten(hex, amount) {
  try {
    var c = hex.trim();
    if (c.indexOf('#') !== 0) return '#fff';
    if (c.length === 4) c = '#'+c[1]+c[1]+c[2]+c[2]+c[3]+c[3];
    var r=parseInt(c.slice(1,3),16),g=parseInt(c.slice(3,5),16),b=parseInt(c.slice(5,7),16);
    var mix=function(v){ return Math.round(v+(255-v)*amount); };
    return 'rgb('+mix(r)+','+mix(g)+','+mix(b)+')';
  } catch(e){ return '#fff'; }
}

function normCustomerName(s){ return String(s||'').trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ').toLowerCase(); }
function canonicalDayName(v){
  var n = String(v||'').trim().toLowerCase().slice(0,3);
  for (var i=0;i<DAY_ORDER.length;i++) if (DAY_ORDER[i].toLowerCase().slice(0,3)===n) return DAY_ORDER[i];
  return '';
}
function getRowBySheetRow(rowIndex){
  if (!_data || !_data.rows) return null;
  for (var i=0;i<_data.rows.length;i++) if (String(_data.rows[i].sheetRow)===String(rowIndex)) return _data.rows[i];
  return null;
}
/* -- state -- */
var DAY_ORDER  = ['Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday','Sunday'];
var CUSTOMER_WEEK_DAYS = ['Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday'];
var DAY_COLORS = {
  Monday:'cust-day-mon', Tuesday:'cust-day-tue', Wednesday:'cust-day-wed',
  Thursday:'cust-day-thu', Friday:'cust-day-fri', Saturday:'cust-day-sat', Sunday:'cust-day-sun'
};
var PRESETS = ['#00b050','#4d93d9','#fefe00','#ffc000','#9900cc','#f2ceef','#e561dc','#ef4444','#f97316','#64748b','#ffffff','#1e293b'];

var _data      = null;
var _viewMode  = 'planner';
var _listType  = 'clothes';  /* 'clothes' or 'mop' */
var _search    = '';
var _mode      = null;   // 'add' | 'edit'
var _editRow   = null;
var _dragSrc   = null;
var _loadedOnce = { clothes:false, mop:false };
/* Raw server responses only. Do not store normalized _data here. */
var _cacheByType = { clothes:null, mop:null };
var _loadSeq = 0;
var _prefetchingByType = { clothes:false, mop:false };
/* Local order overrides protect the UI from stale reloads while Apps Script/CentralDB catches up.
   Shape: { clothes:{ rowIndex: order }, mop:{ rowIndex: order } } */
var _localOrderOverrides = { clothes:{}, mop:{} };

/* Drag order autosave state.
   UX rule: drag must feel instant. We update the planner immediately, debounce
   the save, and if the user keeps dragging while a save is running we send only
   the latest order afterwards. Old save responses never overwrite newer moves. */
var _orderSave = {
  timer: null,
  inFlight: false,
  pending: null,
  pendingVersion: 0,
  lastStartedVersion: 0,
  failCount: 0
};

/* ============================================
   LOAD
   ============================================ */
function load(force) {
  force = !!force;
  var thisSeq = ++_loadSeq;
  if (!force && _loadedOnce[_listType] && _cacheByType[_listType]) {
    onLoaded(_cacheByType[_listType], true);
    return;
  }
  showMsg('<div class="cust-spinner"></div><span>Loading customers...</span>');
  hideViews();
  var fn = (_listType === 'mop') ? 'getMopData' : 'getCustomerData';
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(function(obj){
      // Ignore old reloads that return after a newer action/drag has already updated the screen.
      if (thisSeq !== _loadSeq) return;
      _loadedOnce[_listType] = true;
      _cacheByType[_listType] = obj;
      onLoaded(obj, false);
      // PERFORMANCE: warm the sibling CLOTHES/MOP list after first paint. The
      // backend reuses the same cached CustomerSchedule snapshot, so switching
      // list types becomes instant without delaying the visible screen.
      setTimeout(prefetchSiblingCustomerList, 0);
    })
    .withFailureHandler(function(e){ if (thisSeq === _loadSeq) showMsg('Error: '+(e&&e.message||e)); })
    [fn]();
}


function prefetchSiblingCustomerList(){
  var other = _listType === 'mop' ? 'clothes' : 'mop';
  if (_loadedOnce[other] || _cacheByType[other] || _prefetchingByType[other]) return;
  _prefetchingByType[other] = true;
  var generation = _loadSeq;
  var fn = other === 'mop' ? 'getMopData' : 'getCustomerData';
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(function(obj){
      _prefetchingByType[other] = false;
      // A save/delete/reload may have invalidated both caches while this background
      // request was in flight. Never repopulate the cache with that stale snapshot.
      if (generation !== _loadSeq) return;
      if (obj && obj.status !== 'error') {
        _loadedOnce[other] = true;
        _cacheByType[other] = obj;
      }
    })
    .withFailureHandler(function(){ _prefetchingByType[other] = false; })
    [fn]();
}

function onLoaded(obj) {
  if (obj && obj.status === 'error') { showMsg('Error: '+(obj.message||'Unknown')); return; }
  var raw = obj && obj.data;
  var rowIdx = obj && obj.rowIndices;
  if (!raw || raw.length < 2) { showMsg('No customers found.'); return; }

  var hdr = raw[0];
  var lh  = hdr.map(function(h){ return String(h||'').trim().toLowerCase(); });
  // Support both SortingArea and CentralDB CustomerSchedule column names
  function findCol(keys){ for(var k=0;k<keys.length;k++){ var j=lh.indexOf(keys[k]); if(j>=0) return j; } return -1; }
  var iDay          = findCol(['day','productionday']);
  var iName         = findCol(['customer','customername']);
  var iColor        = findCol(['routecolor','colour','color']);
  var iClothes      = findCol(['qtyclothes','qty clothes']);
  var iTrolley      = findCol(['trolleymop','trolley']);
  var iMops         = iTrolley;
  var iInfo         = findCol(['information']);
  var iClothesNotes = findCol(['clothesnotes']);
  var iMopNotes     = findCol(['mopinstructions']);
  var iMopProds     = findCol(['mopproducts']);
  var iDeliveryDay  = findCol(['deliveryday']);
  var iRoute        = findCol(['route']);
  var iDisplayName  = findCol(['displayname']);
  var iDeliveryOrder= findCol(['deliveryorder']);

  _data = {
    header: hdr, lh: lh, rowIndices: rowIdx, options: (obj && obj.options) ? obj.options : {},
    iDay:iDay, iName:iName, iColor:iColor, iClothes:iClothes, iMops:iMops,
    iTrolley:iTrolley, iInfo:iInfo, iClothesNotes:iClothesNotes, iMopNotes:iMopNotes,
    iDeliveryDay:iDeliveryDay, iRoute:iRoute, iDisplayName:iDisplayName, iDeliveryOrder:iDeliveryOrder,
    rows: raw.slice(1).map(function(r,i){
      return {
        sheetRow: rowIdx[i],
        day:     iDay     >= 0 ? String(r[iDay]    ||'').trim() : '',
        name:    iName    >= 0 ? String(r[iName]   ||'').trim() : '',
        color:   iColor   >= 0 ? String(r[iColor]  ||'').trim() : '',
        clothes:  iClothes  >= 0 ? String(r[iClothes] ||'').trim() : '',
        mops:     iMops     >= 0 ? String(r[iMops]    ||'').trim() : '',
        trolley:  iTrolley  >= 0 ? String(r[iTrolley] ||'').trim() : '',
        info:         iInfo         >= 0 ? String(r[iInfo]         ||'').trim() : '',
        clothesnotes: iClothesNotes >= 0 ? String(r[iClothesNotes] ||'').trim() : '',
        mopnotes:     iMopNotes      >= 0 ? String(r[iMopNotes]      ||'').trim() : '',
        mopprods:     iMopProds      >= 0 ? String(r[iMopProds]      ||'').trim() : '',
        deliveryDay:  iDeliveryDay   >= 0 ? String(r[iDeliveryDay]   ||'').trim() : '',
        route:        iRoute         >= 0 ? String(r[iRoute]         ||'').trim() : '',
        displayName:  iDisplayName   >= 0 ? String(r[iDisplayName]   ||'').trim() : '',
        deliveryOrder:iDeliveryOrder >= 0 ? String(r[iDeliveryOrder] ||'').trim() : '',
        serviceType:  (function(){ var si=lh.indexOf('servicetype'); return si>=0?String(r[si]||'').trim():''; })(),
        raw: r
      };
    }).filter(function(r){
      /* Keep every real CentralDB CustomerSchedule row.
         IMPORTANT: older versions hid names containing "HOUSEHOLD", which
         incorrectly removed valid laundry customers such as "NENAGH CNU HOUSEHOLD"
         from the Clothes planner even though the row existed in CentralDB with
         ClothesOrder/MopOrder. Only rows with no customer name are ignored. */
      return !!String(r.name || '').trim();
    })
  };
  applyLocalOrderOverridesToData(_listType);
  // Keep the Customer tab's full data separate. The Sorting Area sidebar
  // needs a normalized Customer/Day/RouteColor structure; using the raw
  // CentralDB headers here (CustomerName/ProductionDay) made the sidebar empty
  // after navigating away and back.
  window.customerFullTableData = { header:hdr, data:raw.slice(1), rowIndices:rowIdx };
  window.customerTableData = {
    header: ['Customer', 'Day', 'RouteColor'],
    data: raw.slice(1).map(function(r){
      return [
        iName  >= 0 ? r[iName]  : '',
        iDay   >= 0 ? r[iDay]   : '',
        iColor >= 0 ? r[iColor] : ''
      ];
    }),
    rowIndices: rowIdx
  };

  showMsg('');
  render();
}

/* ============================================
   RENDER
   ============================================ */
function render() {
  if (!_data) return;
  hideViews();
  if (_viewMode === 'planner') renderPlanner();
  else renderList();
}
function hideViews() {
  el('cust-planner-wrap').style.display = 'none';
  el('cust-list-wrap').style.display = 'none';
}
function showMsg(html) {
  var m = el('cust-msg');
  m.style.display = html ? '' : 'none';
  m.innerHTML = html;
}
function getOrderColumnIndexForType(type){
  var col = (String(type||_listType).toLowerCase()==='mop') ? 'moporder' : 'clothesorder';
  return (_data && _data.lh) ? _data.lh.indexOf(col) : -1;
}
function getProductionOrderValue(row){
  var idx = getOrderColumnIndexForType(_listType);
  if (idx < 0 || !row || !row.raw) return '';
  var n = Number(row.raw[idx]);
  return (isFinite(n) && n > 0) ? n : '';
}
function productionOrderBadge(row){
  var n = getProductionOrderValue(row);
  var day = String(row && row.day || '').trim();
  var listLabel = _listType === 'mop' ? 'MOP' : 'Clothes';
  var details = n ? ('Production order #' + n) : 'Production order missing';
  if (day) details += ' • ' + day;
  details += ' • ' + listLabel + ' list';
  return '<span class="cust-order-badge '+(n?'':'ca-db-order-missing')+'" title="'+esc(details)+'">'+(n||'!')+'</span>';
}
function applyLocalOrderOverridesToData(type){
  if (!_data || !_data.rows) return;
  var map = _localOrderOverrides[type || _listType] || {};
  var oIdx = getOrderColumnIndexForType(type || _listType);
  if (oIdx < 0) return;
  _data.rows.forEach(function(r){
    var key = String(r.sheetRow || '');
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(map, key) && r.raw) r.raw[oIdx] = map[key];
  });
}
function setLocalOrderOverrides(type, payload){
  type = type || _listType;
  if (!_localOrderOverrides[type]) _localOrderOverrides[type] = {};
  (payload || []).forEach(function(x){
    if (x && x.rowIndex) _localOrderOverrides[type][String(x.rowIndex)] = Number(x.order || 0);
  });
}
function clearLocalOrderOverrides(type, payload){
  type = type || _listType;
  if (!_localOrderOverrides[type]) return;
  (payload || []).forEach(function(x){ if (x && x.rowIndex) delete _localOrderOverrides[type][String(x.rowIndex)]; });
}
function invalidateCustomerCaches(){
  _loadedOnce.clothes = false; _loadedOnce.mop = false;
  _cacheByType.clothes = null; _cacheByType.mop = null;
  _prefetchingByType.clothes = false; _prefetchingByType.mop = false;
  _loadSeq++;
}
function invalidateDistributionPlannerCache(){
  /* The Routes screen has its own short-lived delivery cache. Customer edits can
     change DeliveryDay / Route / DeliveryOrder, so force the next Routes render
     to fetch fresh CentralDB data instead of reusing a still-valid local snapshot. */
  try {
    if (typeof App !== 'undefined' && App) App._plLoadedAt = 0;
  } catch(e) {}
}
/* Routes can change DeliveryOrder without reloading this Customer IIFE. Expose
   one narrow invalidation hook so the next Customer load cannot reuse stale rows. */
try {
  window.invalidateDistributionCustomerCache = function(){ invalidateCustomerCaches(); };
} catch(e) {}

/* ============================================
   PLANNER
   ============================================ */
function renderPlanner() {
  var wrap = el('cust-planner-wrap');
  var srch = _search.toLowerCase();

  var byDay = {};
  DAY_ORDER.forEach(function(d){ byDay[d] = []; });
  _data.rows.forEach(function(r){
    var key = null;
    for (var di=0; di<DAY_ORDER.length; di++) {
      if (DAY_ORDER[di].toLowerCase().slice(0,3) === r.day.toLowerCase().slice(0,3)) { key=DAY_ORDER[di]; break; }
    }
    if (!key) return;
    if (srch) {
      var hay = [r.name, r.day, r.deliveryDay, r.route, r.deliveryOrder].join(' ').toLowerCase();
      if (hay.indexOf(srch) < 0) return;
    }
    byDay[key].push(r);
  });

  /* Sort each day by ClothesOrder / MopOrder so position changes show immediately */
  var lhR = (_data&&_data.lh)||[];
  var orderColR = (_listType==='mop') ? 'moporder' : 'clothesorder';
  var oIdxR = lhR.indexOf(orderColR);
  DAY_ORDER.forEach(function(d){
    if (!byDay[d].length) return;
    byDay[d].sort(function(a, b){
      var va = oIdxR>=0 && a.raw ? a.raw[oIdxR] : '';
      var vb = oIdxR>=0 && b.raw ? b.raw[oIdxR] : '';
      var oa = (va !== '' && va != null && !isNaN(Number(va))) ? Number(va) : 9999;
      var ob = (vb !== '' && vb != null && !isNaN(Number(vb))) ? Number(vb) : 9999;
      if(oa === ob) return 0;
      return oa - ob;
    });
  });

  var activeDays = DAY_ORDER.filter(function(d){ return byDay[d].length > 0; });
  if (!activeDays.length) {
    showMsg(srch ? 'No results for "'+esc(srch)+'".' : 'No customers found.');
    return;
  }

  var maxRows = 0;
  activeDays.forEach(function(d){ if (byDay[d].length > maxRows) maxRows = byDay[d].length; });

  var html = '<table id="cust-planner">';
  html += '<colgroup>';
  activeDays.forEach(function(){
    html += '<col class="cg-name"><col class="cg-'+(_listType==='mop'?'trolley':'clothes')+'"><col class="cg-act">';
  });
  html += '</colgroup>';
  html += '<thead><tr class="cust-day-row">';
  activeDays.forEach(function(day){
    html += '<th colspan="3" class="'+(DAY_COLORS[day]||'')+'">' +day.toUpperCase()+'</th>';
  });
  html += '</tr><tr class="cust-sub-row">';
  activeDays.forEach(function(day){
    var c = DAY_COLORS[day]||'';
    html += '<th class="'+c+' cust-col-name">CUSTOMER</th>'
      +(_listType==='mop' ? '<th class="'+c+' cust-col-trolley">TROLLEY</th>' : '<th class="'+c+' cust-col-clothes">CLOTHES</th>')
      +'<th class="'+c+' cust-col-act"></th>';
  });
  html += '</tr></thead><tbody>';

  for (var i=0; i<maxRows; i++) {
    html += '<tr>';
    activeDays.forEach(function(day){
      var r  = byDay[day][i];
      var bg = (r && r.color) ? lighten(r.color,.83) : '#fff';
      if (r) {
        var orderBadge = productionOrderBadge(r);
        var dot = r.color ? '<span class="cust-dot" style="background:'+r.color+'"></span>' : '';
        var cz = (!r.clothes||r.clothes==='0') ? ' zero' : '';
        var mz = (!r.mops   ||r.mops   ==='0') ? ' zero' : '';
        html +=
          '<td class="cust-td-name cust-col-name" style="background:'+bg+'"'+
            ' data-row="'+r.sheetRow+'" data-day="'+esc(day)+'" data-idx="'+i+'"'+
            ' title="Click to edit">'+orderBadge+dot+esc(r.name)+'</td>'+
          (_listType==='mop'
            ? '<td class="cust-td-num cust-col-trolley" style="background:'+bg+'">'+esc(r.trolley||'')+'</td>'
            : '<td class="cust-td-num cust-col-clothes'+cz+'" style="background:'+bg+'">'+esc(r.clothes||'0')+'</td>'
          )+
          '<td class="cust-td-act cust-col-act" style="background:'+bg+'">'+
            '<div class="cust-act-wrap">'+
              '<button class="cust-ic-btn cust-ic-move" draggable="true"'+
                ' data-row="'+r.sheetRow+'" data-day="'+esc(day)+'" data-idx="'+i+'" title="Drag to reorder the production list. This changes ClothesOrder or MopOrder only.">&#8942;</button>'+
              '<button class="cust-ic-btn cust-ic-edit" data-row="'+r.sheetRow+'" title="Edit">&#9999;</button>'+
              '<button class="cust-ic-btn cust-ic-del"  data-row="'+r.sheetRow+'" data-name="'+esc(r.name)+'" title="Delete">&#10005;</button>'+
            '</div></td>';
      } else {
        html += '<td class="cust-td-empty" colspan="3" style="background:#fafafa"></td>';
      }
    });
    html += '</tr>';
  }
  html += '</tbody></table>';

  wrap.innerHTML = html;
  wrap.style.display = '';
  showMsg('');
  bindPlanner(wrap, byDay);
  /* Update scroll arrows after table is painted */
  setTimeout(function(){
    if (typeof window._custUpdateScrollArrows === 'function') window._custUpdateScrollArrows();
  }, 50);
}

function bindPlanner(wrap, byDay) {
  var i, btns;
  btns = wrap.querySelectorAll('.cust-td-name');
  for (i=0; i<btns.length; i++) {
    btns[i].addEventListener('click', (function(b){ return function(){ openModal('edit', b.getAttribute('data-row')); }; })(btns[i]));
  }
  btns = wrap.querySelectorAll('.cust-ic-edit');
  for (i=0; i<btns.length; i++) {
    btns[i].addEventListener('click', (function(b){ return function(ev){ ev.stopPropagation(); openModal('edit', b.getAttribute('data-row')); }; })(btns[i]));
  }
  btns = wrap.querySelectorAll('.cust-ic-del');
  for (i=0; i<btns.length; i++) {
    btns[i].addEventListener('click', (function(b){ return function(ev){ ev.stopPropagation(); askDelete(b.getAttribute('data-row'), b.getAttribute('data-name')); }; })(btns[i]));
  }
  bindDrag(wrap, byDay);
}


function queueOrderSave(payload, listType, dayLabel) {
  payload = Array.isArray(payload) ? payload.filter(function(x){ return x && x.rowIndex; }) : [];
  if (!payload.length) {
    showSyncToast('Order updated', 'ok');
    hideSyncToast(650);
    return;
  }

  _orderSave.pending = {
    payload: payload,
    listType: listType,
    dayLabel: dayLabel || ''
  };
  _orderSave.pendingVersion++;
  setLocalOrderOverrides(listType, payload);

  showSyncToast('Order updated locally • saving...', '');

  if (_orderSave.timer) clearTimeout(_orderSave.timer);
  _orderSave.timer = setTimeout(flushOrderSaveQueue, 650);
}

function flushOrderSaveQueue() {
  if (_orderSave.inFlight) return;
  if (!_orderSave.pending) return;

  var job = _orderSave.pending;
  var version = _orderSave.pendingVersion;
  _orderSave.pending = null;
  _orderSave.inFlight = true;
  _orderSave.lastStartedVersion = version;

  showSyncToast('Saving order in background...', '');

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(function(res){
      _orderSave.inFlight = false;

      if (res && res.status === 'error') {
        _orderSave.failCount++;
        showSyncToast('Order not saved yet • retrying...', 'err');
        if (!(_orderSave.pending)) {
          _orderSave.pending = job;
          _orderSave.pendingVersion++;
        }
        if (_orderSave.timer) clearTimeout(_orderSave.timer);
        _orderSave.timer = setTimeout(flushOrderSaveQueue, Math.min(2000 + (_orderSave.failCount * 1000), 7000));
        return;
      }

      _orderSave.failCount = 0;

      if (_orderSave.pending) {
        showSyncToast('Saving latest order...', '');
        setTimeout(flushOrderSaveQueue, 60);
      } else {
        clearLocalOrderOverrides(job.listType, job.payload);
        showSyncToast('Order saved', 'ok');
        hideSyncToast(700);
      }
    })
    .withFailureHandler(function(e){
      _orderSave.inFlight = false;
      _orderSave.failCount++;
      showSyncToast('Order not saved yet • retrying...', 'err');
      if (!(_orderSave.pending)) {
        _orderSave.pending = job;
        _orderSave.pendingVersion++;
      }
      if (_orderSave.timer) clearTimeout(_orderSave.timer);
      _orderSave.timer = setTimeout(flushOrderSaveQueue, Math.min(2000 + (_orderSave.failCount * 1000), 7000));
    })
    .updateCustomerOrderBatch(job.payload, job.listType);
}

function bindDrag(wrap, byDay) {
  var i, moveBtns, trs;

  moveBtns = wrap.querySelectorAll('.cust-ic-move');
  for (i=0; i<moveBtns.length; i++) {
    moveBtns[i].addEventListener('dragstart', (function(b){
      return function(ev){
        _dragSrc = {
          row: b.getAttribute('data-row'),   /* sheetRow is a string e.g. "CID-KINS-A4C10::Monday" */
          day: b.getAttribute('data-day'),
          idx: Number(b.getAttribute('data-idx'))
        };
        b.closest('tr').classList.add('cust-dragging');
        ev.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
      };
    })(moveBtns[i]));

    moveBtns[i].addEventListener('dragend', (function(b){
      return function(){
        var tr = b.closest('tr');
        if (tr) tr.classList.remove('cust-dragging');
        wrap.querySelectorAll('.cust-drag-over').forEach(function(r){ r.classList.remove('cust-drag-over'); });
        _dragSrc = null;
      };
    })(moveBtns[i]));
  }

  trs = wrap.querySelectorAll('tbody tr');
  for (i=0; i<trs.length; i++) {
    trs[i].addEventListener('dragover', (function(tr){
      return function(ev){
        if (!_dragSrc) return;
        ev.preventDefault();
        wrap.querySelectorAll('.cust-drag-over').forEach(function(r){ r.classList.remove('cust-drag-over'); });
        tr.classList.add('cust-drag-over');
      };
    })(trs[i]));

    trs[i].addEventListener('drop', (function(tr){
      return function(ev){
        ev.preventDefault();
        wrap.querySelectorAll('.cust-drag-over').forEach(function(r){ r.classList.remove('cust-drag-over'); });
        if (!_dragSrc) return;

        /* -- Find target customer in same day column -- */
        var tds = tr.querySelectorAll('.cust-td-name');
        var targetRow = null;
        for (var j=0; j<tds.length; j++) {
          if (tds[j].getAttribute('data-day') === _dragSrc.day) { targetRow = tds[j].getAttribute('data-row'); break; }
        }

        var dayCustomers = byDay[_dragSrc.day] || [];
        var srcIdx = _dragSrc.idx, tgtIdx = -1;
        if (targetRow) {
          for (var k=0; k<dayCustomers.length; k++) {
            if (String(dayCustomers[k].sheetRow) === String(targetRow)) { tgtIdx=k; break; }
          }
        }

        var srcRow = _dragSrc.row;
        var _dragSrc_day_local = _dragSrc.day;
        _dragSrc = null;

        if (tgtIdx < 0 || tgtIdx === srcIdx) return;

        /* ── Collect full day list (sorted as currently displayed) ── */
        var lhDrag      = (_data&&_data.lh)||[];
        var orderColDrag= (_listType==='mop') ? 'moporder' : 'clothesorder';
        var oIdxDrag    = lhDrag.indexOf(orderColDrag);
        var updateFnDrag= (_listType==='mop') ? 'updateMop' : 'updateCustomer';

        /* dayCustomers is already sorted by the last render (byDay was sorted) */
        /* Build a mutable copy we can reorder */
        var dayList = dayCustomers.slice();

        /* Remove src from its current position, insert at target */
        var srcItem = dayList.splice(srcIdx, 1)[0];
        dayList.splice(tgtIdx, 0, srcItem);

        /* ── Update raw order values in memory and save the full day order.
           Sending the complete day order is safer than sending only changed rows:
           if an older reload or partial write happens later, the next queued save
           still has the whole final order and can restore the exact planner state. */
        var orderPayload = [];
        for (var pi=0; pi<dayList.length; pi++){
          var newOrder = pi + 1;
          if(oIdxDrag>=0 && dayList[pi].raw) dayList[pi].raw[oIdxDrag] = newOrder;
          orderPayload.push({ rowIndex: dayList[pi].sheetRow, order: newOrder });
        }

        /* ── Optimistic re-render: replace rows for this day in _data.rows.
           The user sees the new order instantly and can keep dragging while the
           background queue saves the latest state. */
        var otherRows = (_data.rows||[]).filter(function(row){
          var rday = row.day ? row.day.toLowerCase().slice(0,3) : '';
          var sday = _dragSrc_day_local ? _dragSrc_day_local.toLowerCase().slice(0,3) : '';
          return rday !== sday;
        });
        _data.rows = otherRows.concat(dayList);
        render();

        /* ── Flash the moved row ── */
        setTimeout(function(){
          var movedTds = el('cust-planner-wrap') && el('cust-planner-wrap').querySelectorAll('.cust-td-name');
          if (!movedTds) return;
          for (var m=0; m<movedTds.length; m++) {
            if (String(movedTds[m].getAttribute('data-row')) === String(srcRow)) {
              var movedTr = movedTds[m].closest('tr');
              if (movedTr) {
                movedTr.classList.add('cust-moved');
                setTimeout(function(t){ return function(){ t.classList.remove('cust-moved'); }; }(movedTr), 700);
              }
              break;
            }
          }
        }, 30);

        queueOrderSave(orderPayload, _listType, _dragSrc_day_local);
      };
    })(trs[i]));
  }
}

/* ============================================
   LIST VIEW
   ============================================ */
function renderList() {
  var wrap = el('cust-list-wrap');
  var srch = _search.toLowerCase();
  var filtered = _data.rows.filter(function(r){
    if (!srch) return true;
    return r.name.toLowerCase().indexOf(srch) >= 0 || r.day.toLowerCase().indexOf(srch) >= 0;
  });
  if (!filtered.length) {
    wrap.innerHTML = '<p style="text-align:center;color:#94a3b8;padding:32px">No customers match.</p>';
    wrap.style.display = ''; showMsg(''); return;
  }
  var html = '<div style="border-radius:12px;overflow:hidden;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.06)">'+
    '<table class="cust-list-table"><thead><tr>';
  _data.header.forEach(function(h){ html += '<th>'+esc(h)+'</th>'; });
  html += '<th style="text-align:center;width:90px">Actions</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
  filtered.forEach(function(r){
    html += '<tr>';
    r.raw.forEach(function(cell,idx){
      if (_data.lh[idx]==='customer' || _data.lh[idx]==='customername') {
        var dot = r.color ? '<span class="cust-dot" style="background:'+r.color+'"></span>' : '';
        html += '<td>'+dot+esc(cell)+'</td>';
      } else if (_data.lh[idx]==='deliveryorder') {
        var dn = Number(cell);
        var dd = String(r.deliveryDay||'').trim();
        var rt = String(r.route||'').trim();
        var dt = (dn>0?'Delivery order #'+dn:'Delivery order missing') + (dd?' • '+dd:'') + (rt?' • Route '+rt:'');
        html += '<td style="text-align:center">'+((dn>0||dd)?'<span class="cust-order-badge '+(dn>0?'':'ca-db-order-missing')+'" title="'+esc(dt)+'">'+(dn>0?dn:'!')+'</span>':'')+'</td>';
      } else if (_data.lh[idx]==='routecolor') {
        var circ = cell ? '<span style="display:inline-block;width:14px;height:14px;border-radius:50%;background:'+esc(cell)+';border:1px solid #0003;vertical-align:middle"></span>' : '';
        html += '<td style="text-align:center">'+circ+'</td>';
      } else {
        html += '<td>'+esc(cell)+'</td>';
      }
    });
    html += '<td style="text-align:center;white-space:nowrap">'+
      '<button class="cust-ic-btn cust-ic-edit" data-row="'+r.sheetRow+'" style="margin-right:4px">&#9999;</button>'+
      '<button class="cust-ic-btn cust-ic-del"  data-row="'+r.sheetRow+'" data-name="'+esc(r.name)+'">&#10005;</button>'+
      '</td></tr>';
  });
  html += '</tbody></table></div>';
  wrap.innerHTML = html; wrap.style.display = ''; showMsg('');
  var i, btns;
  btns = wrap.querySelectorAll('.cust-ic-edit');
  for (i=0; i<btns.length; i++) {
    btns[i].addEventListener('click', (function(b){ return function(){ openModal('edit',b.getAttribute('data-row')); }; })(btns[i]));
  }
  btns = wrap.querySelectorAll('.cust-ic-del');
  for (i=0; i<btns.length; i++) {
    btns[i].addEventListener('click', (function(b){ return function(){ askDelete(b.getAttribute('data-row'),b.getAttribute('data-name')); }; })(btns[i]));
  }
}

/* ============================================
   CUSTOMER STUDIO — modal logic
   ============================================ */

var DAY_COLORS_STUDIO = {
  Monday:'#1e40af', Tuesday:'#6d28d9', Wednesday:'#92400e',
  Thursday:'#065f46', Friday:'#9f1239', Saturday:'#1e3a5f', Sunday:'#374151'
};
/* Canonical route colours used operationally in Distribution/Sorting.
   Keep this mapping explicit: the Customer Studio palette must match the values
   already stored in CustomerSchedule rather than offering unrelated defaults. */
var ROUTE_COLOR_BY_ROUTE = {
  '610':'#00b050',
  '611':'#4d93d9',
  '612':'#e561dc',
  '613':'#9900cc',
  '614':'#ffffff',
  '615':'#fefe00',
  '616':'#ffc000',
  '617':'#f2ceef',
  '618':'#ff0000'
};
var STUDIO_PALETTE = Object.keys(ROUTE_COLOR_BY_ROUTE).map(function(route){
  return ROUTE_COLOR_BY_ROUTE[route];
});

function csNormalizeHex(value){
  var c = String(value||'').trim();
  if (/^[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(c)) c = '#'+c;
  return /^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(c) ? c.toLowerCase() : '';
}
function csRouteKey(value){ return String(value||'').trim(); }
function csAppRouteMeta(route){
  var key = csRouteKey(route);
  if(!key) return null;
  try {
    var appObj = (typeof App !== 'undefined' && App && App.D) ? App : null;
    var routes = (appObj && Array.isArray(appObj.D.routes)) ? appObj.D.routes : [];
    for(var i=0;i<routes.length;i++){
      if(String(routes[i].route||'').trim()===key) return routes[i];
    }
  } catch(e) {}
  return null;
}
function csRouteColorForRoute(route){
  var key = csRouteKey(route);
  if(ROUTE_COLOR_BY_ROUTE[key]) return ROUTE_COLOR_BY_ROUTE[key];
  var meta = csAppRouteMeta(key);
  return csNormalizeHex(meta && (meta.colour || meta.color || meta.RouteColor));
}
function csRouteDisplayName(route){
  var meta = csAppRouteMeta(route);
  return meta ? String(meta.displayName || meta.DisplayName || '').trim() : '';
}
function csRouteLabelForColor(color){
  var c = csNormalizeHex(color);
  var routes = [];
  Object.keys(ROUTE_COLOR_BY_ROUTE).forEach(function(route){
    if(csNormalizeHex(ROUTE_COLOR_BY_ROUTE[route])===c) routes.push(route);
  });
  return routes.length ? ('Route '+routes.join('/')+' · '+String(color).toUpperCase()) : String(color).toUpperCase();
}

function csNormName(s){ return String(s||'').trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ').toLowerCase(); }
function csCanonDay(v){
  var n=String(v||'').trim().toLowerCase().slice(0,3);
  var days=['Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday','Sunday'];
  for(var i=0;i<days.length;i++) if(days[i].toLowerCase().slice(0,3)===n) return days[i];
  return '';
}
function csFindRow(rowIndex){
  if(!_data||!_data.rows) return null;
  for(var i=0;i<_data.rows.length;i++)
    if(String(_data.rows[i].sheetRow)===String(rowIndex)) return _data.rows[i];
  return null;
}
function csCustomerId(r){
  if(!r) return '';
  var idIdx = (_data&&_data.lh) ? _data.lh.indexOf('customerid') : -1;
  if(idIdx>=0 && r.raw){
    var fromCol = String(r.raw[idIdx]||'').trim();
    if(fromCol) return fromCol;
  }
  var parts = String(r.sheetRow||'').split('::');
  return String(parts[0]||'').trim();
}
function csOccurrences(rowIndex){
  var base=csFindRow(rowIndex);
  if(!base) return [];
  var customerId = csCustomerId(base);
  if(customerId){
    return _data.rows.filter(function(r){ return csCustomerId(r)===customerId; });
  }
  var key=csNormName(base.name);
  return _data.rows.filter(function(r){ return !csCustomerId(r) && csNormName(r.name)===key; });
}
function csQtyLabel(){ return _listType==='mop' ? 'Trolley / Qty' : 'Qty Clothes'; }
function csQtyVal(r){
  if(_listType==='mop') return r.trolley||r.mops||'';
  return r.clothes||'';
}
function csInfoVal(r){
  // Strict separation: Clothes tab → ClothesNotes only; MOP tab → MopInstructions only.
  // Never mix the two even for CLOTHES + MOP customers.
  if(_listType==='mop') return r.mopnotes||'';
  return r.clothesnotes||'';
}
function csDayCount(day){
  return (_data&&_data.rows||[]).filter(function(r){ return csCanonDay(r.day)===day; }).length;
}
function csDayPosition(r, day){
  // Prefer the actual order stored in DB (ClothesOrder or MopOrder)
  if(r && r.raw && _data && _data.lh){
    var orderCol = (_listType==='mop') ? 'moporder' : 'clothesorder';
    var oIdx = _data.lh.indexOf(orderCol);
    if(oIdx>=0){
      var stored = Number(r.raw[oIdx]);
      if(stored > 0) return stored;
    }
  }
  // Fallback: position by index in day list
  var rows=(_data&&_data.rows||[]).filter(function(row){ return csCanonDay(row.day)===day; });
  var idx=-1;
  for(var i=0;i<rows.length;i++) if(String(rows[i].sheetRow)===String(r&&r.sheetRow)){ idx=i; break; }
  return idx>=0 ? idx+1 : rows.length+1;
}

/* Open Customer Studio for edit */
function openModal(mode, rowIndex) {
  if (mode === 'add') { openAddModal(); return; }
  if (!_data) return;
  _mode = 'edit'; _editRow = rowIndex || null;

  var baseRow = csFindRow(rowIndex);
  if (!baseRow) return;
  var occ = csOccurrences(rowIndex);
  var uniqueDays = [];
  occ.forEach(function(r){
    var d=csCanonDay(r.day);
    if(d && uniqueDays.indexOf(d)<0) uniqueDays.push(d);
  });
  var DAY_ORDER_S = ['Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday','Sunday'];
  uniqueDays.sort(function(a,b){ return DAY_ORDER_S.indexOf(a)-DAY_ORDER_S.indexOf(b); });

  var totalQty = occ.reduce(function(s,r){
    return s+(parseFloat(String(csQtyVal(r)).replace(',','.'))||0);
  }, 0);
  var displayDays = uniqueDays.map(function(d){ return d.slice(0,3); }).join(' \u2022 ') || 'No days';
  // Find the first non-empty color across all occurrences of this customer
  var baseColor = (function(){
    if(baseRow.color && baseRow.color.trim()) return baseRow.color.trim();
    for(var i=0;i<occ.length;i++){
      if(occ[i].color && occ[i].color.trim()) return occ[i].color.trim();
    }
    return '#0ea5a6';
  })();

  var body = el('cust-modal-body');
  body.innerHTML = '';

  /* Hero */
  var hero = document.createElement('div');
  hero.className = 'cs-hero';
  hero.innerHTML =
    '<div class="cs-hero-info">'+
      '<div class="cs-hero-name">'+
        '<span class="cs-hero-dot" style="background:'+esc(baseColor)+';border-color:'+esc(baseColor)+'40"></span>'+
        esc(baseRow.name||'')+'</div>'+
      '<div class="cs-hero-sub">Production days: <strong>'+esc(displayDays)+'</strong>. '+
        'Turn days ON/OFF and edit each active day. OFF cards stay collapsed.</div>'+
    '</div>'+
    '<div class="cs-kpis">'+
      '<div class="cs-kpi"><b>'+uniqueDays.length+'</b><span>Days</span></div>'+
      '<div class="cs-kpi"><b>'+occ.length+'</b><span>Rows</span></div>'+
      '<div class="cs-kpi"><b>'+(totalQty||'\u2014')+'</b><span>'+esc(csQtyLabel())+'</span></div>'+
    '</div>';
  body.appendChild(hero);

  /* No mode selector — always use week planner */

  /* Day board */
  var board = document.createElement('div');
  board.className = 'cs-day-board';

  var WEEK_DAYS = ['Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday'];
  WEEK_DAYS.forEach(function(day){
    var r = null;
    for(var i=0;i<occ.length;i++) if(csCanonDay(occ[i].day)===day){ r=occ[i]; break; }
    var on = !!r;
    var raw = r||baseRow;
    var storedColor = String(raw.color||'').trim();
    var curColor = storedColor||baseColor;       /* visual fallback only */
    var colorValue = r ? storedColor : curColor; /* preserve blank existing rows */
    var bgColor = DAY_COLORS_STUDIO[day]||'#374151';

    var card = document.createElement('div');
    card.className = 'cs-day-card'+(on?'':' cs-off');
    card.dataset.dayCard = day;

    /* Head */
    var head = document.createElement('div');
    head.className = 'cs-day-head';
    head.style.background = bgColor;
    head.innerHTML =
      '<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;cursor:pointer">'+
        '<input type="checkbox" class="cs-day-on" data-day="'+day+'"'+(on?' checked':'')+'>'+
        '\u00a0'+day.slice(0,3).toUpperCase()+
      '</label>'+
      '<span class="cs-day-badge cs-day-status">'+(on?'ON LIST':'OFF')+'</span>';
    card.appendChild(head);

    /* Body */
    var cardBody = document.createElement('div');
    cardBody.className = 'cs-day-body';

    /* Qty */
    var qtyDiv = document.createElement('div');
    qtyDiv.innerHTML =
      '<div class="cs-day-label">'+esc(csQtyLabel())+'</div>'+
      '<input class="cs-day-input" data-day="'+day+'" data-day-field="qty" value="'+esc(csQtyVal(raw))+'" placeholder="0" title="'+esc(csQtyVal(raw))+'">';
    cardBody.appendChild(qtyDiv);

    /* Info (MOP Instructions or Clothes Notes) */
    var infoDiv = document.createElement('div');
    infoDiv.innerHTML =
      '<div class="cs-day-label">'+(_listType==='mop'?'MOP Instructions':'Clothes Notes')+'</div>'+
      '<input class="cs-day-input" data-day="'+day+'" data-day-field="info" value="'+esc(csInfoVal(raw))+'" placeholder="'+(_listType==='mop'?'MOP instructions':'Notes / route info')+'" title="'+esc(csInfoVal(raw))+'">';
    cardBody.appendChild(infoDiv);

    /* MOP Products multi-select + DeliveryDay/Route per day card */
    if (_listType === 'mop') {
      var prodsDiv = document.createElement('div');
      var iMopProdsLocal = (_data&&_data.lh) ? _data.lh.indexOf('mopproducts') : -1;
      var prodsRaw = (iMopProdsLocal>=0 && raw.raw) ? String(raw.raw[iMopProdsLocal]||'') : (raw.mopprods||'');
      var prodsSelected = prodsRaw.split(';').map(function(s){ return s.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
      var MOP_OPTS = ['STANDARD POCKET MOPS','GREY STRIPED WHITE MOPS','GREY STRIPED WHITE MOP','SPIN MOP','CLEANING CLOTHS','VELCRO MOP','SCRUB SUITS','TWISTER MOP','WHITE MOP','BAG'];
      prodsSelected.forEach(function(v){ if(v && MOP_OPTS.indexOf(v)<0) MOP_OPTS.push(v); });
      var selSize = Math.min(MOP_OPTS.length, 5);
      var optHtml = MOP_OPTS.map(function(p){
        var isSel = prodsSelected.some(function(s){ return s.trim().toUpperCase()===p.toUpperCase(); });
        return '<option value="'+esc(p)+'"'+(isSel?' selected':'')+'>'+esc(p)+'</option>';
      }).join('');
      prodsDiv.innerHTML =
        '<div class="cs-day-label">MOP Products <span style="font-weight:400;text-transform:none;font-size:9px;color:#64748b">(hold Ctrl to multi-select)</span></div>'+
        '<select class="cs-mop-select" data-day="'+day+'" data-day-field="mopprods" multiple size="'+selSize+'">'+optHtml+'</select>';
      cardBody.appendChild(prodsDiv);
    }

    /* DeliveryDay + Route + DisplayName are per CustomerSchedule occurrence.
       Existing blank values are preserved exactly; defaults are inherited only for a
       newly enabled day so an unrelated comment edit cannot manufacture route data. */
    (function(){
      var lhD    = (_data&&_data.lh)||[];
      var rIdxD   = lhD.indexOf('route');
      var ddIdxD  = lhD.indexOf('deliveryday');
      var doIdxD  = lhD.indexOf('deliveryorder');
      var dnIdxD  = lhD.indexOf('displayname');
      var routeD  = (rIdxD>=0  && raw.raw) ? String(raw.raw[rIdxD]||'')  : '';
      var delDayD = (ddIdxD>=0 && raw.raw) ? String(raw.raw[ddIdxD]||'') : '';
      var delOrdD = (doIdxD>=0 && raw.raw) ? String(raw.raw[doIdxD]||'') : '';
      var dispD   = (dnIdxD>=0 && raw.raw) ? String(raw.raw[dnIdxD]||'') : '';
      if(!routeD && !r && rIdxD>=0){
        for(var rr=0; rr<occ.length; rr++){
          if(occ[rr].raw && String(occ[rr].raw[rIdxD]||'').trim()){
            routeD = String(occ[rr].raw[rIdxD]||'').trim();
            break;
          }
        }
      }
      if(!delDayD && !r){
        var P2D={Monday:'Tuesday',Tuesday:'Wednesday',Wednesday:'Thursday',
                 Thursday:'Friday',Friday:'Saturday',Saturday:'Monday',Sunday:'Monday'};
        delDayD = P2D[day]||'';
      }
      if(!dispD && !r && routeD) dispD = csRouteDisplayName(routeD);
      var routeDerivedName = csRouteDisplayName(routeD);
      var ddRouteDiv = document.createElement('div');
      ddRouteDiv.style.cssText = 'display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:6px;';
      ddRouteDiv.innerHTML =
        '<div><div class="cs-day-label">Delivery Day</div>'+ 
          '<input class="cs-day-input" data-day="'+day+'" data-day-field="deliveryday" value="'+esc(delDayD)+'" title="'+esc(delDayD)+'" placeholder="Delivery day"></div>'+ 
        '<div><div class="cs-day-label">Route</div>'+ 
          '<input class="cs-day-input" data-day="'+day+'" data-day-field="route" value="'+esc(routeD)+'" title="'+esc(routeD)+'" placeholder="Route number"></div>'+ 
        '<div><div class="cs-day-label">Delivery Order</div>'+ 
          '<input type="number" min="1" class="cs-day-input" data-day="'+day+'" data-day-field="deliveryorder" value="'+esc(delOrdD)+'" title="'+esc(delOrdD)+'" placeholder="Delivery position"></div>'+ 
        '<div><div class="cs-day-label">Display Name</div>'+ 
          '<input class="cs-day-input" data-day="'+day+'" data-day-field="displayname" data-route-derived-name="'+esc(routeDerivedName)+'" value="'+esc(dispD)+'" title="'+esc(dispD)+'" placeholder="Route display name"></div>';
      cardBody.appendChild(ddRouteDiv);
    })();

    /* Route colour */
    var colorDiv = document.createElement('div');
    var palette = buildCsPalette(curColor);
    colorDiv.innerHTML =
      '<div class="cs-day-label">Route Colour</div>'+
      '<input type="hidden" data-day="'+day+'" data-day-field="color" value="'+esc(colorValue)+'">'+
      '<div class="cs-color-row">'+
        '<div class="cs-color-swatch" style="background:'+esc(curColor)+'" data-swatch="'+day+'" title="Click to change colour">'+
          '<input type="color" value="'+esc(curColor)+'" data-color-picker="'+day+'">'+
        '</div>'+
        '<span class="cs-color-code" data-color-code="'+day+'" data-color-toggle="'+day+'" title="Click to pick a colour">'+esc(curColor)+'</span>'+
      '</div>'+
      '<div class="cs-palette" data-palette="'+day+'">'+
        palette.map(function(c){
          return '<button type="button" class="cs-palette-swatch'+(c.toLowerCase()===curColor.toLowerCase()?' selected':'')+'"'+
            ' data-day="'+day+'" data-color="'+esc(c)+'" style="background:'+esc(c)+'"'+
            ' title="'+esc(csRouteLabelForColor(c))+'"></button>';
        }).join('')+
      '</div>';
    cardBody.appendChild(colorDiv);

    /* Position. Existing rows with a missing DB order stay blank; do not
       auto-repair them while saving an unrelated field. */
    var posDiv = document.createElement('div');
    var orderFieldKey = (_listType==='mop') ? 'moporder' : 'clothesorder';
    var orderFieldIdx = (_data&&_data.lh) ? _data.lh.indexOf(orderFieldKey) : -1;
    var storedPosition = (r && orderFieldIdx>=0 && r.raw) ? String(r.raw[orderFieldIdx]||'').trim() : '';
    var positionValue = r ? storedPosition : String(csDayPosition(r,day));
    posDiv.innerHTML =
      '<div class="cs-day-label">Position in this day</div>'+ 
      '<input type="number" class="cs-day-input" data-day="'+day+'" data-day-field="position"'+
        ' min="1" max="'+(csDayCount(day)+(on?0:1))+'" value="'+esc(positionValue)+'">';
    cardBody.appendChild(posDiv);

    card.appendChild(cardBody);
    board.appendChild(card);
  });
  body.appendChild(board);

  /* Event bindings */
  board.querySelectorAll('.cs-day-on').forEach(function(cb){
    cb.addEventListener('change', function(){
      var card = cb.closest('.cs-day-card');
      if(card) card.classList.toggle('cs-off', !cb.checked);
      var st = card && card.querySelector('.cs-day-status');
      if(st) st.textContent = cb.checked ? 'ON LIST' : 'OFF';
    });
  });

  /* Click OFF header to toggle ON */
  board.querySelectorAll('.cs-day-card.cs-off .cs-day-head').forEach(function(h){
    h.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      if(e.target && e.target.matches('input')) return;
      var cb = h.querySelector('.cs-day-on');
      if(cb){ cb.checked=true; cb.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true})); }
    });
  });

  /* Native color picker */
  board.querySelectorAll('[data-color-picker]').forEach(function(picker){
    picker.addEventListener('input', function(){
      csSetDayColor(board, picker.dataset.colorPicker, picker.value);
    });
  });

  /* Toggle palette open/closed when clicking the swatch or the hex code text */
  function togglePalette(day){
    var pal = board.querySelector('[data-palette="'+day+'"]');
    if(pal) pal.classList.toggle('open');
  }
  board.querySelectorAll('[data-swatch]').forEach(function(sw){
    sw.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      /* Only toggle if NOT clicking the hidden native picker */
      if(e.target && e.target.type==='color') return;
      e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
      togglePalette(sw.dataset.swatch);
    });
  });
  board.querySelectorAll('[data-color-toggle]').forEach(function(code){
    code.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      e.stopPropagation();
      togglePalette(code.dataset.colorToggle);
    });
  });

  /* Palette swatches */
  board.querySelectorAll('.cs-palette-swatch').forEach(function(btn){
    btn.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
      csSetDayColor(board, btn.dataset.day, btn.dataset.color);
      /* Close palette after picking */
      var pal = board.querySelector('[data-palette="'+btn.dataset.day+'"]');
      if(pal) pal.classList.remove('open');
    });
  });

  /* Route-aware assistance. A known route applies its canonical colour. DisplayName
     remains editable; it is auto-refreshed only when blank or still equal to the
     previous route-derived name, so operator custom labels are never overwritten. */
  board.querySelectorAll('[data-day-field="route"]').forEach(function(routeInput){
    routeInput.addEventListener('change', function(){
      var day = routeInput.dataset.day;
      var mappedColor = csRouteColorForRoute(routeInput.value);
      if(mappedColor) csSetDayColor(board, day, mappedColor);
      var displayInput = board.querySelector('[data-day="'+day+'"][data-day-field="displayname"]');
      if(displayInput){
        var oldDerived = String(displayInput.getAttribute('data-route-derived-name')||'');
        var nextDerived = csRouteDisplayName(routeInput.value);
        var current = String(displayInput.value||'').trim();
        if(nextDerived && (!current || current===oldDerived)){
          displayInput.value = nextDerived;
          displayInput.title = nextDerived;
        }
        displayInput.setAttribute('data-route-derived-name', nextDerived || '');
      }
    });
  });

  /* Add global fields section (Customer, ServiceType, CustomerID + extra schedule fields) */
  var globalSec = document.createElement('div');
  globalSec.className = 'cs-global-section';

  var nameIdx  = _data.iName;
  var svcIdx   = (function(){ var lh=_data.lh||[]; var i=lh.indexOf('servicetype'); return i>=0?i:-1; })();
  var idIdx    = (function(){ var lh=_data.lh||[]; var i=lh.indexOf('customerid');  return i>=0?i:-1; })();

  function makeGField(labelTxt, colIdx, readOnly, overrideVal){
    var wrap = document.createElement('div'); wrap.className = 'cs-g-field';
    var lbl  = document.createElement('div'); lbl.className = 'cs-g-label'; lbl.textContent = labelTxt;
    var inp  = document.createElement('input'); inp.className = 'cs-g-input';
    if(colIdx >= 0){
      inp.setAttribute('data-cust-global-field', colIdx);
      inp.className += ' cs-global-input';
    }
    var val = overrideVal !== undefined ? overrideVal
            : (colIdx>=0 && baseRow.raw) ? String(baseRow.raw[colIdx]||'') : '';
    inp.value = val;
    inp.title = val; /* tooltip for truncated text */
    if(readOnly) inp.setAttribute('readonly','');
    /* Update tooltip when value changes */
    inp.addEventListener('input', function(){ this.title = this.value; });
    wrap.appendChild(lbl); wrap.appendChild(inp);
    return wrap;
  }

  /* Row 1: Customer + ServiceType */
  var coreGrid = document.createElement('div');
  coreGrid.className = 'cs-global-grid-2';
  coreGrid.style.marginBottom = '10px';
  if(nameIdx>=0) coreGrid.appendChild(makeGField('Customer',    nameIdx, false));
  if(svcIdx>=0)  coreGrid.appendChild(makeGField('ServiceType', svcIdx,  true));
  if(coreGrid.children.length) globalSec.appendChild(coreGrid);

  /* All remaining header columns (skip day-card fields and fields shown separately) */
  var skipIdx = {};
  var lhLocal  = (_data&&_data.lh)||[];
  var iRoute   = lhLocal.indexOf('route');
  var iDelDay  = lhLocal.indexOf('deliveryday');
  var iDisplayNameSkip = lhLocal.indexOf('displayname');
  var iMopProdsSkip  = lhLocal.indexOf('mopproducts');
  var iClothesOrder  = lhLocal.indexOf('clothesorder');
  var iMopOrderSkip  = lhLocal.indexOf('moporder');
  var iDeliveryOrderSkip = lhLocal.indexOf('deliveryorder');
  [_data.iDay, _data.iName, svcIdx, _data.iClothes, _data.iMops, _data.iTrolley,
   _data.iColor, _data.iClothesNotes, _data.iMopNotes, _data.iInfo,
   iRoute, iDelDay, iDisplayNameSkip, iMopProdsSkip, iClothesOrder, iMopOrderSkip, iDeliveryOrderSkip].forEach(function(i){ if(i>=0) skipIdx[i]=true; });

  var extraCols = [];
  (_data.header||[]).forEach(function(col, i){
    if(skipIdx[i]) return;
    var lk = String(col||'').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g,'');
    if(!lk) return;
    extraCols.push({idx:i, label:col});
  });

  if(extraCols.length){
    var extraGrid = document.createElement('div');
    extraGrid.className = 'cs-global-grid';
    extraCols.forEach(function(c){
      var isReadOnly = (c.label.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g,'') === 'customerid');
      extraGrid.appendChild(makeGField(c.label, c.idx, isReadOnly));
    });
    globalSec.appendChild(extraGrid);
  }

  /* DeliveryDay + Route are now shown inside each day card (per-occurrence).
     They are also in the skipIdx so they don't appear in extraCols below. */

  body.appendChild(globalSec);

  el('cust-modal').classList.add('open');
}

function csSetDayColor(board, day, hex){
  var hidden = board.querySelector('[data-day="'+day+'"][data-day-field="color"]');
  if(hidden) hidden.value = hex;
  var sw = board.querySelector('[data-swatch="'+day+'"]');
  if(sw) sw.style.background = hex;
  var picker = board.querySelector('[data-color-picker="'+day+'"]');
  if(picker) picker.value = hex;
  var code = board.querySelector('[data-color-code="'+day+'"]');
  if(code) code.textContent = hex;
  board.querySelectorAll('.cs-palette-swatch[data-day="'+day+'"]').forEach(function(b){
    b.classList.toggle('selected', String(b.dataset.color).toLowerCase()===String(hex).toLowerCase());
  });
}

function buildCsPalette(currentColor){
  var out = [];
  var seen = {};
  function add(c){
    c = csNormalizeHex(c);
    if(!c || seen[c]) return;
    seen[c] = true;
    out.push(c);
  }
  STUDIO_PALETTE.forEach(add);
  if(_data && _data.rows) _data.rows.forEach(function(r){ add(r.color); });
  add(currentColor);
  return out;
}

function closeModal() {
  el('cust-modal').classList.remove('open');
  _mode = null; _editRow = null;
}

/* Backdrop click */
el('cust-modal').addEventListener('click', function(ev){
  if (ev.target === this) closeModal();
});

/* Save — calls updateCustomer / addCustomer per day (no bulk function exists) */
function onModalSave() {
  var btn = el('cust-modal-save');
  if (!_data || !_editRow) return;

  var baseRow = csFindRow(_editRow);
  if (!baseRow) return;

  btn.disabled = true;
  btn.innerHTML = '<span class="cust-spin"></span> Saving...';

  var board = el('cust-modal-body') && el('cust-modal-body').querySelector('.cs-day-board');
  var globalInputs = el('cust-modal-body').querySelectorAll('[data-cust-global-field]');

  /* Build the list of days to process */
  var WEEK_DAYS = ['Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday'];
  var occ = csOccurrences(_editRow);

  /* Collect per-day intentions */
  var tasks = [];
  WEEK_DAYS.forEach(function(day){
    var cbEl = board && board.querySelector('.cs-day-on[data-day="'+day+'"]');
    var enabled = cbEl ? cbEl.checked : false;
    var existingRow = null;
    for(var i=0;i<occ.length;i++) if(csCanonDay(occ[i].day)===day){ existingRow=occ[i]; break; }

    /* Keep a before-image so the backend writes only fields the operator
       actually changed. This prevents stale modal snapshots from replaying shared
       Route/DeliveryDay/RouteColor/DisplayName or service data. */
    var sourceRow = existingRow || baseRow;
    var originalVals = sourceRow.raw.slice();
    var vals = originalVals.slice();

    /* Apply day-specific fields from the card */
    if (board) {
      board.querySelectorAll('[data-day="'+day+'"][data-day-field]').forEach(function(inp){
        applyDayFieldToVals(vals, inp);
      });
    }

    /* Apply global fields (CustomerName, ServiceType, etc.) */
    globalInputs.forEach(function(ginp){
      var gi = Number(ginp.getAttribute('data-cust-global-field'));
      if(!isNaN(gi) && gi>=0 && vals.length>gi) vals[gi] = ginp.value;
    });

    /* Ensure Day column is set correctly */
    if(_data.iDay >= 0) vals[_data.iDay] = day;

    var posEl = board && board.querySelector('[data-day="'+day+'"][data-day-field="position"]');
    var position = posEl ? (Number(posEl.value)||1) : 1;

    var changedFields = [];
    if(existingRow){
      for(var ci=0; ci<vals.length; ci++){
        var beforeVal = originalVals[ci] == null ? '' : String(originalVals[ci]);
        var afterVal  = vals[ci] == null ? '' : String(vals[ci]);
        if(beforeVal !== afterVal){
          var fieldKey = String((_data.header||[])[ci]||'').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g,'');
          if(fieldKey) changedFields.push(fieldKey);
        }
      }
    }

    tasks.push({
      day: day,
      enabled: enabled,
      wasOnList: !!existingRow,
      sheetRow: existingRow ? existingRow.sheetRow : null,
      vals: vals,
      position: position,
      changedFields: changedFields
    });
  });

  var enabledTasks = tasks.filter(function(t){ return t.enabled; });
  var disabledExistingTasks = tasks.filter(function(t){ return !t.enabled && t.wasOnList; });

  if(!enabledTasks.length && !disabledExistingTasks.length){
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.innerHTML = 'Save Changes';
    alert('No changes to save.');
    return;
  }

  /*
     Save every day decision in ONE Apps Script call.
     - enabled + existing: update schedule row
     - enabled + new: add schedule row
     - disabled + existing: remove only the current service from that day

     This is required for MOP: when a user unticks a day in the Edit modal, the
     planner must remove MOP from that day. If the CentralDB row is CLOTHES + MOP,
     the DB keeps the CLOTHES service and clears only the MOP fields.
  */
  var payload = tasks.map(function(task){
    return {
      day: task.day,
      enabled: task.enabled,
      wasOnList: task.wasOnList,
      sheetRow: task.sheetRow,
      vals: task.vals,
      position: task.position,
      changedFields: task.changedFields
    };
  });

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(function(res){
      btn.disabled = false;
      btn.innerHTML = 'Save Changes';
      if (res && res.status === 'error') {
        alert(res.message || 'Error saving customer.');
        return;
      }
      closeModal();
      invalidateCustomerCaches();
      invalidateDistributionPlannerCache();
      load(true);
    })
    .withFailureHandler(function(e){
      btn.disabled = false;
      btn.innerHTML = 'Save Changes';
      alert('Error saving customer: ' + (e && e.message || e));
    })
    .saveCustomerDaysBatch(payload, _listType);
}

function applyDayFieldToVals(vals, inp){
  var field = inp.dataset.dayField;
  if (field === 'qty'){
    var qi = (_listType==='mop')
      ? (_data.iTrolley>=0?_data.iTrolley:_data.iMops)
      : _data.iClothes;
    if(qi>=0) vals[qi]=inp.value;
  } else if (field === 'info'){
    // Strict: only write to the correct column per tab
    var ii = (_listType==='mop')
      ? (_data.iMopNotes>=0?_data.iMopNotes:-1)
      : (_data.iClothesNotes>=0?_data.iClothesNotes:-1);
    if(ii>=0) vals[ii]=inp.value;
  } else if (field === 'mopprods'){
    // MOP Products multi-select — join selected options with '; '
    var lh = (_data&&_data.lh)||[];
    var mi = lh.indexOf('mopproducts');
    if(mi>=0){
      var sel = inp.selectedOptions || inp.options;
      var chosen = [];
      for(var oi=0;oi<sel.length;oi++){
        if(sel[oi].selected) chosen.push(sel[oi].value);
      }
      vals[mi] = chosen.join('; ');
    }
  } else if (field === 'position'){
    // Write to ClothesOrder or MopOrder depending on current tab
    var lhP = (_data&&_data.lh)||[];
    var orderCol = (_listType==='mop') ? 'moporder' : 'clothesorder';
    var oi2 = lhP.indexOf(orderCol);
    if(oi2>=0) vals[oi2] = inp.value;
  } else if (field === 'deliveryday'){
    var lhD = (_data&&_data.lh)||[];
    var di = lhD.indexOf('deliveryday');
    if(di>=0) vals[di]=inp.value;
  } else if (field === 'route'){
    var lhR = (_data&&_data.lh)||[];
    var ri = lhR.indexOf('route');
    if(ri>=0) vals[ri]=inp.value;
  } else if (field === 'deliveryorder'){
    var lhO = (_data&&_data.lh)||[];
    var doi = lhO.indexOf('deliveryorder');
    if(doi>=0) vals[doi]=inp.value;
  } else if (field === 'displayname'){
    var lhN = (_data&&_data.lh)||[];
    var ni = lhN.indexOf('displayname');
    if(ni>=0) vals[ni]=inp.value;
  } else if (field === 'color'){
    if(_data.iColor>=0) vals[_data.iColor]=inp.value;
  }
}

/* Add / schedule Customer modal (professional guarded flow) */
var _addOptions = null;
var _addSelectedCustomer = null;

function addModalLoading() {
  el('cust-add-body').innerHTML = '<div class="ca-loading"><span class="cust-spin"></span> Loading customer list...</div>';
}
function addFieldHtml(id, label, inputHtml, help) {
  return '<div class="ca-field"><label for="'+id+'">'+label+'</label>'+inputHtml+(help?'<small>'+help+'</small>':'')+'</div>';
}
function getAddExistingCustomerById(id) {
  var list = (_addOptions && _addOptions.customers) || [];
  for (var i=0;i<list.length;i++) if (String(list[i].CustomerID) === String(id)) return list[i];
  return null;
}
function getAddExistingCustomerByName(name) {
  var key = normCustomerName(name);
  if (!key) return null;
  var list = (_addOptions && _addOptions.customers) || [];
  for (var i=0;i<list.length;i++) if (normCustomerName(list[i].CustomerName) === key) return list[i];
  return null;
}
function serviceLabelHas(serviceType, token){ return String(serviceType||'').toUpperCase().indexOf(token) >= 0; }
function currentAddServiceType(){
  // Add Customer is scoped to the current planner tab.
  return _listType === 'mop' ? 'MOP' : 'CLOTHES';
}
function getSelectedAddDays(){
  var out = [];
  document.querySelectorAll('#ca-day-options input[type=checkbox]:checked').forEach(function(b){
    var d = canonicalDayName(b.value); if(d) out.push(d);
  });
  return out;
}
function getNextVisibleOrder(day, service){
  if (!_data || !_data.rows) return '';
  var orderField = service === 'MOP' ? 'moporder' : 'clothesorder';
  var idx = (_data.lh||[]).indexOf(orderField);
  var max = 0;
  _data.rows.forEach(function(r){
    if (canonicalDayName(r.day) !== canonicalDayName(day)) return;
    if (service === 'MOP' && !serviceLabelHas(r.serviceType, 'MOP')) return;
    if (service === 'CLOTHES' && !serviceLabelHas(r.serviceType, 'CLOTHES')) return;
    var n = idx >= 0 && r.raw ? Number(r.raw[idx] || 0) : 0;
    if (n > max) max = n;
  });
  return max + 1;
}
function renderAddExistingSummary(cust) {
  var box = el('ca-existing-summary');
  if (!box) return;
  if (!cust) {
    box.innerHTML = '<div class="ca-muted">Type a new name or choose an existing customer. Existing customers can safely receive an additional CLOTHES or MOP service.</div>';
    return;
  }
  var days = cust.schedule || [];
  var dayHtml = days.length ? days.map(function(d){
    var parts=[];
    if(d.hasClothes) parts.push('C #' + (d.clothesOrder || '-'));
    if(d.hasMop) parts.push('MOP #' + (d.mopOrder || '-'));
    return '<span class="ca-day-pill">'+esc(d.day)+' <b>'+esc(parts.join(' / ') || d.serviceType || '')+'</b></span>';
  }).join('') : '<span class="ca-muted">No schedule rows yet</span>';
  box.innerHTML = '<div class="ca-existing-card"><b>'+esc(cust.CustomerName)+'</b><span>'+esc(cust.CustomerID)+' • '+esc(cust.ServiceType||'')+'</span><div class="ca-pill-row">'+dayHtml+'</div></div>';
}
function updateAddPreview(){
  var days = getSelectedAddDays();
  var svc = currentAddServiceType();
  var cust = _addSelectedCustomer;
  var warnings = [];
  if (!days.length) warnings.push('Choose at least one production day.');
  var lines = [];
  days.forEach(function(day){
    var existingSameDay = null;
    if (cust && cust.schedule) {
      for (var i=0;i<cust.schedule.length;i++) if (canonicalDayName(cust.schedule[i].day) === canonicalDayName(day)) existingSameDay = cust.schedule[i];
    }
    if (existingSameDay) {
      if (svc === 'CLOTHES' && existingSameDay.hasClothes) warnings.push(day + ': CLOTHES already exists; it will be updated.');
      if (svc === 'MOP' && existingSameDay.hasMop) warnings.push(day + ': MOP already exists; it will be updated.');
      if (svc === 'MOP' && !existingSameDay.hasMop && existingSameDay.hasClothes) warnings.push(day + ': this will convert this day to CLOTHES + MOP.');
      if (svc === 'CLOTHES' && !existingSameDay.hasClothes && existingSameDay.hasMop) warnings.push(day + ': this will convert this day to CLOTHES + MOP.');
    }
    var order = svc === 'MOP' ? (existingSameDay && existingSameDay.mopOrder ? existingSameDay.mopOrder : getNextVisibleOrder(day, 'MOP')) : (existingSameDay && existingSameDay.clothesOrder ? existingSameDay.clothesOrder : getNextVisibleOrder(day, 'CLOTHES'));
    lines.push(day + ' #' + order);
  });
  var prev = el('ca-placement-preview');
  if (prev) prev.innerHTML = '<b>Placement:</b> '+esc(svc)+' • '+(lines.length ? esc(lines.join(' / ')) : '-') + (warnings.length ? '<div class="ca-warn">'+warnings.map(esc).join('<br>')+'</div>' : '');
}
function chooseAddMode(mode){
  var existingPane = el('ca-existing-pane'), newPane = el('ca-new-pane');
  var exRadio = el('ca-mode-existing'), newRadio = el('ca-mode-new');
  if (exRadio) exRadio.checked = mode !== 'new';
  if (newRadio) newRadio.checked = mode === 'new';
  if (existingPane) existingPane.style.display = mode === 'new' ? 'none' : '';
  if (newPane) newPane.style.display = mode === 'new' ? '' : 'none';
  if (mode === 'new') { _addSelectedCustomer = null; renderAddExistingSummary(null); }
  updateAddPreview();
}
function normalizeHexColor(value){
  var c = String(value || '').trim();
  if (!c) return '';
  if (/^[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(c)) c = '#' + c;
  if (/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(c)) return c.toLowerCase();
  return '';
}
function fallbackRouteColors(){
  return STUDIO_PALETTE.slice();
}
function getRouteColorPalette(){
  var seen = {};
  var out = [];
  function add(c, count){
    c = normalizeHexColor(c);
    if (!c || seen[c]) return;
    seen[c] = true;
    out.push({ color:c, count: count || 0 });
  }
  ((_addOptions && _addOptions.routeColors) || []).forEach(function(x){ add(x && (x.color || x.RouteColor || x), x && x.count); });
  if (_data && _data.rows) _data.rows.forEach(function(r){ add(r.color, 0); });
  fallbackRouteColors().forEach(function(c){ add(c, 0); });
  return out;
}
function setAddRouteColor(color){
  color = normalizeHexColor(color);
  if (!color) return false;
  var hidden = el('ca-routecolor');
  var code = el('ca-routecolor-code');
  var current = el('ca-routecolor-current');
  if (hidden) hidden.value = color;
  if (code) code.textContent = color.toUpperCase();
  if (current) current.style.background = color;
  var wrap = el('ca-routecolor-palette');
  if (wrap) wrap.querySelectorAll('.cs-palette-swatch').forEach(function(b){
    b.classList.toggle('selected', normalizeHexColor(b.getAttribute('data-color')) === color);
  });
  return true;
}
function renderAddRouteColorPicker(defaultColor){
  var wrap = el('ca-routecolor-wrap');
  if (!wrap) return;
  var palette = getRouteColorPalette();
  var start = normalizeHexColor(defaultColor) || (palette[0] && palette[0].color) || '#00b050';
  wrap.innerHTML =
    '<input type="hidden" id="ca-routecolor" value="'+esc(start)+'">'+
    '<div class="cs-color-row">'+
      '<span id="ca-routecolor-current" class="cs-color-swatch" title="Selected route colour" style="background:'+esc(start)+'"></span>'+
      '<span id="ca-routecolor-code" class="cs-color-code">'+esc(start.toUpperCase())+'</span>'+
    '</div>'+
    '<div id="ca-routecolor-palette" class="cs-palette">'+
      palette.map(function(x){
        var c = normalizeHexColor(x.color);
        var title = c.toUpperCase() + (x.count ? ' • used '+x.count+' times' : '');
        return '<button type="button" class="cs-palette-swatch" data-color="'+esc(c)+'" title="'+esc(title)+'" style="background:'+esc(c)+'"></button>';
      }).join('')+
    '</div>'+
    '<div class="ca-color-tools">'+
      '<input id="ca-routecolor-new" type="text" placeholder="#RRGGBB" maxlength="7" autocomplete="off">'+
      '<button type="button" id="ca-routecolor-add">Add new colour</button>'+
    '</div>'+
    '<div class="ca-color-hint">Choose one of the colours already used, or add a new hex colour.</div>';
  var pal = el('ca-routecolor-palette');
  if (pal) pal.querySelectorAll('.cs-palette-swatch').forEach(function(btn){
    btn.addEventListener('click', function(){ setAddRouteColor(btn.getAttribute('data-color')); });
  });
  var addBtn = el('ca-routecolor-add');
  if (addBtn) addBtn.addEventListener('click', function(){
    var input = el('ca-routecolor-new');
    var color = normalizeHexColor(input ? input.value : '');
    if (!color) { if (input) input.classList.add('ca-invalid'); return; }
    if (input) input.classList.remove('ca-invalid');
    var pal = el('ca-routecolor-palette');
    if (pal && !pal.querySelector('[data-color="'+color+'"]')) {
      var b = document.createElement('button');
      b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'cs-palette-swatch'; b.setAttribute('data-color', color);
      b.title = color.toUpperCase() + ' • new'; b.style.background = color;
      b.addEventListener('click', function(){ setAddRouteColor(color); });
      pal.appendChild(b);
    }
    setAddRouteColor(color);
  });
  var input = el('ca-routecolor-new');
  if (input) input.addEventListener('input', function(){ input.classList.remove('ca-invalid'); });
  setAddRouteColor(start);
}
function buildAddModal(options) {
  _addOptions = options || { customers: [] };
  _addSelectedCustomer = null;
  var body = el('cust-add-body');
  var defaultService = _listType === 'mop' ? 'MOP' : 'CLOTHES';
  var customerOptions = (_addOptions.customers || []).map(function(c){
    return '<option value="'+esc(c.CustomerID)+'">'+esc(c.CustomerName)+' — '+esc(c.ServiceType||'')+'</option>';
  }).join('');
  body.innerHTML =
    '<div class="ca-top-note">Add a schedule row safely. Use an existing customer when a customer starts CLOTHES or MOP in addition to their current service.</div>'+
    '<div class="ca-mode-row">'+
      '<label class="ca-mode-card"><input type="radio" name="ca-mode" id="ca-mode-existing" checked> Existing customer <small>Add/update a day or add another service.</small></label>'+
      '<label class="ca-mode-card"><input type="radio" name="ca-mode" id="ca-mode-new"> New customer <small>Create master + schedule.</small></label>'+
    '</div>'+
    '<div id="ca-existing-pane">'+
      addFieldHtml('ca-existing','Find existing customer','<select id="ca-existing"><option value="">- choose existing customer -</option>'+customerOptions+'</select>','This avoids duplicates when CLOTHES becomes MOP or MOP becomes CLOTHES.')+
      '<div id="ca-existing-summary"></div>'+
    '</div>'+
    '<div id="ca-new-pane" style="display:none">'+
      addFieldHtml('ca-name','New Customer Name','<input id="ca-name" type="text" autocomplete="off" placeholder="Exact customer name">','If this name already exists, the system will use the existing customer instead of creating a duplicate.')+
      '<div class="ca-grid-2">'+
        addFieldHtml('ca-eircode','Eircode','<input id="ca-eircode" type="text" autocomplete="off">','')+
        addFieldHtml('ca-stops','Stops','<input id="ca-stops" type="number" min="1" step="1" value="1">','')+
      '</div>'+
    '</div>'+
    '<div class="ca-grid-2">'+
      addFieldHtml('ca-days','Production Days','<div id="ca-day-options" class="ca-day-grid">'+DAY_ORDER.map(function(d){return '<label class="ca-day-check"><input type="checkbox" value="'+d+'"> '+d.slice(0,3)+'</label>';}).join('')+'</div>','Choose one or more days. One schedule row is created/updated per selected day.')+
      addFieldHtml('ca-routecolor','Route Color','<div id="ca-routecolor-wrap"></div>','Operational route colours already used in the planner.')+
    '</div>'+
    '<div class="ca-field"><label>Service to add</label><div class="ca-service-locked">'+(_listType==='mop'?'MOP only — because you are in the MOP planner':'CLOTHES only — because you are in the Clothes planner')+'</div></div>'+
    '<div class="ca-grid-2">'+
      addFieldHtml('ca-qtyclothes','Qty Clothes','<input id="ca-qtyclothes" type="text" placeholder="e.g. 1M">','Required for CLOTHES when known.')+
      addFieldHtml('ca-trolleymop','Trolley MOP','<input id="ca-trolleymop" type="text" placeholder="e.g. 1S">','Required for MOP when known.')+
    '</div>'+
    addFieldHtml('ca-mopproducts','MOP Products','<input id="ca-mopproducts" type="text" placeholder="STANDARD POCKET MOPS">','Optional, MOP only.')+
    addFieldHtml('ca-info','Information / Instructions','<textarea id="ca-info" rows="3" placeholder="Operational notes, delivery details, MOP instructions..."></textarea>','Visible in the customer record/schedule notes.')+
    '<div id="ca-placement-preview" class="ca-preview"></div>'+
    '<div id="ca-form-alert" class="ca-alert" style="display:none"></div>';

  renderAddExistingSummary(null);
  renderAddRouteColorPicker((_addOptions.routeColors && _addOptions.routeColors[0] && _addOptions.routeColors[0].color) || '#00b050');
  updateAddPreview();

  el('ca-mode-existing').addEventListener('change', function(){ chooseAddMode('existing'); });
  el('ca-mode-new').addEventListener('change', function(){ chooseAddMode('new'); });
  el('ca-existing').addEventListener('change', function(){ _addSelectedCustomer = getAddExistingCustomerById(this.value); renderAddExistingSummary(_addSelectedCustomer); updateAddPreview(); });
  ['ca-name','ca-qtyclothes','ca-trolleymop','ca-info'].forEach(function(id){ var x=el(id); if(x) x.addEventListener('input', updateAddPreview); if(x) x.addEventListener('change', updateAddPreview); }); document.querySelectorAll('#ca-day-options input').forEach(function(x){ x.addEventListener('change', updateAddPreview); });
}
function openAddModal(){
  el('cust-add-save').innerHTML = 'Save placement';
  el('cust-add-title').innerHTML = '&#10010; Add Customer / Service';
  el('cust-add-modal').classList.add('open');
  addModalLoading();
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(function(res){
      if (res && res.status === 'error') { el('cust-add-body').innerHTML = '<div class="ca-alert">'+esc(res.message||'Could not load customers')+'</div>'; return; }
      buildAddModal(res || { customers: [] });
    })
    .withFailureHandler(function(e){ el('cust-add-body').innerHTML = '<div class="ca-alert">Error loading customer list: '+esc(e&&e.message||e)+'</div>'; })
    .getCustomerMasterOptions();
}
function closeAddModal(){ el('cust-add-modal').classList.remove('open'); }
el('cust-add-modal').addEventListener('click', function(ev){ if(ev.target===this) closeAddModal(); });
function setAddAlert(msg){ var a=el('ca-form-alert'); if(!a) return; a.style.display=''; a.innerHTML=msg; }
function markAddInvalid(ids){ ids.forEach(function(id){ var x=el(id); if(x) x.classList.add('ca-invalid'); }); }
function clearAddInvalid(){ var b=el('cust-add-body'); if(!b) return; b.querySelectorAll('.ca-invalid').forEach(function(x){ x.classList.remove('ca-invalid'); }); var a=el('ca-form-alert'); if(a){ a.style.display='none'; a.innerHTML=''; } }
function applyOptimisticPlacement(payload, res){
  if (!_data || !_data.header || !_data.rows) return;
  var svc = String((payload && payload.ServiceType) || '').toUpperCase();
  var belongs = (_listType === 'mop') ? svc.indexOf('MOP') >= 0 : svc.indexOf('CLOTHES') >= 0;
  if (!belongs) return;
  var cid = String((res && (res.CustomerID || res.customerId)) || payload.CustomerID || '').trim();
  if (!cid) return;
  var day = canonicalDayName(payload.ProductionDay || '');
  var rowIndex = cid + '::' + day + (_listType === 'mop' ? '::MOP' : '');
  var existing = getRowBySheetRow(rowIndex);
  var raw = (_data.header || []).map(function(h){
    var key = String(h || '').toLowerCase();
    if (key === 'productionday') return day;
    if (key === 'customername') return payload.CustomerName || '';
    if (key === 'routecolor') return payload.RouteColor || '';
    if (key === 'qtyclothes') return payload.QtyClothes || '';
    if (key === 'trolleymop') return payload.TrolleyMop || '';
    if (key === 'clothesnotes') return payload.ClothesNotes || payload.Information || '';
    if (key === 'mopinstructions') return payload.MopInstructions || payload.Information || '';
    if (key === 'mopproducts') return payload.MopProducts || '';
    if (key === 'deliveryday') return payload.DeliveryDay || '';
    if (key === 'route') return payload.Route || '';
    if (key === 'displayname') return payload.DisplayName || '';
    if (key === 'customerid') return cid;
    if (key === 'servicetype') return payload.ServiceType || '';
    if (key === 'clothesorder') return payload.ClothesOrder || (res && res.ClothesOrder) || '';
    if (key === 'moporder') return payload.MopOrder || (res && res.MopOrder) || '';
    return '';
  });
  if (existing) {
    existing.raw = raw;
    existing.day = day;
    existing.name = payload.CustomerName || existing.name;
    existing.color = payload.RouteColor || existing.color;
    existing.clothes = payload.QtyClothes || existing.clothes;
    existing.trolley = payload.TrolleyMop || existing.trolley;
    existing.mops = payload.TrolleyMop || existing.mops;
    existing.serviceType = payload.ServiceType || existing.serviceType;
  } else {
    _data.rows.push({
      sheetRow: rowIndex,
      day: day,
      name: payload.CustomerName || '',
      color: payload.RouteColor || '',
      clothes: payload.QtyClothes || '',
      mops: payload.TrolleyMop || '',
      trolley: payload.TrolleyMop || '',
      info: payload.Information || '',
      clothesnotes: payload.ClothesNotes || '',
      mopnotes: payload.MopInstructions || '',
      mopprods: payload.MopProducts || '',
      serviceType: payload.ServiceType || '',
      raw: raw
    });
  }
  render();
}
function onAddSave(){
  clearAddInvalid();
  var mode = el('ca-mode-new') && el('ca-mode-new').checked ? 'new' : 'existing';
  var days = getSelectedAddDays();
  var svc = currentAddServiceType();
  var invalid = [];
  var customerId = '';
  var name = '';
  if (mode === 'existing') {
    customerId = el('ca-existing') ? el('ca-existing').value : '';
    var c = getAddExistingCustomerById(customerId);
    if (!c) invalid.push('ca-existing');
    else { name = c.CustomerName; _addSelectedCustomer = c; }
  } else {
    name = el('ca-name') ? String(el('ca-name').value||'').trim() : '';
    if (!name) invalid.push('ca-name');
    var duplicate = getAddExistingCustomerByName(name);
    if (duplicate) { customerId = duplicate.CustomerID; _addSelectedCustomer = duplicate; }
  }
  if (!days.length) invalid.push('ca-days');
  if (invalid.length) { markAddInvalid(invalid); setAddAlert('Please complete the required fields before saving.'); return; }

  var placements = days.map(function(day){
    var existingSameDay = null;
    if (_addSelectedCustomer && _addSelectedCustomer.schedule) {
      for (var i=0;i<_addSelectedCustomer.schedule.length;i++) if (canonicalDayName(_addSelectedCustomer.schedule[i].day) === canonicalDayName(day)) existingSameDay = _addSelectedCustomer.schedule[i];
    }
    return {
      ProductionDay: day,
      RouteColor: el('ca-routecolor') ? el('ca-routecolor').value : '',
      QtyClothes: svc === 'CLOTHES' && el('ca-qtyclothes') ? el('ca-qtyclothes').value : '',
      TrolleyMop: svc === 'MOP' && el('ca-trolleymop') ? el('ca-trolleymop').value : '',
      MopProducts: svc === 'MOP' && el('ca-mopproducts') ? el('ca-mopproducts').value : '',
      Information: el('ca-info') ? el('ca-info').value : '',
      ClothesNotes: svc === 'CLOTHES' && el('ca-info') ? el('ca-info').value : '',
      MopInstructions: svc === 'MOP' && el('ca-info') ? el('ca-info').value : '',
      ClothesOrder: svc === 'CLOTHES' ? (existingSameDay && existingSameDay.clothesOrder ? existingSameDay.clothesOrder : getNextVisibleOrder(day, 'CLOTHES')) : '',
      MopOrder: svc === 'MOP' ? (existingSameDay && existingSameDay.mopOrder ? existingSameDay.mopOrder : getNextVisibleOrder(day, 'MOP')) : ''
    };
  });

  var payload = { CustomerID: customerId, CustomerName: name, ServiceType: svc, Eircode: el('ca-eircode') ? el('ca-eircode').value : '', Stops: el('ca-stops') ? el('ca-stops').value : '', Information: el('ca-info') ? el('ca-info').value : '', placements: placements };

  var btn = el('cust-add-save');
  btn.disabled = true; btn.innerHTML = '<span class="cust-spin"></span> Saving...';
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(function(res){
      btn.disabled = false; btn.innerHTML = 'Save placement';
      if (res && res.status === 'error') { setAddAlert(esc(res.message || 'Could not save.')); return; }
      closeAddModal();
      showSyncToast((res && res.message) || 'Customer saved', 'ok');
      hideSyncToast(2300);
      var saved = (res && res.placements) || [];
      if (saved.length) saved.forEach(function(pl){ applyOptimisticPlacement(Object.assign({}, payload, pl, { ProductionDay: pl.ProductionDay, ServiceType: svc, CustomerID: res.CustomerID || res.customerId || customerId, CustomerName: res.CustomerName || name }), pl); });
      else placements.forEach(function(pl){ applyOptimisticPlacement(Object.assign({}, payload, pl), res || {}); });
      invalidateCustomerCaches();
      invalidateDistributionPlannerCache();
      setTimeout(function(){ load(true); }, 400);
    })
    .withFailureHandler(function(e){ btn.disabled = false; btn.innerHTML = 'Save placement'; setAddAlert('Error saving: '+esc(e&&e.message||e)); })
    .addCustomerSmartMulti(payload);
}

/* -- Precise delete modal -- */
var _pendingDelAction = null;
var _pendingDelOptions = null;

function _serviceLabelFromRowKey(rowIndex){ return String(rowIndex || '').indexOf('::MOP') >= 0 ? 'MOP' : 'CLOTHES'; }
function _servicePartsText(serviceType){
  var s = String(serviceType || '').toUpperCase();
  var out = [];
  if (s.indexOf('CLOTHES') >= 0) out.push('CLOTHES');
  if (s.indexOf('MOP') >= 0) out.push('MOP');
  return out.length ? out.join(' + ') : (serviceType || '-');
}
function askDelete(sheetRow, name) {
  _pendingDelAction = null;
  _pendingDelOptions = null;
  var modal = el('cust-delete-modal');
  var body = el('cust-delete-body');
  var title = el('cust-delete-title');
  var st = el('cust-delete-status');
  var confirm = el('cust-delete-confirm');
