  if (title) title.textContent = 'Delete: ' + (name || 'Customer');
  if (st) st.textContent = 'Loading customer days...';
  if (confirm) confirm.disabled = true;
  if (body) body.innerHTML = '<div class="ca-loading"><span class="spinner-border spinner-border-sm"></span><span>Loading days and services for this customer...</span></div>';
  if (modal) modal.classList.add('open');

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(function(res){
      if (!res || res.status === 'error') {
        if (body) body.innerHTML = '<div class="ca-alert">'+esc(res && res.message || 'Could not load delete options.')+'</div>';
        if (st) st.textContent = 'Unable to load options';
        return;
      }
      _pendingDelOptions = res;
      renderDeleteOptions(res, sheetRow);
    })
    .withFailureHandler(function(e){
      if (body) body.innerHTML = '<div class="ca-alert">'+esc(e&&e.message||e)+'</div>';
      if (st) st.textContent = 'Unable to load options';
    })
    .getCustomerDeleteOptions(sheetRow);
}
function closeDeleteModal(){
  var modal = el('cust-delete-modal');
  if (modal) modal.classList.remove('open');
  _pendingDelAction = null;
  _pendingDelOptions = null;
}
function renderDeleteOptions(res, clickedRowIndex){
  var body = el('cust-delete-body');
  var title = el('cust-delete-title');
  var st = el('cust-delete-status');
  var confirm = el('cust-delete-confirm');
  var currentService = res.clickedService || _serviceLabelFromRowKey(clickedRowIndex);
  if (title) title.textContent = 'Delete: ' + (res.CustomerName || 'Customer');
  if (confirm) confirm.disabled = true;
  if (st) st.textContent = 'Choose one delete option';
  var rows = res.schedule || [];
  var html = '';
  html += '<div class="cdel-warning"><b>Be precise:</b> this customer appears on '+rows.length+' day(s). Choose whether to remove only one service/day, a full day, one service from all days, or all planner rows. For CLOTHES + MOP, removing one service keeps the other service in the planner.</div>';
  html += '<table class="cdel-days"><thead><tr><th>Day</th><th>Current service</th><th>Clothes order</th><th>MOP order</th><th>Route color</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
  rows.forEach(function(r){
    var color = r.RouteColor ? '<span class="cust-dot" style="background:'+esc(r.RouteColor)+'"></span>'+esc(r.RouteColor) : '-';
    html += '<tr><td><b>'+esc(r.ProductionDay)+'</b></td><td><span class="cdel-pill">'+esc(_servicePartsText(r.ServiceType))+'</span></td><td>'+esc(r.ClothesOrder||'-')+'</td><td>'+esc(r.MopOrder||'-')+'</td><td>'+color+'</td></tr>';
  });
  html += '</tbody></table>';
  html += '<div class="cdel-section-title">Delete options</div><div class="cdel-grid">';
  (res.actions || []).forEach(function(a, idx){
    html += '<label class="cdel-action" data-action-idx="'+idx+'"><input type="radio" name="cdelAction" value="'+idx+'"><div><b>'+esc(a.title)+'</b><span>'+esc(a.detail)+'</span></div></label>';
  });
  html += '</div>';
  body.innerHTML = html;
  var cards = body.querySelectorAll('.cdel-action');
  for (var i=0; i<cards.length; i++) {
    cards[i].addEventListener('click', (function(card){ return function(){
      for (var j=0; j<cards.length; j++) cards[j].classList.remove('selected');
      card.classList.add('selected');
      var input = card.querySelector('input');
      if (input) input.checked = true;
      var idx = Number(card.getAttribute('data-action-idx'));
      _pendingDelAction = (res.actions || [])[idx] || null;
      if (confirm) confirm.disabled = !_pendingDelAction;
      if (st) st.textContent = _pendingDelAction ? _pendingDelAction.title : 'Choose one delete option';
    }; })(cards[i]));
  }
}
function confirmDeleteAction(){
  if (!_pendingDelAction || !_pendingDelOptions) return;
  var confirm = el('cust-delete-confirm');
  var st = el('cust-delete-status');
  if (confirm) { confirm.disabled = true; confirm.textContent = 'Deleting...'; }
  if (st) st.textContent = 'Deleting selected scope...';
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(function(res){
      if (confirm) confirm.textContent = 'Delete selected';
      if (!res || res.status === 'error') {
        alert('Delete failed: ' + (res && res.message || 'Unknown error'));
        if (confirm) confirm.disabled = false;
        return;
      }
      closeDeleteModal();
      showSyncToast((res && res.message) || 'Deleted', 'ok');
      hideSyncToast(2200);
      invalidateCustomerCaches();
      invalidateDistributionPlannerCache();
      load(true);
    })
    .withFailureHandler(function(e){
      if (confirm) { confirm.disabled = false; confirm.textContent = 'Delete selected'; }
      alert('Delete failed: '+(e&&e.message||e));
    })
    .deleteCustomerPlannerAction(_pendingDelAction);
}

/* -- Sync toast helpers -- */
var _syncTimer = null;
function showSyncToast(msg, state) {
  var t = el('cust-sync-toast');
  var m = el('cust-sync-msg');
  if (!t || !m) return;
  m.textContent = msg || 'Saving...';
  t.className = state ? state : '';
  t.classList.add('show');
  if (_syncTimer) clearTimeout(_syncTimer);
}
function hideSyncToast(delay) {
  if (_syncTimer) clearTimeout(_syncTimer);
  _syncTimer = setTimeout(function(){
    var t = el('cust-sync-toast');
    if (t) t.classList.remove('show');
  }, delay || 0);
}


/* ============================================
   PRINT PLANNER
   - Uses the original strong RouteColor from CentralDB for print.
   - Keeps production planner row order (ClothesOrder / MopOrder).
   - Displays the same production order shown on screen:
     ClothesOrder in Clothes and MopOrder in MOP.
   - Close button uses window.close() inside the popup window.
   ============================================ */
function printPlanner() {
  if (!_data || !_data.rows) { alert('No data to print.'); return; }

  // Apenas 6 dias = 3 folhas, 2 dias por folha
  var DAY_ORDER_PRINT = ['Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday'];

  var byDay = {};
  DAY_ORDER_PRINT.forEach(function(d){ byDay[d] = []; });

  _data.rows.forEach(function(r){
    var d = String(r.day || '').toLowerCase().slice(0,3);
    DAY_ORDER_PRINT.forEach(function(day){
      if (day.toLowerCase().slice(0,3) === d) byDay[day].push(r);
    });
  });

  // Keep the stable production planner sequence, never the current DOM row position.
  var printProductionOrderCol = (_listType === 'mop') ? 'moporder' : 'clothesorder';
  var printProductionOrderIdx = (_data && _data.lh) ? _data.lh.indexOf(printProductionOrderCol) : -1;
  function printProductionOrder(row){
    if (printProductionOrderIdx < 0 || !row || !row.raw) return 999999;
    var n = Number(row.raw[printProductionOrderIdx]);
    return (isFinite(n) && n > 0) ? n : 999999;
  }
  function printProductionOrderBadge(row){
    var n = printProductionOrder(row);
    return n === 999999 ? '' : n;
  }
  DAY_ORDER_PRINT.forEach(function(day){
    byDay[day].sort(function(a,b){
      var oa = printProductionOrder(a), ob = printProductionOrder(b);
      if (oa !== ob) return oa - ob;
      return String(a.name||'').localeCompare(String(b.name||''));
    });
  });

  var pages = [
    ['Monday','Tuesday'],
    ['Wednesday','Thursday'],
    ['Friday','Saturday']
  ];

  var FONT = 6.3;
var HEADER_FONT = 9;
var PAGE_PAD_MM = 3;
var HEADER_H_MM = 5.5;

// maior lista entre Monday-Saturday
var GLOBAL_MAX_ROWS = Math.max.apply(null, DAY_ORDER_PRINT.map(function(day){
  return (byDay[day] || []).length;
})) || 1;

// mesma altura para todas as linhas da semana inteira
var ROW_H_MM = (297 - (PAGE_PAD_MM * 2) - HEADER_H_MM - 6) / GLOBAL_MAX_ROWS;
  //var PAD_H = 8;

  function escPrint(s){
    return String(s == null ? '' : s)
      .replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;')
      .replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
  }

  function contrastColor(hex) {
    try {
      var c = String(hex || '').trim();
      if (c.length === 4) c = '#'+c[1]+c[1]+c[2]+c[2]+c[3]+c[3];
      if (!/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(c)) return '#0f172a';
      var r=parseInt(c.slice(1,3),16), g=parseInt(c.slice(3,5),16), b=parseInt(c.slice(5,7),16);
      return ((0.299*r + 0.587*g + 0.114*b) / 255 > 0.55) ? '#0f172a' : '#fff';
    } catch(e){ return '#0f172a'; }
  }

  function buildDay(day, rows, rowH) {
    var html = rows.map(function(r){
      // Print uses the original strong RouteColor saved in CentralDB.
      // Do not soften/lighten here; operators use the printed colours as route blocks.
      var bg = r.color && /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(String(r.color).trim()) ? String(r.color).trim() : '#f1f5f9';
      var fg = contrastColor(bg);
      // Use exactly the same order number as the Customer screen badge.
      // Clothes view = ClothesOrder; MOP view = MopOrder.
      var ord = printProductionOrderBadge(r);
      var ordText = ord ? String(ord) : '!';

      // Trolley quantity printed inside the coloured route block, aligned right.
      // Clothes list uses QtyClothes; MOP list uses TrolleyMop (fallback: mapped mops value).
      var trolleyRaw = (_listType === 'mop')
        ? (r.trolley != null && String(r.trolley).trim() !== '' ? r.trolley : r.mops)
        : r.clothes;
      var trolleyText = String(trolleyRaw == null ? '' : trolleyRaw).trim();
      if (trolleyText === '0') trolleyText = '';

      return '<tr><td style="' +
        'height:'+rowH+'mm;background:'+bg+';color:'+fg+';' +
        'font-size:'+FONT+'pt;font-weight:800;padding:0 1mm;' +
        'line-height:'+rowH+'mm;' +
        'white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;position:relative;">' +
          '<span style="position:absolute;left:1.2mm;top:0;height:'+rowH+'mm;line-height:'+rowH+'mm;width:8mm;text-align:left;font-size:'+(FONT*0.82)+'pt;font-weight:800;opacity:.72;color:'+fg+';">' + escPrint(ordText) + '</span>' +
          '<span style="display:block;width:100%;height:'+rowH+'mm;line-height:'+rowH+'mm;text-align:center;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;padding:0 12mm;">' + escPrint(r.name) + '</span>' +
          (trolleyText ? '<span style="position:absolute;right:1.2mm;top:0;height:'+rowH+'mm;line-height:'+rowH+'mm;min-width:9mm;text-align:right;font-size:'+(FONT*0.9)+'pt;font-weight:900;color:'+fg+';">' + escPrint(trolleyText) + '</span>' : '') +
      '</td></tr>';
    }).join('');

    return '<td style="width:48%;vertical-align:top;">' +
      '<table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;table-layout:fixed;">' +
        '<thead><tr><th style="' +
          'height:'+HEADER_H_MM+'mm;background:#111827;color:#fff;' +
          'font-size:'+HEADER_FONT+'pt;font-weight:900;text-align:left;' +
          'padding:0 1.5mm;text-transform:uppercase;">'+
          escPrint(day) + ' <span style="font-size:10px;opacity:.7;">(' + rows.length + ')</span>' +
        '</th></tr></thead>' +
        '<tbody>' + html + '</tbody>' +
      '</table>' +
    '</td>';
  }

  var pagesHtml = pages.map(function(pair, idx){
    var rows1 = byDay[pair[0]] || [];
    var rows2 = byDay[pair[1]] || [];
    //var maxRows = Math.max(rows1.length, rows2.length, 1);

    // mesma altura de linha para os dois dias da folha; ocupa a folha inteira
    //var rowH = Math.floor((USABLE - HEADER_H) / maxRows);
    var rowH = ROW_H_MM;

    return '<div class="ppage' + (idx === pages.length - 1 ? ' last' : '') + '">' +
      '<table style="width:100%;height:100%;border-collapse:collapse;table-layout:fixed;">' +
        '<tr>' +
          buildDay(pair[0], rows1, rowH) +
          '<td style="width:4%;"></td>' +
          buildDay(pair[1], rows2, rowH) +
        '</tr>' +
      '</table>' +
    '</div>';
  }).join('');

  var win = window.open('', '_blank', 'width=900,height=1000,scrollbars=yes');
  if (!win) { alert('Please allow popups for this site.'); return; }

  var doc = win.document;
  doc.open();
  doc.write(
    '<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>Customer Print</title>' +
    '<style>' +
      '*{box-sizing:border-box;margin:0;padding:0;}' +
      'body{font-family:"Segoe UI",Arial,sans-serif;-webkit-print-color-adjust:exact;print-color-adjust:exact;}' +
      '.toolbar{background:#1e293b;color:#fff;padding:8px 14px;font-size:12px;display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;}' +
      '.toolbar button{height:30px;padding:0 14px;border:0;border-radius:6px;cursor:pointer;font-weight:700;}' +
      '.ppage{width:210mm;height:297mm;padding:4mm;background:#fff;overflow:hidden;}' +
      '@media screen{body{background:#e2e8f0}.ppage{margin:12px auto;box-shadow:0 4px 20px #0003}}' +
      '@media print{@page{size:A4 portrait;margin:0}.toolbar{display:none!important}.ppage{margin:0;break-after:page;page-break-after:always}.ppage.last{break-after:auto;page-break-after:auto}}' +
    '</style></head><body>' +
    '<div class="toolbar"><span>Customer print: 6 days / 3 pages / badge = ClothesOrder or MopOrder / production row sequence / strong route colours / fixed font '+FONT+'px</span>' +
    '<button onclick="window.print()" style="margin-left:auto;background:#0ea5a6;color:#fff;">Print</button>' +
    '<button onclick="window.close()" style="background:#475569;color:#fff;">Close</button></div>' +
    pagesHtml +
    '</body></html>'
  );
  doc.close();
  win.focus();
}

function _custInit_() {
  var b;
  b=el('cust-modal-close');  if(b) b.addEventListener('click', closeModal);
  b=el('cust-modal-cancel'); if(b) b.addEventListener('click', closeModal);
  b=el('cust-modal-save');   if(b) b.addEventListener('click', onModalSave);
  b=el('cust-add-close');    if(b) b.addEventListener('click', closeAddModal);
  b=el('cust-add-cancel');   if(b) b.addEventListener('click', closeAddModal);
  b=el('cust-add-save');     if(b) b.addEventListener('click', onAddSave);
  b=el('cust-delete-close');  if(b) b.addEventListener('click', closeDeleteModal);
  b=el('cust-delete-cancel'); if(b) b.addEventListener('click', closeDeleteModal);
  b=el('cust-delete-confirm'); if(b) b.addEventListener('click', confirmDeleteAction);
  b=el('btnAddCustomer');    if(b) b.addEventListener('click', function(){ openAddModal(); });
  b=el('btnReloadCustomer'); if(b) b.addEventListener('click', function(){ invalidateCustomerCaches(); load(true); });
  b=el('btnTypeClothes'); if(b) b.addEventListener('click', function(){
    _listType='clothes'; this.classList.add('active');
    var mb=el('btnTypeMop'); if(mb) mb.classList.remove('active');
    load(false);
  });
  b=el('btnTypeMop'); if(b) b.addEventListener('click', function(){
    _listType='mop'; this.classList.add('active');
    var cb=el('btnTypeClothes'); if(cb) cb.classList.remove('active');
    load(false);
  });
  b=el('btnViewPlanner');    if(b) b.addEventListener('click', function(){ _viewMode='planner'; this.classList.add('active'); el('btnViewList').classList.remove('active'); render(); });
  b=el('btnViewList');       if(b) b.addEventListener('click', function(){ _viewMode='list'; this.classList.add('active'); el('btnViewPlanner').classList.remove('active'); render(); });
  b=el('cust-search-input'); if(b) b.addEventListener('input', function(){ _search=this.value.trim(); render(); });
  b=el('btnPrintPlanner'); if(b) b.addEventListener('click', printPlanner);

  /* -- Planner scroll arrows -- */
  var _scrollOuter = el('cust-scroll-outer');
  var _scrollWrap  = el('cust-planner-wrap');

  function _updateScrollArrows() {
    if (!_scrollOuter || !_scrollWrap) return;
    var sl = _scrollWrap.scrollLeft;
    var max = _scrollWrap.scrollWidth - _scrollWrap.clientWidth;
    if (sl > 4)       _scrollOuter.classList.add('can-left');
    else              _scrollOuter.classList.remove('can-left');
    if (sl < max - 4) _scrollOuter.classList.add('can-right');
    else              _scrollOuter.classList.remove('can-right');
  }

  if (_scrollWrap) {
    _scrollWrap.addEventListener('scroll', _updateScrollArrows);
    _scrollWrap.addEventListener('wheel', function(e){
      if (Math.abs(e.deltaY) > Math.abs(e.deltaX)) {
        _scrollWrap.scrollLeft += e.deltaY;
        e.preventDefault();
      }
    }, { passive:false });
  }

  /* Scroll by roughly one day-group width (4 cols * ~50px each) */
  var _scrollStep = Math.max(420, Math.floor((_scrollWrap ? _scrollWrap.clientWidth : 900) * 0.75));
  var al = el('cust-scroll-arrow-l');
  var ar = el('cust-scroll-arrow-r');
  if (al) al.addEventListener('click', function(){
    if (_scrollWrap) _scrollWrap.scrollLeft -= _scrollStep;
  });
  if (ar) ar.addEventListener('click', function(){
    if (_scrollWrap) _scrollWrap.scrollLeft += _scrollStep;
  });

  /* Touch / mouse drag to scroll (for non-scrollbar users) */
  (function(){
    var isDown = false, startX, scrollStart;
    if (!_scrollWrap) return;
    _scrollWrap.addEventListener('mousedown', function(e){
      /* only drag on the table background, not buttons */
      if (e.target.tagName === 'BUTTON' || e.target.tagName === 'INPUT') return;
      isDown = true; startX = e.clientX; scrollStart = _scrollWrap.scrollLeft;
      _scrollWrap.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
      e.preventDefault();
    });
    document.addEventListener('mousemove', function(e){
      if (!isDown) return;
      _scrollWrap.scrollLeft = scrollStart - (e.clientX - startX);
    });
    document.addEventListener('mouseup', function(){
      isDown = false;
      if (_scrollWrap) _scrollWrap.style.cursor = '';
    });
  })();

  /* Re-check arrows after render (called from render()) */
  window._custUpdateScrollArrows = _updateScrollArrows;

  /* Escape key closes modal */
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function(ev){
    if (ev.key==='Escape' || ev.keyCode===27) closeModal();
  });

  window.CustomerTab = window.CustomerTab || {};
  window.CustomerTab.onShow = load;
  window.loadCustomerTable = load;

  // Standalone Customer-only app: in the original project this screen was loaded
  // when the Customer tab became visible. Here there is no sidebar/tabs, so we
  // must start the first load explicitly.
  // Integrated build: only auto-load as a standalone app (no tab nav present).
  // In the Sorting project the load fires via CustomerTab.onShow / loadCustomerTable
  // when the Customer tab opens, so we must NOT eager-load on page load here.
  var _hasTabNav = true; // integrated in Distribution App; load only when Customers tab opens
  if (!_hasTabNav && !window.__customerStandaloneAutoLoadStarted) {
    window.__customerStandaloneAutoLoadStarted = true;
    setTimeout(function(){
      try {
        load(true);
      } catch (e) {
        showMsg('Error starting customer load: ' + (e && e.message ? e.message : e));
      }
    }, 50);
  }
}

if (document.readyState==='loading') {
  window.addEventListener('load', _custInit_);
} else {
  _custInit_();
}

/* -- Patch showTab to hide Customer overlays when leaving the tab -- */
(function(){
  function _patchShowTab_(){
    var orig = window.showTab;
    if (typeof orig !== 'function') return;
    window.showTab = function(id){
      /* Hide Customer overlays when switching away */
      if (id !== 'Customer') {
        var modal = document.getElementById('cust-modal');
        var sync  = document.getElementById('cust-sync-toast');
        var del   = document.getElementById('cust-delete-modal');
        if (modal) modal.classList.remove('open');
        if (sync)  sync.classList.remove('show');
        if (del)   del.classList.remove('show');
      }
      orig.call(this, id);
    };
  }
  /* showTab may not exist yet when this runs - wait for it */
  if (typeof window.showTab === 'function') {
    _patchShowTab_();
  } else {
    window.addEventListener('load', _patchShowTab_);
  }
})();

})();

})(); /* end Customer Planner IIFE */
</script>
      </section>

      <!-- HISTORY -->
      <section class="section" id="s-history">
        <div class="sec-header">
          <div><div class="sec-title">History</div><div class="sec-sub">Saved rosters, vehicle maintenance and driver infringements</div></div>
          <button class="btn btn-outline" id="add-maint-btn" onclick="App.openMaintModal(null)" style="display:none">
            <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><line x1="12" y1="5" x2="12" y2="19"/><line x1="5" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12"/></svg>Add Past Record
          </button>
          <button class="btn btn-primary" id="add-infr-btn" onclick="App.openInfringementModal(null)" style="display:none">
            <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><line x1="12" y1="5" x2="12" y2="19"/><line x1="5" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12"/></svg>Add Infringement
          </button>
        </div>
        <!-- Tabs -->

        Record the actual kilometres travelled by each active vehicle for this roster week. Planned KM is calculated from the assigned routes. Returned vehicles remain in history but cannot receive a new entry.
      </div>
      <div id="vehicle-mileage-list" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:0"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-footer" style="justify-content:space-between">
      <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="App.closeModal('vehicle-mileage-modal')">Cancel</button>
      <button class="btn btn-primary" id="save-vehicle-mileage-btn" onclick="App.saveVehicleMileage()">Save Mileage</button>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

        <div class="help-item"><div class="help-ic">👤</div><div class="help-txt"><h4>Drivers</h4><p>Add and manage drivers. "Hide from Roster" removes a driver from the planner without deleting their history. Deactivating keeps records but stops future scheduling.</p></div></div>
        <div class="help-item"><div class="help-ic">🚚</div><div class="help-txt"><h4>Fleet Maintenance &amp; Defects</h4><p>One place to report a defect (corrective) or schedule maintenance. Marking a truck "Out of Service" automatically blocks it on the Roster. Each defect creates a printable A4 report.</p></div></div>
        <div class="help-item"><div class="help-ic">🗺️</div><div class="help-txt"><h4>Routes</h4><p>Delivery and collection stops for each route and day, with weight and trolley counts. Use Print for a paper run-sheet.</p></div></div>
        <div class="help-item"><div class="help-ic">📅</div><div class="help-txt"><h4>Leave Calendar</h4><p>Approve or reject driver holiday requests, and add or edit approved leave. Approved days automatically free up or block drivers on the Roster.</p></div></div>

<!-- MODAL: Confirm -->

  <div class="modal" style="max-width:380px">

<div class="toast-container" id="toasts"></div>

<script>
const App = {

  D: { drivers:[], allDrivers:[], routes:[], weeks:[], currentWeek:'', viewerUrl:'', customers:[], vehicles:[], vehicleDefects:[], vehicleDefectStats:null, infringements:[], maintLog:null, leaveCalendar:[], leaveRequests:[] },
  roster: { weekStart:'', assignments:[], requiredRoutes:{mon:0,tue:0,wed:0,thu:0,fri:0,sat:0,sun:0} },
  editingCell: null,
  unsaved: false,
  customerCollapsed: {},
  // Starts expanded on every page load. The user may collapse it for the current session.
  _fleetAlertsCollapsed: false,
  _routesLoading: false,
  _routeWaiters: [],

  DAYS:       ['mon','tue','wed','thu','fri','sat','sun'],
  DAY_LABELS: ['Mon','Tue','Wed','Thu','Fri','Sat','Sun'],
  DAY_FULL:   ['Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday','Sunday'],

  init() {
    // Single server call — returns app config + first roster in one round trip.
    // Keep a visible fallback so a backend error never leaves the page spinning forever.
    document.getElementById('save-btn').style.display  = '';
    document.getElementById('print-btn').style.display = '';
    this._bootTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      this._showBootError('The roster is taking too long to load. Check the Apps Script execution log and the CentralDB connection.');
    }, 45000);

    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(d => {
        clearTimeout(this._bootTimer);
        try {
          if (!d || !d.roster) throw new Error('The server returned incomplete initial data.');
          this.D = d;
          this.D.maintLog       = null;
          this.D.pendingReturns = d.pendingReturns || [];
          this.D.leaveCalendar  = d.leaveCalendar || [];
          this.D.leaveRequests  = d.leaveRequests || [];
          document.getElementById('viewer-url-text').textContent =
            d.viewerUrl || 'Deploy the script first to get the link.';
          this._applyRoster(d.roster, d.roster.weekStart);
          // Routes are intentionally outside the critical boot payload. Warm them
          // immediately in the background, then repaint route-coloured roster badges.
          this.ensureRoutesLoaded(ok => { if (ok) this.renderRoster(); });
          this._checkPendingReturns();
          this.renderFleetComplianceAlerts();
          this.renderVehicleDefectDashboard();
          this.renderLeaveRosterNotice();
          if (typeof this.startLeavePolling === 'function') this.startLeavePolling();
        } catch (err) {
          this._showBootError((err && err.message) || 'Could not initialise the roster.');
        }
      })
      .withFailureHandler(e => {
        clearTimeout(this._bootTimer);
        const msg = (e && e.message) || 'Unknown server error';
        this._showBootError('Load error: ' + msg);
        this.toast('Load error: ' + msg, 'e');
      })
      .getInitialData();
  },

  _showBootError(message) {
    const loading = document.getElementById('roster-loading');
    if (loading) loading.style.display = 'none';
    const wrap = document.getElementById('roster-wrap');
    if (wrap) {
      wrap.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state"><h3>Roster could not be loaded</h3><p>'
        + this.esc(message || 'Unknown error')
        + '</p><p style="margin-top:8px;font-size:11px">Open Apps Script &gt; Executions to see the backend error.</p></div>';
    }
  },

  ensureRoutesLoaded(cb) {
    if (Array.isArray(this.D.routes) && this.D.routes.length) {
      if (typeof cb === 'function') cb(true);
      return;
    }
    if (typeof cb === 'function') this._routeWaiters.push(cb);
    if (this._routesLoading) return;
    this._routesLoading = true;
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(rts => {
        this._routesLoading = false;
        this.D.routes = Array.isArray(rts) ? rts : [];
        this._resetCustFilter();
        const waiters = this._routeWaiters.splice(0);
        waiters.forEach(fn => { try { fn(this.D.routes.length > 0); } catch(e) {} });
      })
      .withFailureHandler(() => {
        this._routesLoading = false;
        const waiters = this._routeWaiters.splice(0);
        waiters.forEach(fn => { try { fn(false); } catch(e) {} });
      })
      .getRoutes();
  },

  nav(sec) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.section').forEach(s => s.classList.remove('active'));
    document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(n => n.classList.remove('active'));
    document.getElementById('s-' + sec).classList.add('active');
    document.getElementById('nav-' + sec).classList.add('active');
    const titles = { roster:'Weekly Roster', drivers:'Drivers', routes:'Routes', vehicles:'Fleet Maintenance & Defect Management', leave:'Leave Calendar', customers:'Customers', history:'History', share:'Driver Link' };
    document.getElementById('topbar-title').textContent = titles[sec] || sec;
    document.getElementById('save-btn').style.display  = sec === 'roster' ? '' : 'none';
    document.getElementById('print-btn').style.display = sec === 'roster' ? '' : 'none';
    if (sec === 'drivers') {
      this.renderDrivers();
      this.ensureInfringementsLoaded(() => this.renderDrivers());
    }
    if (sec === 'routes')    this.renderCustomers();
    if (sec === 'vehicles')  this.renderVehicles();
    if (sec === 'leave')     this.renderLeaveCalendar();
    if (sec === 'share')     this.loadLeaveSupervisorEmail();
    if (sec === 'customers') {
      if (window.CustomerTab && typeof window.CustomerTab.onShow === 'function') window.CustomerTab.onShow(false);
      else if (typeof window.loadCustomerTable === 'function') window.loadCustomerTable(false);
    }

    if (sec === 'history') {
      this._histTab = this._histTab || 'rosters';
      this.renderHistory();
    }
  },

  // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  //  Week locking rules
  //  • Current week  → always editable
  //  • Previous week → editable on Mondays only (grace day)
  //  • Older weeks   → always locked
  // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  isWeekLocked(weekStart) {
    const todayStr = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
    const thisMonday = this.mondayOf(todayStr);

    // Current week: never locked
    if (weekStart >= thisMonday) return false;

    // Previous week: locked unless today is Monday
    const prevDate = new Date(thisMonday + 'T12:00:00Z');
    prevDate.setUTCDate(prevDate.getUTCDate() - 7);
    const prevMonday = prevDate.toISOString().slice(0,10);
    if (weekStart === prevMonday) {
      // Editable only on Mondays (getUTCDay() === 1)
      return new Date().getUTCDay() !== 1;
    }

    // Any older week: always locked
    return true;
  },

  lockMessage: function(weekStart) {
    const todayStr = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    const thisMonday = this.mondayOf(todayStr);
    const prevDate = new Date(thisMonday + 'T12:00:00Z');
    prevDate.setUTCDate(prevDate.getUTCDate() - 7);
    const prevMonday = prevDate.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    if (weekStart === prevMonday) {
      return "Last week's roster is locked - changes are allowed on Mondays only.";
    }
    return 'This roster is from a past week and is permanently locked.';
  },

  // ── Shared: apply loaded roster to state and render ─────────
  _applyRoster(r, weekStart) {
    if (r.isFresh && this.roster && this.roster.requiredRoutes) {
      // Apps Script HTML Service compatibility: avoid object spread in the page parser.
      r.requiredRoutes = Object.assign({}, this.roster.requiredRoutes);
    }

    // ── Driver visibility rules ──────────────────────────────
    // A driver appears in a week's roster if:
    //   1. joinedDate is empty OR joinedDate <= weekStart  (they existed that week)
    //   2. deactivatedDate is empty OR deactivatedDate >= weekStart  (they hadn't left yet)
    //      Note: deactivatedDate = Monday of week they were deactivated,
    //      so they appear in that week but NOT in future weeks.
    const allDrivers = this.D.allDrivers || this.D.drivers;
    const ws         = weekStart; // "YYYY-MM-DD"

    const renderList = allDrivers.filter(d => {
      const joined   = d.joinedDate      || '';
      const deacted  = d.deactivatedDate || '';
      const okJoined = !joined  || joined  <= ws;
      const okDeact  = !deacted || deacted >= ws;
      // Drivers flagged "Hide from Roster" never appear in the roster grid
      if (d.rosterHidden) return false;
      return okJoined && okDeact;
    }).map(d => Object.assign({}, d, {
      _historical: d.active === false  // mark inactive drivers for visual cue
    }));

    const savedMap = {};
    (r.assignments || []).forEach(a => { savedMap[String(a.driverId)] = a; });

    r.assignments = renderList.map(d => (
      savedMap[String(d.id)] || { driverId: d.id, mon:'', tue:'', wed:'', thu:'', fri:'', sat:'', sun:'' }
    ));

    r.weekStart         = weekStart;
    this.roster         = r;
    this.roster.published    = r.published    || false;
    this.roster.actualHours  = r.actualHours  || {};
    this._rosterDrivers = renderList;
    this.unsaved        = false;
    document.getElementById('unsaved-badge').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('roster-loading').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('roster-wrap').style.opacity   = '1';
    this.updateWeekLabel();
    this.renderRoster();
  },

  loadWeek(ws) {
    const monday = this.mondayOf(ws);
    document.getElementById('roster-loading').style.display = 'flex';
    document.getElementById('roster-wrap').style.opacity = '.4';
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(r => { this._applyRoster(r, monday); })
      .withFailureHandler(e => {
        this.toast('Error loading week: ' + e.message, 'e');
        document.getElementById('roster-loading').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('roster-wrap').style.opacity = '1';
      })
      .getRosterData(monday);
  },


  prevWeek() {
    const d = new Date(this.roster.weekStart + 'T12:00:00Z');
    d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() - 7);
    this.loadWeek(d.toISOString().slice(0,10));
  },

  nextWeek() {
    const d = new Date(this.roster.weekStart + 'T12:00:00Z');
    d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + 7);
    this.loadWeek(d.toISOString().slice(0,10));
  },

  goToWeek(val)      { if (val) this.loadWeek(val); },
  goToCurrentWeek()  { this.loadWeek(this.D.currentWeek); },

  mondayOf(ds) {
    const d = new Date(ds + 'T12:00:00Z');
    const dow = d.getUTCDay();
    d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() - (dow === 0 ? 6 : dow - 1));
    return d.toISOString().slice(0,10);
  },

  getWeekDates(ws) {
    const d = new Date(ws + 'T12:00:00Z');
    return Array.from({length:7}, (_,i) => {
      const x = new Date(d); x.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate()+i);
      return x.toISOString().slice(0,10);
    });
  },

  fmtShort(ds) { const p=ds.split('-'); return p[2]+'/'+p[1]; },
  fmtFull(ds)  { const p=ds.split('-'); return p[2]+'/'+p[1]+'/'+p[0]; },

  updateWeekLabel() {
    const ws     = this.roster.weekStart;
    const dates  = this.getWeekDates(ws);
    const today  = this.mondayOf(new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10));
    const isPast = ws < today;
    const isFuture = ws > today;
    const isCurrent = ws === today;

    // ── Date label ──
    document.getElementById('week-label').textContent =
      this.fmtShort(dates[0]) + ' — ' + this.fmtShort(dates[6]) + '/' + dates[6].split('-')[0];
    document.getElementById('week-date-picker').value = ws;

    // ── Context pill next to navigation ──
    const pill = document.getElementById('week-context-pill');
    if (isCurrent) {
      pill.innerHTML = '<span class="week-context week-ctx-current">● This Week</span>';
    } else if (isPast) {
      const weeksAgo = Math.round((new Date(today) - new Date(ws)) / 604800000);
      pill.innerHTML = '<span class="week-context week-ctx-past">◀ ' + weeksAgo + ' week' + (weeksAgo!==1?'s':'') + ' ago</span>';
    } else {
      const weeksAhead = Math.round((new Date(ws) - new Date(today)) / 604800000);
      pill.innerHTML = '<span class="week-context week-ctx-future">▶ ' + weeksAhead + ' week' + (weeksAhead!==1?'s':'') + ' ahead</span>';
    }

    // ── "Back to This Week" button ──
    document.getElementById('goto-current-btn').style.display = isCurrent ? 'none' : '';

    // ── Week bar background tint ──
    const bar = document.getElementById('week-bar');
    bar.style.background    = isPast ? '#fffbeb' : isFuture ? '#eff6ff' : 'transparent';
    bar.style.borderRadius  = (isPast || isFuture) ? '8px' : '0';
    bar.style.padding       = (isPast || isFuture) ? '8px 12px' : '0';
    bar.style.marginBottom  = (isPast || isFuture) ? '0' : '12px';
    bar.style.border        = isPast ? '1.5px solid #fcd34d' : isFuture ? '1.5px solid #93c5fd' : 'none';

    // ── Off-week warning banner ──
    const offBar = document.getElementById('week-offweek-bar');
    const offMsg = document.getElementById('week-offweek-msg');
    if (isCurrent) {
      offBar.className = 'week-offweek-bar';
      offBar.style.display = 'none';
    } else if (isPast) {
      offBar.className = 'week-offweek-bar past';
      offMsg.textContent = 'You are viewing a PAST week (' + this.fmtFull(dates[0]) + '). Any changes will overwrite historical data.';
      offBar.style.display = '';
    } else {
      offBar.className = 'week-offweek-bar future';
      offMsg.textContent = 'You are viewing a FUTURE week (' + this.fmtFull(dates[0]) + '). Save and publish when ready.';
      offBar.style.display = '';
    }

    // ── Roster table opacity hint for past weeks ──
    const wrap = document.getElementById('roster-wrap');
    if (wrap) wrap.style.opacity = isPast ? '0.92' : '1';
  },

  // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  //  Route ↔ Day matching
  //
  //  Accepted Day field formats:
  //    "Monday"                   single full name
  //    "Mon"                      abbreviation
  //    "MWF"                      letter-code shorthand
  //    "Monday, Wednesday, Friday" comma-separated
  //    "Mon Wed Fri"              space-separated
  //    "Daily" / "All"            every day
  //    "Weekdays" / "Mon-Fri"     Mon–Fri
  //    ""  (blank)                treat as Daily
  // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  routeAvailableForDate(route, dateStr) {
    if (!route) return false;
    if (route.active === false) return false;
    const ds = String(dateStr || '').slice(0,10);
    if (ds && route.validFrom && ds < route.validFrom) return false;
    if (ds && route.validUntil && ds > route.validUntil) return false;
    return true;
  },

  routeMatchesDay(route, dayKey) {
    const raw = (route.day || '').trim();
    if (!raw) return true;

    const s = raw.toLowerCase().replace(/[–\-]/g,' ').trim();

    // Universal keywords
    if (['daily','all','any','everyday','all days','every day','7 days','7days'].includes(s)) return true;

    const wdKeys = ['mon','tue','wed','thu','fri'];
    if (['weekdays','mon to fri','monday to friday','mon fri','monday friday'].includes(s))
      return wdKeys.includes(dayKey);
    if (['weekends','sat sun','saturday sunday'].includes(s))
      return dayKey === 'sat' || dayKey === 'sun';

    // Canonical aliases for each day key
    const aliases = {
      mon: ['monday','mon','mo'],
      tue: ['tuesday','tue','tu'],
      wed: ['wednesday','wed','we'],
      thu: ['thursday','thu','th'],
      fri: ['friday','fri','fr'],
      sat: ['saturday','sat','sa'],
      sun: ['sunday','sun','su']
    };
    const targets = aliases[dayKey] || [];

    // Split on commas, spaces, slashes, ampersands
    const parts = s.split(/[\s,\/&+|]+/).map(p => p.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    for (const p of parts) {
      if (targets.some(t => p === t || (p.length >= 3 && t.startsWith(p)))) return true;
    }

    // Letter-code pattern (MWF, MTWThF, MTWF, etc.)
    // Only attempt if the string looks like a pure letter code
    const code = raw.replace(/[\s\-–]/g,'');
    if (/^[MTWFSthHuao]+$/i.test(code) && code.length >= 1 && code.length <= 12) {
      const up = code.toUpperCase();
      const found = new Set();
      let i = 0;
      while (i < up.length) {
        const two = up.slice(i, i+2);
        if      (two === 'TH') { found.add('thu'); i += 2; }
        else if (two === 'SU') { found.add('sun'); i += 2; }
        else if (two === 'SA') { found.add('sat'); i += 2; }
        else if (up[i] === 'M') { found.add('mon'); i++; }
        else if (up[i] === 'T') { found.add('tue'); i++; }
        else if (up[i] === 'W') { found.add('wed'); i++; }
        else if (up[i] === 'F') { found.add('fri'); i++; }
        else if (up[i] === 'S') { found.add('sat'); i++; }
        else i++;
      }
      if (found.size > 0) return found.has(dayKey);
    }

    return false;
  },

  // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  //  Driver Leave / Holiday Calendar
  // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  _leaveTypeClass(type) {
    const t = String(type||'').toLowerCase();
    if (t.indexOf('holiday') !== -1) return 'holiday';
    if (t.indexOf('day') !== -1 || t.indexOf('off') !== -1) return 'dayoff';
    return '';
  },

  _monthKey(dateStr) { return String(dateStr||'').slice(0,7); },

  _monthLabel(key) {
    const d = new Date(key + '-01T12:00:00Z');
    return d.toLocaleDateString('en-IE', { month:'short', year:'numeric' });
  },

  _isRosterWorkValue(val) {
    val = String(val||'').trim();
    if (!val) return false;
    return !['Off','Hols','Absent','Sick'].includes(val);
  },

  driverLeaveFor(driverId, dateStr) {
    return (this.D.leaveCalendar || []).find(l => String(l.DriverID) === String(driverId) && String(l.LeaveDate).slice(0,10) === String(dateStr));
  },

  leaveCellBadge(leave, conflict) {
    const label = leave ? (String(leave.LeaveType||'Leave').toLowerCase().indexOf('holiday')!==-1 ? 'Hols' : 'Leave') : 'Leave';
    return `<button class="badge ${conflict?'b-leave-conflict':'b-leave'}" title="${this.esc(leave ? (leave.LeaveType + ' approved') : 'Approved leave')}">${conflict?'⚠ ':''}${this.esc(label)}</button>`;
  },

  renderLeaveRosterNotice() {
    const pending = (this.D.leaveRequests || []).filter(r => String(r.Status||'Pending').toLowerCase()==='pending');

    // Badge on the sidebar menu (visible from any screen)
    const badge = document.getElementById('nav-leave-badge');
    if (badge) {
      if (pending.length) { badge.style.display = 'inline-flex'; badge.textContent = pending.length > 99 ? '99+' : String(pending.length); }
      else { badge.style.display = 'none'; }
    }

    const el = document.getElementById('leave-request-alert');
    if (!el) return;
    if (!pending.length) { el.style.display = 'none'; return; }
    el.style.display = 'flex';
    const names = Array.from(new Set(pending.map(r => r.DriverName).filter(Boolean))).slice(0,3).join(', ');
    el.innerHTML = `<div><strong>${pending.length} leave request${pending.length!==1?'s':''} pending approval</strong>${names?' — '+this.esc(names):''}</div><button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="App.nav('leave')">Review</button>`;
  },

  startLeavePolling() {
    if (this._leavePollTimer) return;
    const POLL_MS = 60 * 1000;
    this._leavePollTimer = setInterval(() => {
      const onLeaveScreen = document.getElementById('s-leave') && document.getElementById('s-leave').classList.contains('active');
      const prevPending = (this.D.leaveRequests || []).filter(r => String(r.Status||'Pending').toLowerCase()==='pending').length;
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(r => {
          this.D.leaveCalendar = (r && r.calendar) || [];
          this.D.leaveRequests = (r && r.requests) || [];
          this.renderLeaveRosterNotice();
          const nowPending = this.D.leaveRequests.filter(x => String(x.Status||'Pending').toLowerCase()==='pending').length;
          if (nowPending > prevPending && !onLeaveScreen) {
            this.toast(`${nowPending - prevPending} new leave request${(nowPending-prevPending)!==1?'s':''} received`, 'i');
          }
          if (onLeaveScreen && typeof this.renderLeaveCalendar === 'function') this.renderLeaveCalendar();
        })
        .withFailureHandler(() => {})
        .getLeaveDataForSupervisor();
    }, POLL_MS);
  },

  reloadLeaveData(cb) {
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(r => {
        this.D.leaveCalendar = (r && r.calendar) || [];
        this.D.leaveRequests = (r && r.requests) || [];
        this.renderLeaveRosterNotice();
        this.renderRoster();
        if (cb) cb();
      })
      .withFailureHandler(e => this.toast('Leave load error: ' + e.message, 'e'))
      .getLeaveDataForSupervisor();
  },

  _populateLeaveDriverFilters() {
    const activeDrivers = (this.D.allDrivers||this.D.drivers||[]).filter(d=>d.active!==false);
    const filterOpts = '<option value="">All drivers</option>' + activeDrivers.map(d => `<option value="${this.esc(d.id)}">${this.esc(d.name)}</option>`).join('');
    const f = document.getElementById('leave-filter-driver');
    if (f && !f.dataset.ready) { f.innerHTML = filterOpts; f.dataset.ready='1'; }
    const s = document.getElementById('leave-driver');
    if (s) s.innerHTML = activeDrivers.map(d => `<option value="${this.esc(d.id)}">${this.esc(d.name)}</option>`).join('');
  },

  renderLeaveCalendar() {
    this._populateLeaveDriverFilters();
    this.renderLeavePending();
    const grid = document.getElementById('leave-calendar-grid');
    if (!grid) return;
    const driverFilter = (document.getElementById('leave-filter-driver')||{}).value || '';
    const typeFilter = (document.getElementById('leave-filter-type')||{}).value || '';
    const start = this.mondayOf(new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10));
    const months = [];
    for (let i=0;i<12;i++) {
      const d = new Date(start + 'T12:00:00Z');
      d.setUTCMonth(d.getUTCMonth()+i);
      d.setUTCDate(1);
      months.push(d.toISOString().slice(0,7));
    }
    const grouped = {};
    (this.D.leaveCalendar||[]).forEach(l => {
      if (driverFilter && String(l.DriverID)!==String(driverFilter)) return;
      if (typeFilter && String(l.LeaveType)!==String(typeFilter)) return;
      const key = this._monthKey(l.LeaveDate);
      if (!months.includes(key)) return;
      const groupKey = [l.RequestID,l.DriverID,l.StartDate,l.EndDate,l.LeaveType].join('|');
      if (!grouped[key]) grouped[key] = {};
      if (!grouped[key][groupKey]) grouped[key][groupKey] = Object.assign({}, l, { dates:[] });
      grouped[key][groupKey].dates.push(String(l.LeaveDate).slice(0,10));
    });
    let html = '';
    months.forEach(m => {
      html += `<div class="leave-month"><div class="leave-month-h">${this.esc(this._monthLabel(m))}</div><div class="leave-month-body">`;
      const events = Object.values(grouped[m]||{}).sort((a,b)=>String(a.StartDate).localeCompare(String(b.StartDate)));
      if (!events.length) html += '<div class="leave-empty">No approved leave</div>';
      events.forEach(ev => {
        const cls = this._leaveTypeClass(ev.LeaveType);
        const dateText = ev.StartDate === ev.EndDate ? this.fmtShort(ev.StartDate) : `${this.fmtShort(ev.StartDate)} – ${this.fmtShort(ev.EndDate)}`;
        const rid = this.esc(ev.RequestID || '');
        const label = this.esc((ev.DriverName||ev.DriverID) + ' · ' + (ev.LeaveType||'Leave') + ' (' + dateText + ')');
        const actions = rid ? `<div class="leave-event-actions">
          <button class="leave-ev-btn edit" title="Edit" onclick="App.openEditApprovedLeave('${rid}')">✎ Edit</button>
          <button class="leave-ev-btn del" title="Delete" onclick="App.confirmDeleteApprovedLeave('${rid}','${label}')">✕ Delete</button>
        </div>` : '';
        html += `<div class="leave-event ${cls}"><b>${this.esc(ev.DriverName||ev.DriverID)} · ${this.esc(ev.LeaveType||'Leave')}</b>${this.esc(dateText)}${ev.Notes?'<br>'+this.esc(ev.Notes):''}${actions}</div>`;
      });
      html += '</div></div>';
    });
    grid.innerHTML = html;
  },

  renderLeavePending() {
    const el = document.getElementById('leave-pending-list');
    if (!el) return;
    const pending = (this.D.leaveRequests||[]).filter(r => String(r.Status||'Pending').toLowerCase()==='pending');
    if (!pending.length) {
      el.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state" style="padding:28px 12px"><h3>No pending requests</h3><p>New driver requests will appear here.</p></div>';
      return;
    }
    el.innerHTML = pending.map(r => `<div class="leave-request pending">
      <div class="leave-request-main"><div><div class="leave-request-name">${this.esc(r.DriverName||r.DriverID)}</div><div class="leave-request-meta">${this.esc(r.LeaveType||'Leave')} · ${this.esc(r.StartDate)} to ${this.esc(r.EndDate)} · ${this.esc(r.Days||'')} day${String(r.Days)==='1'?'':'s'}${r.Notes?'<br>Note: '+this.esc(r.Notes):''}${r.ConflictSummary?'<br><span style="color:#92400e">'+this.esc(r.ConflictSummary)+'</span>':''}</div></div><span class="leave-badge pending">Pending</span></div>
      <div class="leave-request-actions"><button class="btn btn-success btn-sm" onclick="App.approveLeaveRequest('${this.esc(r.RequestID)}', event)">Approve</button><button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onclick="App.rejectLeaveRequest('${this.esc(r.RequestID)}', event)">Reject</button></div>
    </div>`).join('');
  },

  openLeaveModal() {
    this._populateLeaveDriverFilters();
    const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
    document.getElementById('leave-edit-id').value = '';          // add mode
    document.getElementById('leave-modal-title').textContent = 'Add Approved Leave';
    document.getElementById('save-leave-btn').textContent = 'Save Approved Leave';
    document.getElementById('leave-driver').disabled = false;
    document.getElementById('leave-start').value = today;
    document.getElementById('leave-end').value = today;
    document.getElementById('leave-type').value = 'Holiday';
    document.getElementById('leave-approved-by').value = '';
    document.getElementById('leave-notes').value = '';
    this.openModal('leave-modal');
  },

  // Supervisor-only: open the modal pre-filled to EDIT an approved leave.
  openEditApprovedLeave(requestId) {
    const list = this.D.leaveCalendar || [];
    const rows = list.filter(l => String(l.RequestID) === String(requestId));
    if (!rows.length) { this.toast('Could not find that leave entry.', 'e'); return; }
    const ev = rows[0];
    this._populateLeaveDriverFilters();
    document.getElementById('leave-edit-id').value = String(requestId);
    document.getElementById('leave-modal-title').textContent = 'Edit Approved Leave';
    document.getElementById('save-leave-btn').textContent = 'Save Changes';
    const drv = document.getElementById('leave-driver');
    drv.value = String(ev.DriverID || '');
    drv.disabled = true;   // keep the same driver when editing
    document.getElementById('leave-type').value = ev.LeaveType || 'Holiday';
    document.getElementById('leave-approved-by').value = ev.ApprovedBy || '';
    document.getElementById('leave-start').value = String(ev.StartDate || ev.LeaveDate || '').slice(0,10);
    document.getElementById('leave-end').value = String(ev.EndDate || ev.LeaveDate || '').slice(0,10);
    document.getElementById('leave-notes').value = ev.Notes || '';
    this.openModal('leave-modal');
  },

  saveSupervisorLeave() {
    const editId = (document.getElementById('leave-edit-id').value || '').trim();
    const payload = {
      DriverID: document.getElementById('leave-driver').value,
      LeaveType: document.getElementById('leave-type').value,
      StartDate: document.getElementById('leave-start').value,
      EndDate: document.getElementById('leave-end').value || document.getElementById('leave-start').value,
      Notes: document.getElementById('leave-notes').value,
      ApprovedBy: document.getElementById('leave-approved-by').value || 'Supervisor'
    };
    if (!payload.DriverID || !payload.StartDate || !payload.EndDate) { this.toast('Driver and dates are required.', 'e'); return; }
    const btn = document.getElementById('save-leave-btn');
    this._runBtn(btn, 'Saving...', done => {
      const handler = r => {
        done(r.ok !== false && r.success !== false);
        if (r.ok === false || r.success === false) this.toast(r.msg || r.message || 'Leave conflict.', 'e');
        else {
          this.toast(editId ? 'Approved leave updated.' : 'Approved leave saved.', 's');
          this.closeModal('leave-modal');
          this.reloadLeaveData(()=>this.renderLeaveCalendar());
        }
      };
      const run = google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(handler).withFailureHandler(e => { done(false); this.toast(e.message,'e'); });
      if (editId) run.updateApprovedDriverLeave(editId, payload);
      else        run.addApprovedDriverLeave(payload);
    });
  },

  // Supervisor-only: confirm + delete an approved leave (request + calendar rows).
  confirmDeleteApprovedLeave(requestId, label) {
    document.getElementById('confirm-msg').innerHTML =
      'This will remove the approved leave' + (label ? ' for <strong>' + this.esc(label) + '</strong>' : '') +
      ' and free up those dates in the Roster. This cannot be undone.';
    document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn').textContent = 'Delete';
    document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn').className = 'btn btn-danger';
    document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn').onclick = () => {
      const okBtn = document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn');
      this._runBtn(okBtn, 'Deleting…', done => {
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(r => {
            done(r && r.ok);
            if (r && r.ok) {
              this.toast('Approved leave deleted.', 'i');
              this.closeModal('confirm-modal');
              this.reloadLeaveData(()=>this.renderLeaveCalendar());
            } else { this.toast('Error: ' + ((r && r.msg) || 'Could not delete leave'), 'e'); }
          })
          .withFailureHandler(e => { done(false); this.toast(e.message, 'e'); })
          .deleteApprovedDriverLeave(requestId);
      });
    };
    this.openModal('confirm-modal');
  },

  approveLeaveRequest(requestId, ev) {
    this.runBusy(ev, 'Approving…', run => run.approveDriverLeaveRequest(requestId, 'Supervisor'),
      r => {
        if (!r || r.ok === false || r.success === false) this.toast((r && (r.msg || r.message)) || 'Cannot approve request.', 'e');
        else { this.toast('Leave request approved.', 's'); this.reloadLeaveData(()=>this.renderLeaveCalendar()); }
      });
  },

  rejectLeaveRequest(requestId, ev) {
    const reason = prompt('Reason for rejection (optional):') || '';
    this.runBusy(ev, 'Rejecting…', run => run.rejectDriverLeaveRequest(requestId, 'Supervisor', reason),
      r => {
        if (!r || r.ok === false || r.success === false) this.toast((r && (r.msg || r.message)) || 'Cannot reject request.', 'e');
        else { this.toast('Leave request rejected.', 'i'); this.reloadLeaveData(()=>this.renderLeaveCalendar()); }
      });
  },

  // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  //  Roster render
  // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  renderRoster() {
    const locked = this.isWeekLocked(this.roster.weekStart);

    // Show/hide lock banner and save button based on lock state
    const banner = document.getElementById('lock-banner');
    const saveBtn = document.getElementById('save-btn');
    if (locked) {
      banner.style.display = 'flex';
      document.getElementById('lock-banner-msg').textContent = this.lockMessage(this.roster.weekStart);
      saveBtn.style.display  = 'none';
      document.getElementById('print-btn').style.display    = '';
      document.getElementById('publish-btn').style.display  = 'none';
      document.getElementById('unpublished-note').style.display = 'none';
    } else {
      banner.style.display  = 'none';
      saveBtn.style.display = '';
      document.getElementById('print-btn').style.display   = '';

      // Publish button: only for future weeks that have been saved
      const todayWk    = this.mondayOf(new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10));
      const isFuture   = this.roster.weekStart > todayWk;
      const isSaved    = (this.D.weeks||[]).includes(this.roster.weekStart);
      const publishBtn = document.getElementById('publish-btn');
      const note       = document.getElementById('unpublished-note');

      if (isFuture) {
        publishBtn.style.display = '';
        this._updatePublishBtn();
        note.style.display = (!isSaved || !this.roster.published) ? '' : 'none';
      } else {
        publishBtn.style.display = 'none';
        note.style.display       = 'none';
      }
    }

    this.renderReqBar(locked);
    const dates = this.getWeekDates(this.roster.weekStart);
    const asgns = this.roster.assignments || [];
    const req   = this.roster.requiredRoutes || {};

    // ── Check for vehicles in maintenance assigned to drivers ──
    const conflictDrivers = [];
    const renderDriversCheck = this._rosterDrivers || this.D.drivers;
    renderDriversCheck.forEach(drv => {
      const a = asgns.find(x => x.driverId === drv.id);
      if (!a) return;
      this.DAYS.forEach(day => {
        const { vehicle } = this.parseCell(a[day]||'');
        if (!vehicle) return;
        const vObj = (this.D.vehicles||[]).find(v => v.plate === vehicle);
        if (vObj && this.vehIsUnavailable(vObj) && !conflictDrivers.includes(drv.name)) {
          conflictDrivers.push(drv.name);
        }
      });
    });

    const conflictBannerEl = document.getElementById('maint-conflict-banner');
    if (conflictDrivers.length) {
      const names = conflictDrivers.slice(0,3).join(', ') + (conflictDrivers.length > 3 ? ` +${conflictDrivers.length-3} more` : '');
      conflictBannerEl.style.display = '';
      conflictBannerEl.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" style="width:18px;height:18px;stroke:#ea580c;fill:none;stroke-width:2.5;flex-shrink:0"><path d="M10.29 3.86L1.82 18a2 2 0 0 0 1.71 3h16.94a2 2 0 0 0 1.71-3L13.71 3.86a2 2 0 0 0-3.42 0z"/><line x1="12" y1="9" x2="12" y2="13"/><line x1="12" y1="17" x2="12.01" y2="17"/></svg>
        <div style="flex:1;font-size:13px;color:#9a3412">
          <strong>Vehicle unavailable</strong> — ${this.esc(names)} ha${conflictDrivers.length===1?'s':'ve'} a vehicle that is in maintenance, scheduled maintenance or returned. Cells marked with 🔸.
        </div>`;
    } else {
      conflictBannerEl.style.display = 'none';
    }

    const todayWk     = this.mondayOf(new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10));
    const isPastWeek  = this.roster.weekStart < todayWk;
    const actualHours = this.roster.actualHours || {};

    let h = '<table class="roster-table"><thead><tr>';
    h += '<th class="stk stk-0">#</th>';
    h += '<th class="stk stk-1">Lic</th>';
    h += '<th class="stk stk-2 name-col">Driver</th>';
    this.DAY_LABELS.forEach((lbl,i) => {
      h += `<th>${lbl}<br><span style="font-weight:400;font-size:10px">${this.fmtShort(dates[i])}</span></th>`;
    });
    h += '<th>Days</th><th title="Estimated hours from routes">Hrs</th>';
    if (isPastWeek) h += '<th title="Actual hours from timesheet" style="color:#f97316">Actual</th>';
    h += '<th>KM</th><th>Stops</th>';
    h += '</tr></thead><tbody>';

    let totHrs = 0, totKm = 0, totStops = 0;

    // _rosterDrivers includes historical drivers from the saved snapshot
    const renderDrivers = this._rosterDrivers || this.D.drivers;
    renderDrivers.forEach((drv, idx) => {
      const a = asgns.find(x => x.driverId === drv.id) || { mon:'',tue:'',wed:'',thu:'',fri:'',sat:'',sun:'' };
      h += '<tr>';
      h += `<td class="stk stk-0" style="color:#94a3b8;font-size:11px">${idx+1}</td>`;
      h += `<td class="stk stk-1" style="font-size:11px;color:#64748b">${this.esc(drv.license)}</td>`;
      h += `<td class="stk stk-2 name-td">${this.esc(drv.name)}${drv._historical?'<span style="font-size:9px;color:#94a3b8;background:#f1f5f9;padding:1px 5px;border-radius:3px;margin-left:5px;font-weight:600">removed</span>':''}</td>`;
      let days=0, hrs=0, km=0, stops=0;
      this.DAYS.forEach((day, dayIdx) => {
        const val = a[day] || '';
        const dateStr = dates[dayIdx];
        const leave = this.driverLeaveFor(drv.id, dateStr);
        const leaveConflict = !!(leave && this._isRosterWorkValue(val));
        const rt  = this.findRoute(val);
        if (rt && !leaveConflict) { days++; hrs += rt.hours; km += rt.km; stops += rt.stops; }
        else if (val === 'Training' && !leaveConflict) { days++; hrs += 8; }
        const canClick = !locked && !leave;
        const click = canClick ? `onclick="App.openCellModal('${drv.id}','${day}')"` : '';
        const cursor = canClick ? 'cursor:pointer' : 'cursor:not-allowed';
        const tdClass = leaveConflict ? 'roster-leave-conflict' : (leave ? 'roster-leave-cell' : '');
        const content = leave ? this.leaveCellBadge(leave, leaveConflict) : this.cellBadge(val, day, drv.id);
        h += `<td class="${tdClass}" ${click} style="${cursor}">${content}</td>`;
      });
      totHrs   += hrs;
      totKm    += km;
      totStops += stops;
      h += `<td class="total-cell">${days||''}</td>`;
      h += `<td class="total-cell">${hrs ? hrs.toFixed(1) : ''}</td>`;
      if (isPastWeek) {
        const actual = actualHours[String(drv.id)];
        const diff   = (actual !== undefined && hrs) ? (actual - hrs) : null;
        const diffStr = diff !== null ? (diff > 0 ? ' <span style="color:#dc2626;font-size:9px">+'+diff.toFixed(1)+'</span>' : diff < 0 ? ' <span style="color:#16a34a;font-size:9px">'+diff.toFixed(1)+'</span>' : '') : '';
        const actualStr = actual !== undefined ? actual.toFixed(1) + diffStr : '<span style="color:#94a3b8;font-size:10px">—</span>';
        h += `<td class="total-cell" style="color:#f97316;font-weight:600">${actualStr}</td>`;
      }
      h += `<td class="total-cell">${km ? Math.round(km) : ''}</td>`;
      h += `<td class="total-cell">${stops||''}</td>`;
      h += '</tr>';
    });

    h += '</tbody><tfoot>';
    h += '<tr class="sum-row"><td class="stk stk-0"></td><td class="stk stk-1"></td>';
    h += '<td class="stk stk-2 sum-lbl">Routes Planned</td>';
    let totP = 0;
    this.DAYS.forEach(day => {
      const c = asgns.filter(a => {
        const v = a[day]; if (!v) return false;
        const {route} = this.parseCell(v);
        return route && !['Off','Hols','Training','Absent','Sick'].includes(route);
      }).length;
      totP += c; h += `<td style="text-align:center">${c}</td>`;
    });
    h += `<td style="text-align:center;font-weight:800">${totP}</td>`;
    h += `<td style="text-align:center;font-weight:800;color:#475569">${totHrs ? totHrs.toFixed(1)+'h' : ''}</td>`;
    if (isPastWeek) h += '<td></td>';
    h += `<td style="text-align:center;font-weight:800;color:#475569">${totKm ? Math.round(totKm)+' km' : ''}</td>`;
    h += `<td style="text-align:center;font-weight:800;color:#475569">${totStops || ''}</td>`;
    h += '</tr>';

    h += '<tr class="sum-row"><td class="stk stk-0"></td><td class="stk stk-1"></td>';
    h += '<td class="stk stk-2 sum-lbl">Required Routes</td>';
    let totR = 0;
    this.DAYS.forEach(day => {
      const v = parseInt(req[day]) || 0; totR += v;
      h += `<td style="text-align:center">${v}</td>`;
    });
    h += `<td style="text-align:center;font-weight:800">${totR}</td><td></td>${isPastWeek?'<td></td>':''}<td></td><td></td></tr>`;

    h += '<tr class="sum-row"><td class="stk stk-0"></td><td class="stk stk-1"></td>';
    h += '<td class="stk stk-2 sum-lbl">Daily Check</td>';
    let allOk = true;
    this.DAYS.forEach(day => {
      const p = asgns.filter(a => {
        const v = a[day]; if (!v) return false;
        const {route} = this.parseCell(v);
        return route && !['Off','Hols','Training','Absent','Sick'].includes(route);
      }).length;
      const r = parseInt(req[day]) || 0;
      const ok = r === 0 || p >= r;
      if (!ok) allOk = false;
      h += `<td class="${ok ? 'check-ok' : 'check-fail'}">${ok ? 'OK' : '!!'}</td>`;
    });
    h += `<td class="${allOk?'check-ok':'check-fail'}">${allOk?'✓':''}</td><td></td>${isPastWeek?'<td></td>':''}<td></td><td></td></tr>`;
    h += '</tfoot></table>';
    document.getElementById('roster-wrap').innerHTML = h;
  },

  _vehicleUsageForDay(day, plate, excludeDriverId) {
    const target = String(plate || '').replace(/\s+/g,'').toUpperCase();
    if (!target) return [];
    return (this.roster.assignments || []).filter(a => {
      if (excludeDriverId !== undefined && String(a.driverId) === String(excludeDriverId)) return false;
      const parsed = this.parseCell(a[day] || '');
      return String(parsed.vehicle || '').replace(/\s+/g,'').toUpperCase() === target;
    });
  },

  _vehicleSecondTurn(day, driverId, plate) {
    if (!day || !plate) return false;
    const target = String(plate).replace(/\s+/g,'').toUpperCase();
    const users = (this.roster.assignments || []).filter(a => {
      const parsed = this.parseCell(a[day] || '');
      return String(parsed.vehicle || '').replace(/\s+/g,'').toUpperCase() === target;
    });
    return users.findIndex(a => String(a.driverId) === String(driverId)) > 0;
  },

  cellBadge(val, day, driverId) {
    if (!val)             return '<button class="badge b-empty">—</button>';
    if (val === 'Off')      return '<button class="badge b-off">Off</button>';
    if (val === 'Hols')     return '<button class="badge b-hols">Hols</button>';
    if (val === 'Training') return '<button class="badge b-training">Train.</button>';
    if (val === 'Absent')   return '<button class="badge b-absent">Absent</button>';
    if (val === 'Sick')     return '<button class="badge b-sick">Sick</button>';
    const { route, vehicle } = this.parseCell(val);
    const rt    = this.findRoute(val);
    const bg    = rt ? rt.colour : '#94a3b8';
    const tx    = this.textColor(bg);
    const lum   = this.bgLum(bg);
    const border = lum > 0.88 ? ';border:1.5px solid #94a3b8' : '';
    const label  = rt ? rt.route : route;
    const secondTurn = vehicle ? this._vehicleSecondTurn(day, driverId, vehicle) : false;
    const tip    = rt ? `${rt.route} — ${rt.displayName}${vehicle?' · '+vehicle:''}${secondTurn?' · Second turn (2T)':''}` : val;
    const vShort = vehicle || '';

    // Check if assigned vehicle is currently unavailable
    const vObj     = vehicle ? (this.D.vehicles||[]).find(v => v.plate === vehicle) : null;
    const vBad     = vObj ? this.vehIsUnavailable(vObj) : false;
    const vWarn    = vBad ? ' title="Vehicle unavailable!"' : '';
    const vWarnDot = vBad
      ? '<span style="position:absolute;top:-4px;right:-4px;width:10px;height:10px;background:#f97316;border:1.5px solid #fff;border-radius:50%;pointer-events:none"></span>'
      : '';

    const badgeBtn = `<span style="position:relative;display:inline-block">
      <button class="badge b-route" style="background:${bg};color:${tx}${border}" title="${this.esc(tip)}"${vWarn}>${this.esc(label)}</button>
      ${vWarnDot}
    </span>`;

    return vehicle
      ? `<div class="cell-badge-wrap">${badgeBtn}<span class="cell-vehicle-lbl"${vBad?' style="color:#f97316;font-weight:800"':''}>${this.esc(vShort)}${secondTurn?'<span class="shift-2t" title="Second vehicle turn">2T</span>':''}</span></div>`
      : badgeBtn;
  },

  // ── Cell value helpers (route|vehicle format) ─────────────
  // Cell values: "Route 610" | "Route 610|211 D 34010" | "Off" | ""
  parseCell(val) {
    if (!val) return { route: '', vehicle: '' };
    if (['Off','Hols','Training','Absent','Sick'].includes(val)) return { route: val, vehicle: '' };
    const i = val.indexOf('|');
    return i === -1 ? { route: val, vehicle: '' } : { route: val.slice(0,i), vehicle: val.slice(i+1) };
  },

  formatCell(route, vehicle) {
    if (!route) return '';
    return vehicle ? `${route}|${vehicle}` : route;
  },

  // Look up route by displayName first (new data), then route number (legacy data)
  findRoute(val) {
    if (!val) return null;
    const { route } = this.parseCell(val);
    if (!route) return null;
    return this.D.routes.find(r => r.displayName === route)
        || this.D.routes.find(r => r.route === route)
        || null;
  },

  renderReqBar(locked) {
    const req = this.roster.requiredRoutes || {};
    let h = '<div class="req-bar"><span class="req-bar-label">Required:</span><div class="req-days">';
    this.DAYS.forEach((d,i) => {
      const dis = locked ? 'disabled' : '';
      h += `<div class="req-day">
        <span class="req-day-lbl">${this.DAY_LABELS[i]}</span>
        <input type="number" min="0" max="99" value="${parseInt(req[d])||0}"
          ${dis} onchange="App.updateReq('${d}',this.value)" style="${locked?'opacity:.5;cursor:not-allowed':''}">
      </div>`;
    });
    h += '</div></div>';
    document.getElementById('req-inputs').innerHTML = h;
  },

  updateReq(day, val) {
    if (!this.roster.requiredRoutes) this.roster.requiredRoutes = {};
    this.roster.requiredRoutes[day] = parseInt(val) || 0;
    this.setUnsaved();
    this.renderRoster();
  },

  // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  //  Cell Modal — day filter + taken detection
  // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  _pendingRoute:   '',
  _pendingVehicle: '',

  openCellModal(drvId, day) {
    if (this.isWeekLocked(this.roster.weekStart)) return;
    if (!Array.isArray(this.D.routes) || !this.D.routes.length) {
      this.ensureRoutesLoaded(ok => {
        if (ok) this.openCellModal(drvId, day);
        else this.toast('Routes could not be loaded. Try again.', 'e');
      });
      return;
    }
    const leaveBlock = this.driverLeaveFor(drvId, this.getWeekDates(this.roster.weekStart)[this.DAYS.indexOf(day)]);
    if (leaveBlock) { this.toast('This driver has approved ' + (leaveBlock.LeaveType || 'leave') + ' on this date.', 'e'); return; }
    this.editingCell     = { drvId, day };
    this._pendingRoute   = '';
    this._pendingVehicle = '';

    const drv    = this.D.drivers.find(d => d.id === drvId);
    const a      = (this.roster.assignments || []).find(x => x.driverId === drvId);
    const cur    = a ? (a[day] || '') : '';
    const dayIdx = this.DAYS.indexOf(day);
    const assignmentDate = this.getWeekDates(this.roster.weekStart)[dayIdx];

    document.getElementById('cell-modal-title').textContent =
      (drv ? drv.name : drvId) + ' — ' + this.DAY_FULL[dayIdx];
    document.getElementById('cell-current-val').innerHTML = this.cellBadge(cur);
    document.getElementById('day-filter-tag').textContent = this.DAY_FULL[dayIdx];

    // Pre-select current assignment if editing existing
    if (cur && !['Off','Hols','Training','Absent','Sick'].includes(cur)) {
      const parsed = this.parseCell(cur);
      this._pendingRoute   = parsed.route;
      this._pendingVehicle = parsed.vehicle;
    }

    // Route availability — SIMPLE rule:
    // Show only display names where the Day field directly matches the clicked day.
    // Two rows with the SAME route number AND SAME day → both display names shown (true split route).
    // Two rows with the SAME route number but DIFFERENT days → only the one matching this day shown.
    const seenDN = new Set();
    const dayRoutes = [];
    this.D.routes.forEach(r => {
      if (this.routeMatchesDay(r, day) && this.routeAvailableForDate(r, assignmentDate) && !seenDN.has(r.displayName)) {
        seenDN.add(r.displayName);
        dayRoutes.push(r);
      }
    });

    // Taken: routes already assigned to OTHER drivers today (matched by display name)
    const rawTaken = new Set();
    (this.roster.assignments||[]).forEach(asgn => {
      if (asgn.driverId === drvId) return;
      const {route} = this.parseCell(asgn[day]||'');
      if (route && !['Off','Hols','Training','Absent','Sick'].includes(route)) rawTaken.add(route);
    });
    const isTaken   = r => rawTaken.has(r.displayName) || rawTaken.has(r.route);
    const available = dayRoutes.filter(r => !isTaken(r));
    const taken     = dayRoutes.filter(r =>  isTaken(r));

    // Route buttons
    let availHtml = '';
    if (dayRoutes.length === 0) {
      availHtml = `<div class="no-routes-msg">No routes configured for <strong>${this.DAY_FULL[dayIdx]}</strong>.</div>`;
    } else if (available.length === 0) {
      availHtml = `<div class="no-routes-msg">All routes for <strong>${this.DAY_FULL[dayIdx]}</strong> are already assigned.</div>`;
    } else {
      availHtml = available.map(r => {
        const tc = this.textColor(r.colour);
        const brd = this.bgLum(r.colour) > 0.88 ? ';border:2px solid #94a3b8' : '';
        var isSel = r.displayName === this._pendingRoute;
        return `<button class="r-btn" style="background:${r.colour};color:${tc}${brd}${isSel?';outline:2.5px solid #fff':''}${isSel?';transform:scale(1.06)':''}"
          data-dn="${this.esc(r.displayName)}"
          onclick="App.selectPendingRoute('${this.esc(r.displayName)}')">${this.esc(r.displayName)}${String(r.routeType||'STANDARD')==='EXTERNAL'?'<span class="route-type-mini">External delivery</span>':''}</button>`;
      }).join('');
    }
    document.getElementById('cell-route-btns').innerHTML = availHtml;

    // Taken routes
    const takenSec = document.getElementById('cell-taken-section');
    if (taken.length > 0) {
      document.getElementById('cell-taken-btns').innerHTML = taken.map(r => {
        const tc = this.textColor(r.colour);
        const brd = this.bgLum(r.colour) > 0.88 ? ';border:2px solid #94a3b8' : '';
        return `<button class="r-btn r-btn-taken" style="background:${r.colour};color:${tc}${brd}" disabled>
          ${this.esc(r.displayName)}<span class="taken-lbl">assigned</span></button>`;
      }).join('');
      takenSec.style.display = '';
    } else {
      takenSec.style.display = 'none';
    }

    this._renderVehiclePicker(day);
    this._updateAssignButton();
    this.openModal('cell-modal');
  },

  _renderVehiclePicker(day) {
    const usedCounts = {};
    (this.roster.assignments||[]).forEach(asgn => {
      if (this.editingCell && asgn.driverId === this.editingCell.drvId) return;
      const pv = this.parseCell(asgn[day]||'');
      if (!pv.vehicle) return;
      const key = String(pv.vehicle).replace(/\s+/g,'').toUpperCase();
      usedCounts[key] = (usedCounts[key] || 0) + 1;
    });
    const vBtns = (this.D.vehicles||[]).filter(v => String(v.status||'').toLowerCase() !== 'returned').map(v => {
      const isSel       = v.plate === this._pendingVehicle;
      const key         = String(v.plate||'').replace(/\s+/g,'').toUpperCase();
      const useCount    = usedCounts[key] || 0;
      const isVan       = String(v.type||'').toUpperCase() === 'VAN';
      const secondTurn  = isVan && useCount === 1;
      const capacityHit = isVan ? useCount >= 2 : useCount >= 1;
      const unavail     = this.vehIsUnavailable(v);
      const borderline  = !unavail && this.vehIsBorderline(v);
      const blocked     = capacityHit || unavail;

      let title = '';
      if (unavail) {
        const r = (v.maintStart||v.maintEnd)
          ? ' (' + (v.maintStart?this.fmtFull(v.maintStart):'?') + ' → ' + (v.maintEnd?this.fmtFull(v.maintEnd):'?') + ')'
          : '';
        title = (v.status==='maintenance' ? 'In maintenance' : 'Scheduled maintenance') + r;
      } else if (capacityHit) {
        title = isVan ? 'This VAN already has two driver turns today' : 'In use by another driver today';
      } else if (secondTurn) {
        title = 'This VAN is already used once today — assign as second turn (2T)';
      } else if (borderline) {
        title = this.vehBorderlineNote(v) + ' — can be selected';
      }

      const icon = unavail ? (v.status==='maintenance'?'🔧 ':'📅 ') : (borderline ? '⚠️ ' : '');
      const extraStyle = borderline ? ';border-color:#f97316;outline:1px solid #fed7aa' : (secondTurn ? ';border-color:#3b82f6;outline:1px solid #bfdbfe' : '');

      return '<button class="v-btn' + (isSel?' selected':'') + (blocked?' taken-v unavailable':'') + '"'
        + ' data-plate="' + this.esc(v.plate) + '"'
        + (blocked ? '' : ' onclick="App.selectPendingVehicle(\'' + this.esc(v.plate) + '\')"')
        + (blocked ? ' disabled' : '')
        + (title ? ' title="' + this.esc(title) + '"' : '')
        + ' style="' + (blocked?'cursor:not-allowed':'') + extraStyle + '">'
        + '<span class="v-plate">' + icon + this.esc(v.plate) + (secondTurn?'<span class="shift-2t">2T</span>':'') + '</span>'
        + '<span class="v-type">' + this.esc(v.type) + '</span>'
        + (unavail ? '<span style="font-size:9px;color:#dc2626;font-weight:700;margin-top:1px">' + (v.status==='maintenance'?'Maintenance':'Scheduled') + '</span>' : '')
        + (secondTurn ? '<span style="font-size:9px;color:#1d4ed8;font-weight:700;margin-top:1px">Second turn</span>' : '')
        + (borderline ? '<span style="font-size:9px;color:#ea580c;font-weight:600;margin-top:1px">' + this.vehBorderlineNote(v) + '</span>' : '')
        + '</button>';
    }).join('');
    document.getElementById('cell-vehicle-btns').innerHTML = vBtns ||
      '<span style="color:#94a3b8;font-size:13px">No vehicles registered.</span>';
  },

  selectPendingRoute(dn) {
    this._pendingRoute = dn;
    document.querySelectorAll('#cell-route-btns .r-btn').forEach(function(btn) {
      var isSelected = btn.dataset.dn === dn;
      btn.style.outline   = isSelected ? '2.5px solid #fff' : 'none';
      btn.style.transform = isSelected ? 'scale(1.06)' : 'scale(1)';
    });
    this._updateAssignButton();
  },

  selectPendingVehicle(plate) {
    this._pendingVehicle = this._pendingVehicle === plate ? '' : plate;
    document.querySelectorAll('#cell-vehicle-btns .v-btn').forEach(btn => {
      btn.classList.toggle('selected', btn.dataset.plate === this._pendingVehicle);
    });
    this._updateAssignButton();
  },

  _updateAssignButton() {
    const btn = document.getElementById('cell-assign-btn');
    if (!btn) return;
    if (!this._pendingRoute) { btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Select a route above'; return; }
    const rt = this.D.routes.find(r => r.displayName === this._pendingRoute || r.route === this._pendingRoute);
    const rl = rt ? rt.displayName : this._pendingRoute;
    btn.disabled = false;
    let turn = '';
    if (this._pendingVehicle && this.editingCell) {
      const v = (this.D.vehicles||[]).find(x => x.plate === this._pendingVehicle);
      const uses = this._vehicleUsageForDay(this.editingCell.day, this._pendingVehicle, this.editingCell.drvId).length;
      if (v && String(v.type||'').toUpperCase()==='VAN' && uses === 1) turn = ' · 2T';
    }
    btn.textContent = this._pendingVehicle ? `Assign: ${rl}  ·  ${this._pendingVehicle}${turn}` : `Assign: ${rl}  (no vehicle)`;
  },

  confirmCellAssign() {
    if (!this._pendingRoute) return;
    this.assignCell(this.formatCell(this._pendingRoute, this._pendingVehicle));
  },

  assignCell(val) {
    const { drvId, day } = this.editingCell;
    let a = (this.roster.assignments || []).find(x => x.driverId === drvId);
    if (!a) {
      a = { driverId:drvId, mon:'',tue:'',wed:'',thu:'',fri:'',sat:'',sun:'' };
      this.roster.assignments.push(a);
    }
    a[day] = val;
    this.setUnsaved();
    this.closeModal('cell-modal');
    this.renderRoster();
  },

  saveRoster() {
    if (!this.roster.weekStart) return;
    if (this.isWeekLocked(this.roster.weekStart)) {
      this.toast('This roster is locked and cannot be saved.', 'e');
      return;
    }

    // ── Validation ──────────────────────────────────────────
    const STATUS_V = ['Off','Hols','Training','Absent','Sick'];
    const missingAssignment = [];
    const missingVehicle    = [];
    const leaveConflicts    = [];
    const vehicleUses       = {};
    const weekDates = this.getWeekDates(this.roster.weekStart);

    this.D.drivers.forEach(drv => {
      const a = (this.roster.assignments||[]).find(x => String(x.driverId) === String(drv.id))
              || { mon:'',tue:'',wed:'',thu:'',fri:'',sat:'',sun:'' };
      this.DAYS.forEach((day, i) => {
        const val = a[day] || '';
        const leave = this.driverLeaveFor(drv.id, weekDates[i]);

        // Approved leave already fulfils this roster position. The cell is locked
        // in the grid, so an empty stored assignment must not be reported as missing.
        // A stale work assignment is still blocked as a genuine leave conflict.
        if (leave) {
          if (this._isRosterWorkValue(val)) {
            leaveConflicts.push(`${drv.name} — ${this.DAY_LABELS[i]} (${leave.LeaveType || 'Leave'})`);
          }
          return;
        }

        if (!val) {
          missingAssignment.push(`${drv.name} — ${this.DAY_LABELS[i]}`);
        } else if (!STATUS_V.includes(val)) {
          // Has a route: must have a vehicle
          const { vehicle } = this.parseCell(val);
          if (!vehicle) missingVehicle.push(`${drv.name} — ${this.DAY_LABELS[i]}`);
          else {
            const key = day + '|' + String(vehicle).replace(/\s+/g,'').toUpperCase();
            if (!vehicleUses[key]) vehicleUses[key] = { day, vehicle, drivers:[] };
            vehicleUses[key].drivers.push(drv.name);
          }
        }
      });
    });

    const vehicleConflicts = [];
    Object.values(vehicleUses).forEach(use => {
      const v = (this.D.vehicles||[]).find(x => String(x.plate||'').replace(/\s+/g,'').toUpperCase() === String(use.vehicle||'').replace(/\s+/g,'').toUpperCase());
      const isVan = v && String(v.type||'').toUpperCase() === 'VAN';
      const maxUses = isVan ? 2 : 1;
