      if (use.drivers.length > maxUses) vehicleConflicts.push(`${use.vehicle} — ${this.DAY_FULL[this.DAYS.indexOf(use.day)]}: ${use.drivers.join(', ')}`);
    });
    if (vehicleConflicts.length) {
      this.toast(`Vehicle assignment conflict — ${vehicleConflicts.slice(0,2).join(' | ')}`, 'e');
      return;
    }
    if (leaveConflicts.length) {
      const preview = leaveConflicts.slice(0,3).join(', ') + (leaveConflicts.length > 3 ? ` +${leaveConflicts.length-3} more` : '');
      this.toast(`Cannot save: ${leaveConflicts.length} driver leave conflict(s) — ${preview}`, 'e');
      return;
    }
    if (missingAssignment.length) {
      const drivers = Array.from(new Set(missingAssignment.map(m => m.split(' — ')[0])));
      const preview = drivers.slice(0,3).join(', ') + (drivers.length > 3 ? ` +${drivers.length-3} more` : '');
      this.toast(`${missingAssignment.length} day(s) with no assignment — ${preview}`, 'e');
      return;
    }
    if (missingVehicle.length) {
      const drivers = Array.from(new Set(missingVehicle.map(m => m.split(' — ')[0])));
      const preview = drivers.slice(0,3).join(', ') + (drivers.length > 3 ? ` +${drivers.length-3} more` : '');
      this.toast(`${missingVehicle.length} route(s) missing a vehicle — ${preview}`, 'e');
      return;
    }
    // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    this.D.drivers.forEach(d => {
      if (!(this.roster.assignments || []).find(a => a.driverId === d.id)) {
        this.roster.assignments.push({ driverId:d.id, mon:'',tue:'',wed:'',thu:'',fri:'',sat:'',sun:'' });
      }
    });
    const payload = {
      weekStart:      this.roster.weekStart,
      assignments:    this.roster.assignments,
      requiredRoutes: this.roster.requiredRoutes
    };
    const btn = document.getElementById('save-btn');
    this._runBtn(btn, 'Saving...', done => {
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(r => {
          done(r.ok);
          if (r.ok) {
            this.toast('Roster saved successfully ✓', 's');
            this.unsaved = false;
            document.getElementById('unsaved-badge').style.display = 'none';
            if (!this.D.weeks.includes(this.roster.weekStart)) this.D.weeks.unshift(this.roster.weekStart);
          } else { this.toast('Error: ' + r.msg, 'e'); }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(e => { done(false); this.toast('Error: ' + e.message, 'e'); })
        .saveRoster(payload);
    });
  },

  // Publish
  togglePublish() {
    const ws          = this.roster.weekStart;
    const nowPublished = this.roster.published || false;
    const action       = nowPublished ? 'unpublish' : 'publish';
    const msg          = nowPublished
      ? 'Remove this roster from the driver app? Drivers will no longer see next week\'s preview.'
      : 'Publish this roster? Drivers will see it immediately as a "Next Week" preview in their app.';

    document.getElementById('confirm-msg').textContent = msg;
    document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn').textContent = nowPublished ? 'Unpublish' : 'Publish';
    document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn').onclick = () => {
      const okBtn = document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn');
      this._runBtn(okBtn, 'Publishing...', done => {
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(r => {
            done(r.ok);
            if (r.ok) {
              this.roster.published = !nowPublished;
              this.toast(nowPublished ? 'Roster unpublished.' : 'Roster published to drivers!', nowPublished ? 'i' : 's');
              this.closeModal('confirm-modal');
              this._updatePublishBtn();
              document.getElementById('unpublished-note').style.display = this.roster.published ? 'none' : '';
              if (!this.D.weeks.includes(ws)) this.D.weeks.unshift(ws);
            } else { this.toast('Error: ' + r.msg, 'e'); }
          })
          .withFailureHandler(e => { done(false); this.toast(e.message, 'e'); })
          .publishRoster(ws, !nowPublished);
      });
    };
    this.openModal('confirm-modal');
  },

  _updatePublishBtn() {
    const btn = document.getElementById('publish-btn');
    if (!btn) return;
    const pub = this.roster.published || false;
    if (pub) {
      btn.innerHTML = '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" style="width:15px;height:15px;stroke:currentColor;fill:none;stroke-width:2"><path d="M4 12v8a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h12a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-8"/><polyline points="16 6 12 2 8 6"/><line x1="12" y1="2" x2="12" y2="15"/></svg> Published ✓';
      btn.className = 'btn btn-outline btn-published';
    } else {
      btn.innerHTML = '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" style="width:15px;height:15px;stroke:currentColor;fill:none;stroke-width:2"><path d="M4 12v8a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h12a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-8"/><polyline points="16 6 12 2 8 6"/><line x1="12" y1="2" x2="12" y2="15"/></svg> Publish to Drivers';
      btn.className = 'btn btn-outline';
    }
  },

  // ── Button loading state ────────────────────────────────────
  // Usage: this._runBtn(btnEl, 'Saving...', fn)
  // fn receives (done) — call done(true) on success, done(false) on error to restore
  _runBtn(btn, loadingText, fn) {
    // No button (e.g. action triggered without a clickable element): just run.
    if (!btn) { fn(function(){}); return; }
    if (btn.disabled) return; // already running
    const orig = btn.innerHTML;
    const origW = btn.offsetWidth;
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.style.minWidth = origW + 'px';
    btn.innerHTML = '<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px">'
      + '<svg style="width:14px;height:14px;animation:spin .7s linear infinite;flex-shrink:0" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5"><path d="M21 12a9 9 0 1 1-6.219-8.56"/></svg>'
      + loadingText + '</span>';
    let restored = false;
    fn(function(success) {
      if (restored) return;
      restored = true;
      btn.disabled = false;
      btn.style.minWidth = '';
      btn.innerHTML = orig;
    });
  },

  syncColourPicker(textId, pickerId) {
    var v = document.getElementById(textId).value;
    if (/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(v)) document.getElementById(pickerId).value = v;
  },

  // Vehicle return notifications
  _checkPendingReturns() {
    const pending = this.D.pendingReturns || [];
    const btn = document.getElementById('vehicle-return-btn');
    if (!btn) return;
    if (pending.length) {
      btn.style.display = 'inline-flex';
      document.getElementById('vehicle-return-label').textContent =
        pending.length + ' vehicle' + (pending.length !== 1 ? 's' : '') + ' — confirm return';
      // Auto-open modal on load if there are pending returns
      setTimeout(() => this.openReturnModal(), 1200);
    } else {
      btn.style.display = 'none';
    }
  },

  openReturnModal() {
    const pending = this.D.pendingReturns || [];
    if (!pending.length) return;
    const list = document.getElementById('return-vehicles-list');
    list.innerHTML = pending.map(v => {
      const overdueDays = Math.round((new Date() - new Date(v.maintEnd + 'T12:00:00Z')) / 86400000);
      const overdue = overdueDays === 1 ? '1 day overdue' : overdueDays + ' days overdue';
      const sourceLabel = v.source === 'log'
        ? '<span style="font-size:10px;background:#eff6ff;color:#1d4ed8;padding:1px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-weight:600;margin-left:5px">from log</span>'
        : '';
      return '<div style="background:var(--card);border:1px solid #fecaca;border-radius:10px;padding:14px 16px">'
        + '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap">'
        +   '<div>'
        +     '<div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:800;font-family:monospace">' + this.esc(v.plate) + sourceLabel + '</div>'
        +     '<div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--txt2);margin-top:2px">' + this.esc(v.type)
        +       ' &nbsp;·&nbsp; Maint. ended <strong>' + this.fmtFull(v.maintEnd) + '</strong>'
        +       ' <span style="color:#dc2626;font-weight:700">(' + overdue + ')</span>'
        +     '</div>'
        +   '</div>'
        +   '<div style="display:flex;gap:6px">'
        +     '<button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onclick="App.extendMaintenance(\'' + this.esc(v.plate) + '\')">'
        +       '📅 Extend'
        +     '</button>'
        +     '<button class="btn btn-success btn-sm" onclick="App.confirmReturn(\'' + this.esc(v.plate) + '\')">'
        +       '✅ Confirm Return'
        +     '</button>'
        +   '</div>'
        + '</div>'
        + '</div>';
    }).join('');
    this.openModal('vehicle-return-modal');
  },

  confirmReturn(plate) {
    const btn = document.querySelector('#return-vehicles-list button.btn-success[onclick*="' + plate + '"]');
    if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Confirming...'; }
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(r => {
        if (r.ok) {
          this.toast(plate + ' marked as returned. Maint Log updated.', 's');
          // Remove from pending list
          this.D.pendingReturns = (this.D.pendingReturns||[]).filter(v => v.plate !== plate);
          // Update local vehicles
          const vObj = (this.D.vehicles||[]).find(v => v.plate === plate);
          if (vObj) { vObj.status = 'available'; vObj.maintStart = ''; vObj.maintEnd = ''; }
          this._checkPendingReturns();
          // Re-render the modal list or close if empty
          if (!this.D.pendingReturns.length) {
            this.closeModal('vehicle-return-modal');
          } else {
            this.openReturnModal();
          }
          // Refresh vehicles tab if open
          if (document.getElementById('s-vehicles').classList.contains('active')) {
            this.renderVehicles();
          }
        } else { this.toast('Error: ' + r.msg, 'e'); }
      })
      .withFailureHandler(e => this.toast(e.message, 'e'))
      .confirmVehicleReturn(plate);
  },

  extendMaintenance(plate) {
    this.closeModal('vehicle-return-modal');
    const vObj = (this.D.vehicles||[]).find(v => v.plate === plate);
    if (vObj) {
      this.nav('vehicles');
      setTimeout(() => this.openVehicleModal(vObj), 200);
    }
  },

  setUnsaved() {
    this.unsaved = true;
    document.getElementById('unsaved-badge').style.display = '';
  },

  // Drivers
  _showInactive: false,

  toggleInactive() {
    this._showInactive = !this._showInactive;
    const list = document.getElementById('inactive-drivers-list');
    const btn  = document.getElementById('inactive-toggle-btn');
    list.style.display = this._showInactive ? '' : 'none';
    btn.innerHTML = (this._showInactive
      ? '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" style="width:13px;height:13px;stroke:currentColor;fill:none;stroke-width:2"><polyline points="18 15 12 9 6 15"/></svg> Hide inactive drivers'
      : '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" style="width:13px;height:13px;stroke:currentColor;fill:none;stroke-width:2"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9"/></svg> Show inactive drivers');
  },

  renderDrivers() {
    const allDrivers = this.D.allDrivers || this.D.drivers;
    const active   = allDrivers.filter(d => d.active !== false);
    const inactive = allDrivers.filter(d => d.active === false);

    const tb = document.getElementById('drivers-tbody');
    if (!active.length) {
      tb.innerHTML = '<tr><td><div class="empty-state"><h3>No active drivers</h3><p>Click "New Driver" to add one.</p></div></td></tr>';
    } else {
      tb.innerHTML = active.map((d) => {
        const safeD  = JSON.stringify(d).replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
        const inf = this.driverInfringementSummary(d.id);
        const risk = this.driverRiskLevel(inf);
        const lastTxt = inf.total && inf.daysSinceLast !== '-' ? inf.daysSinceLast + ' days ago' : 'None';
        return `<tr class="driver-card-row"><td>
          <div class="driver-summary">
            <div>
              <div class="driver-name-main">${this.esc(d.name)}${d.rosterHidden ? '<span class="roster-hidden-pill">Hidden from Roster</span>' : ''}</div>
              <div class="driver-license-sub">${this.esc(d.license || 'Licence not set')}</div>
            </div>
            <div class="driver-metric">Last Infringement<strong>${this.esc(lastTxt)}</strong></div>
            <div class="driver-metric">Risk Score<strong>${this.riskBadge(risk)}</strong></div>
            <div class="driver-metric">Infringements<strong>${inf.total || 0}</strong></div>
            <div class="driver-metric">Most Serious<strong>${this.esc(inf.mostSerious || '—')}</strong></div>
            <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;justify-content:flex-end;flex-wrap:wrap">
              <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onclick='App.openDriverProfile(${safeD})'>Profile</button>
              <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="App.openDriverModal(${safeD})">Edit</button>
              <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="App.toggleDriverRosterHidden('${d.id}', ${d.rosterHidden ? 'false' : 'true'}, '${this.esc(d.name)}', event)">${d.rosterHidden ? 'Show in Roster' : 'Hide from Roster'}</button>
              <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="App.openInfringementModal(null,'${d.id}')">+ Infringement</button>
              <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" style="color:var(--txt2)" onclick="App.confirmDeactivateDriver('${d.id}','${this.esc(d.name)}')">Deactivate</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </td></tr>`;
      }).join('');
    }

    const wrap = document.getElementById('inactive-drivers-wrap');
    const itb  = document.getElementById('inactive-drivers-tbody');
    if (inactive.length) {
      wrap.style.display = '';
      const toggleBtn = document.getElementById('inactive-toggle-btn');
      if (!this._showInactive) {
        toggleBtn.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" style="width:13px;height:13px;stroke:currentColor;fill:none;stroke-width:2"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9"/></svg> Show inactive drivers (${inactive.length})`;
      }
      itb.innerHTML = inactive.map(d => {
        const period = [
          d.joinedDate      ? `from ${this.fmtFull(d.joinedDate)}`      : '',
          d.deactivatedDate ? `deactivated ${this.fmtFull(d.deactivatedDate)}` : ''
        ].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
        const inf = this.driverInfringementSummary(d.id);
        return `<tr style="color:var(--txt2)"><td>
          <div class="driver-summary" style="opacity:.82">
            <div>
              <div class="driver-name-main">${this.esc(d.name)} <span style="font-size:10px;color:#94a3b8;background:#f1f5f9;padding:1px 5px;border-radius:3px;font-weight:600">inactive</span></div>
              <div class="driver-license-sub">${this.esc(d.license || 'Licence not set')}${period ? ' · ' + this.esc(period) : ''}</div>
            </div>
            <div class="driver-metric">Last Infringement<strong>${inf.total && inf.daysSinceLast !== '-' ? inf.daysSinceLast + ' days ago' : 'None'}</strong></div>
            <div class="driver-metric">Risk Score<strong>${this.riskBadge(this.driverRiskLevel(inf))}</strong></div>
            <div class="driver-metric">Infringements<strong>${inf.total || 0}</strong></div>
            <div class="driver-metric">Most Serious<strong>${this.esc(inf.mostSerious || '—')}</strong></div>
            <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;justify-content:flex-end;flex-wrap:wrap">
              <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onclick='App.openDriverProfile(${JSON.stringify(d).replace(/"/g,'&quot;')})'>Profile</button>
              <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="App.openInfringementModal(null,'${d.id}')">+ Infringement</button>
              <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" style="color:var(--green)" onclick="App.reactivateDriver('${d.id}','${this.esc(d.name)}', event)">Reactivate</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </td></tr>`;
      }).join('');
    } else {
      wrap.style.display = 'none';
    }
  },

  openDriverModal(drv) {
    document.getElementById('driver-modal-title').textContent = drv ? 'Edit Driver' : 'New Driver';
    document.getElementById('drv-id').value   = drv ? drv.id : '';
    document.getElementById('drv-name').value = drv ? drv.name : '';
    document.getElementById('drv-lic').value  = drv ? drv.license : '';
    document.getElementById('drv-roster-hidden').checked = !!(drv && drv.rosterHidden);
    this.openModal('driver-modal');
    setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('drv-name').focus(), 100);
  },

  saveDriver() {
    const id   = document.getElementById('drv-id').value;
    const name = document.getElementById('drv-name').value.trim();
    const lic  = document.getElementById('drv-lic').value.trim();
    const rosterHidden = document.getElementById('drv-roster-hidden').checked;
    if (!name) { this.toast('Name is required.', 'e'); return; }
    const payload = { id, name, license:lic, rosterHidden, isNew:!id };
    // Check for a possible duplicate name first (alerts, does not block).
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(res => {
        const dups = (res && res.duplicates) || [];
        if (dups.length) {
          const names = dups.map(d => d.name + (d.active===false?' (inactive)':'')).join(', ');
          this._confirmDuplicate(
            'Possible duplicate driver',
            `A driver named <strong>${this.esc(name)}</strong> already exists: ${this.esc(names)}.<br><br>Do you still want to add this as a new driver?`,
            () => this._doSaveDriver(payload)
          );
        } else {
          this._doSaveDriver(payload);
        }
      })
      .withFailureHandler(() => this._doSaveDriver(payload))  // if check fails, don't block saving
      .checkDuplicateDriver(name, id || '');
  },

  _doSaveDriver(payload) {
    const saveBtn = document.querySelector('#driver-modal .btn-primary');
    this._runBtn(saveBtn, 'Saving...', done => {
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(r => {
          done(r.ok);
          if (r.ok) {
            this.toast('Driver saved!', 's'); this.closeModal('driver-modal');
            this._reloadDrivers();
          } else { this.toast('Error: ' + r.msg, 'e'); }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(e => { done(false); this.toast(e.message, 'e'); })
        .saveDriver(payload);
    });
  },

  // Generic duplicate-warning dialog. Reuses the confirm modal.
  _confirmDuplicate(title, messageHtml, onProceed) {
    const msgEl = document.getElementById('confirm-msg');
    msgEl.innerHTML = '⚠️ <strong>' + this.esc(title) + '</strong><br><br>' + messageHtml;
    const okBtn = document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn');
    okBtn.textContent = 'Add anyway';
    okBtn.className = 'btn btn-primary';   // not destructive — it's a proceed action
    const restore = () => { okBtn.className = 'btn btn-danger'; };  // back to default for deletes
    okBtn.onclick = () => { this.closeModal('confirm-modal'); restore(); onProceed(); };
    // Also restore if the user cancels (closes the modal without proceeding).
    const overlay = document.getElementById('confirm-modal');
    const onClose = () => { restore(); overlay.removeEventListener('click', onClose); };
    this.openModal('confirm-modal');
  },


  toggleDriverRosterHidden(id, hidden, name, ev) {
    this.runBusy(ev, hidden ? 'Hiding…' : 'Showing…',
      run => run.setDriverRosterHidden(id, hidden),
      r => {
        if (r && r.ok) {
          this.toast((hidden ? 'Hidden from roster: ' : 'Visible in roster: ') + name, 's');
          this._reloadDrivers();
        } else this.toast('Error: ' + ((r && r.msg) || 'Could not update driver'), 'e');
      });
  },

  confirmDeactivateDriver(id, name) {
    document.getElementById('confirm-msg').textContent =
      `Deactivate "${name}"? They will remain in this week's roster but will not appear in future weeks. All historical records are preserved.`;
    document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn').textContent = 'Deactivate';
    document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn').onclick = () => {
      const okBtn = document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn');
      this._runBtn(okBtn, 'Deactivating…', done => {
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(r => {
            done(r && r.ok);
            if (r && r.ok) {
              this.toast(`${name} deactivated.`, 'i'); this.closeModal('confirm-modal');
              this._reloadDrivers();
            } else { this.toast('Error: ' + ((r && r.msg) || 'Could not deactivate'), 'e'); }
          })
          .withFailureHandler(e => { done(false); this.toast(e.message, 'e'); })
          .deactivateDriver(id);
      });
    };
    this.openModal('confirm-modal');
  },

  reactivateDriver(id, name, ev) {
    this.runBusy(ev, 'Reactivating…',
      run => run.reactivateDriver(id),
      r => {
        if (r && r.ok) {
          this.toast(`${name} reactivated!`, 's');
          this._reloadDrivers();
        } else { this.toast('Error: ' + ((r && r.msg) || 'Could not reactivate'), 'e'); }
      });
  },


  // Driver infringements
  infringementSeverities: ['No Severity','Minor infringement','Serious infringement','Very serious infringement','Most serious infringement'],
  infringementTypes: ['Working Time break','Driving Break (EU 561)','Daily Rest (EU 561)','Daily Driving (EU 561)','Daily Walkaround Check'],

  _infrRows() { return this.D.infringements || []; },

  driverInfringementSummary(driverId) {
    const rows = this._infrRows().filter(r => String(r.DriverID||r.driverId||'') === String(driverId));
    if (!rows.length) return { total:0, last:'', daysSinceLast:'-', mostSerious:'', averageGap:'-' };
    const sorted = rows.slice().sort((a,b) => String(b.OccurredAt||'').localeCompare(String(a.OccurredAt||'')));
    const last = String(sorted[0].OccurredAt || '').slice(0,10);
    let daysSinceLast = '-';
    if (last) {
      const diff = Math.floor((new Date().setHours(0,0,0,0) - new Date(last+'T00:00:00').getTime()) / 86400000);
      daysSinceLast = isFinite(diff) ? Math.max(0, diff) : '-';
    }
    const sevRank = { 'no severity':0, 'minor infringement':1, 'serious infringement':2, 'very serious infringement':3, 'most serious infringement':4 };
    const most = rows.reduce((best, r) => {
      const s = String(r.Severity || '');
      return (sevRank[s.toLowerCase()] || 0) > (sevRank[String(best||'').toLowerCase()] || 0) ? s : best;
    }, '');
    let averageGap = '-';
    const dates = rows.map(r => String(r.OccurredAt||'').slice(0,10)).filter(Boolean).sort();
    if (dates.length > 1) {
      let totalGap = 0, gaps = 0;
      for (let i=1;i<dates.length;i++) {
        const gap = Math.round((new Date(dates[i]+'T00:00:00') - new Date(dates[i-1]+'T00:00:00')) / 86400000);
        if (isFinite(gap) && gap >= 0) { totalGap += gap; gaps++; }
      }
      if (gaps) averageGap = Math.round(totalGap / gaps);
    }
    return { total: rows.length, last, daysSinceLast, mostSerious: most, averageGap };
  },

  driverRiskLevel(summary) {
    summary = summary || {};
    const most = String(summary.mostSerious || '').toLowerCase();
    if (most.indexOf('most serious') >= 0 || most.indexOf('very serious') >= 0) return 'High';
    if (most.indexOf('serious') >= 0 || (parseInt(summary.total,10)||0) >= 8 || (summary.daysSinceLast !== '-' && Number(summary.daysSinceLast) <= 14 && (summary.total||0) >= 3)) return 'Medium';
    return 'Low';
  },

  riskBadge(level) {
    const l = String(level || 'Low');
    const cls = l === 'High' ? 'risk-high' : (l === 'Medium' ? 'risk-medium' : 'risk-low');
    const dot = l === 'High' ? '🔴' : (l === 'Medium' ? '🟠' : '🟢');
    return `<span class="risk-pill ${cls}">${dot} ${this.esc(l)}</span>`;
  },

  openDriverProfile(drv) {
    this._driverProfile = drv || null;
    const inf = this.driverInfringementSummary(drv && drv.id);
    document.getElementById('driver-profile-title').textContent = drv ? ('Driver Profile — ' + drv.name) : 'Driver Profile';
    this.switchDriverProfileTab('licence');
    this.openModal('driver-profile-modal');
  },

  switchDriverProfileTab(tab) {
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-driver-tab]').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.driverTab === tab));
    const d = this._driverProfile || {};
    const inf = this.driverInfringementSummary(d.id);
    const risk = this.driverRiskLevel(inf);
    const rows = this._infrRows().filter(r => String(r.DriverID||'') === String(d.id)).sort((a,b) => String(b.OccurredAt||'').localeCompare(String(a.OccurredAt||'')));
    const body = document.getElementById('driver-profile-body');
    if (!body) return;
    if (tab === 'licence') {
      body.innerHTML = `<div class="card" style="padding:16px"><div class="driver-name-main">${this.esc(d.name||'')}</div><div class="driver-license-sub">${this.esc(d.license||'Licence not set')}</div></div>`;
    } else if (tab === 'employment') {
      body.innerHTML = `<table class="data-table"><tbody>
        <tr><th>Driver ID</th><td>${this.esc(d.id||'')}</td></tr>
        <tr><th>Joined</th><td>${d.joinedDate ? this.fmtFull(d.joinedDate) : '—'}</td></tr>
        <tr><th>Status</th><td>${d.active === false ? 'Inactive' : 'Active'}</td></tr>
        <tr><th>Deactivated</th><td>${d.deactivatedDate ? this.fmtFull(d.deactivatedDate) : '—'}</td></tr>
      </tbody></table>`;
    } else if (tab === 'infringements') {
      body.innerHTML = `<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(150px,1fr));gap:10px;margin-bottom:12px">
        <div class="vehicle-defect-card"><div class="k">Total</div><div class="v">${inf.total||0}</div></div>
        <div class="vehicle-defect-card"><div class="k">Most Serious</div><div style="font-weight:900;margin-top:7px">${this.esc(inf.mostSerious||'—')}</div></div>
        <div class="vehicle-defect-card"><div class="k">Last</div><div style="font-weight:900;margin-top:7px">${inf.daysSinceLast !== '-' ? inf.daysSinceLast + ' days ago' : '—'}</div></div>
        <div class="vehicle-defect-card"><div class="k">Average Gap</div><div style="font-weight:900;margin-top:7px">${inf.averageGap !== '-' ? inf.averageGap + ' days' : '—'}</div></div>
        <div class="vehicle-defect-card"><div class="k">Risk Level</div><div style="margin-top:7px">${this.riskBadge(risk)}</div></div>
      </div>` + (rows.length ? `<table class="data-table"><thead><tr><th>Date</th><th>Severity</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map(r=>`<tr><td>${this.esc(String(r.OccurredAt||'').slice(0,10))}</td><td>${this.esc(r.Severity||'')}</td><td>${this.esc(r.InfringementType||'')}</td><td>${this.esc(r.Description||'')}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table>` : '<div class="empty-state"><h3>No infringements</h3></div>');
    } else if (tab === 'performance') {
      body.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state"><h3>Performance</h3><p>Performance metrics can be linked to route hours/KM in a future build.</p></div>';
    } else {
      body.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state"><h3>Training</h3><p>Training records can be added when the training module is connected.</p></div>';
    }
  },

  ensureInfringementsLoaded(cb) {
    if (this._infrLoaded) { if (cb) cb(); return; }
    if (this._infrLoading) return;
    this.reloadInfringements(cb);
  },

  reloadInfringements(cb) {
    this._infrLoading = true;
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(res => {
      this._infrLoading = false;
      if (res && res.ok) {
        this.D.infringements = res.rows || [];
        this._infrLoaded = true;
      }
      this.renderDrivers();
      if (this._histTab === 'infr') this.renderInfringements();
      if (cb) cb(res);
    }).withFailureHandler(e => { this._infrLoading = false; this.toast(e.message,'e'); }).getAllDriverInfringementsForAdmin();
  },

  _populateInfrFilters() {
    const fd = document.getElementById('infr-filter-driver');
    const fs = document.getElementById('infr-filter-sev');
    if (fd && !fd.dataset.ready) {
      const all = this.D.allDrivers || this.D.drivers || [];
      fd.innerHTML = '<option value="">All drivers</option>' + all.map(d => `<option value="${this.esc(d.id)}">${this.esc(d.name)}</option>`).join('');
      fd.dataset.ready = '1';
    }
    if (fs && !fs.dataset.ready) {
      fs.innerHTML = '<option value="">All severities</option>' + this.infringementSeverities.map(s => `<option value="${this.esc(s)}">${this.esc(s)}</option>`).join('');
      fs.dataset.ready = '1';
    }
  },

  filterInfringementsForDriver(driverId) {
    this._populateInfrFilters();
    const fd = document.getElementById('infr-filter-driver');
    if (fd) fd.value = driverId || '';
    this.renderInfringements();
  },

  renderInfringements() {
    this._populateInfrFilters();
    const tb = document.getElementById('infr-tbody');
    if (!tb) return;
    if (!this._infrLoaded && this._infrLoading) {
      tb.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="6"><div class="empty-state"><p>Loading infringements...</p></div></td></tr>';
      return;
    }
    const driverFilter = (document.getElementById('infr-filter-driver') || {}).value || '';
    const sevFilter = (document.getElementById('infr-filter-sev') || {}).value || '';
    let rows = this._infrRows().slice();
    if (driverFilter) rows = rows.filter(r => String(r.DriverID||'') === driverFilter);
    if (sevFilter) rows = rows.filter(r => String(r.Severity||'') === sevFilter);
    rows.sort((a,b) => String(b.OccurredAt||'').localeCompare(String(a.OccurredAt||'')));

    const summary = document.getElementById('infr-summary');
    if (summary) {
      const byDriver = {};
      this._infrRows().forEach(r => {
        const id = String(r.DriverID || '');
        if (!id) return;
        if (!byDriver[id]) byDriver[id] = { total:0, last:'' };
        byDriver[id].total++;
        const occ = String(r.OccurredAt || '').slice(0,10);
        if (occ > byDriver[id].last) byDriver[id].last = occ;
      });
      const cards = Object.keys(byDriver).sort((a,b)=>byDriver[b].total-byDriver[a].total).slice(0,6).map(id => {
        const d = (this.D.allDrivers||[]).find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id)) || {name:id};
        const s = this.driverInfringementSummary(id);
        return `<button class="card" style="padding:12px;text-align:left;border:1px solid var(--border);background:#fff;cursor:pointer" onclick="App.filterInfringementsForDriver('${id}')"><div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--txt2);font-weight:700">${this.esc(d.name)}</div><div style="font-size:22px;font-weight:900;margin-top:3px">${s.total}</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--txt2)">${s.daysSinceLast} days since last</div></button>`;
      }).join('');
      summary.innerHTML = cards || '<div class="empty-state" style="grid-column:1/-1;padding:24px"><h3>No infringements registered yet</h3></div>';
    }

    if (!rows.length) {
      tb.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="6"><div class="empty-state"><h3>No infringements found</h3><p>Add a notification using the button above.</p></div></td></tr>';
      return;
    }
    tb.innerHTML = rows.map(r => {
      const safe = JSON.stringify(r).replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
      const sev = this.esc(r.Severity || '');
      const sevClass = sev.includes('Most') || sev.includes('Very') ? 'st-maint' : (sev.includes('Serious') ? 'st-sched' : 'st-ok');
      return `<tr>
        <td><strong>${this.esc(r.DriverName || r.DriverID || '')}</strong></td>
        <td style="font-size:12px">${this.esc(this._fmtDateTime(r.OccurredAt))}</td>
        <td><span class="st-badge ${sevClass}">${sev}</span></td>
        <td>${this.esc(r.InfringementType || '')}</td>
        <td style="max-width:360px;white-space:normal">${this.esc(r.Description || '')}</td>
        <td><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="App.openInfringementModal(${safe})">Edit</button></td>
      </tr>`;
    }).join('');
  },

  _fmtDateTime(v) {
    if (!v) return '';
    const s = String(v);
    if (s.includes('T')) return s.replace('T',' ').slice(0,16);
    return s;
  },

  openInfringementModal(entry, presetDriverId) {
    const all = this.D.allDrivers || this.D.drivers || [];
    document.getElementById('infr-modal-title').textContent = entry ? 'Edit Driver Infringement' : 'Add Driver Infringement';
    document.getElementById('infr-id').value = entry ? (entry.InfringementID || '') : '';
    document.getElementById('infr-driver').innerHTML = '<option value="">Select driver...</option>' + all.map(d => `<option value="${this.esc(d.id)}" data-name="${this.esc(d.name)}">${this.esc(d.name)}</option>`).join('');
    document.getElementById('infr-severity').innerHTML = '<option value="">Select severity...</option>' + this.infringementSeverities.map(s => `<option value="${this.esc(s)}">${this.esc(s)}</option>`).join('');
    document.getElementById('infr-type-list').innerHTML = this.infringementTypes.map(t => `<option value="${this.esc(t)}"></option>`).join('');
    document.getElementById('infr-driver').value = entry ? String(entry.DriverID || '') : (presetDriverId || '');
    document.getElementById('infr-occurred').value = entry ? String(entry.OccurredAt || '').slice(0,16) : '';
    document.getElementById('infr-severity').value = entry ? String(entry.Severity || '') : '';
    document.getElementById('infr-type').value = entry ? String(entry.InfringementType || '') : '';
    document.getElementById('infr-desc').value = entry ? String(entry.Description || '') : '';
    document.getElementById('delete-infr-btn').style.display = entry ? '' : 'none';
    this.openModal('infringement-modal');
  },

  saveInfringement() {
    const sel = document.getElementById('infr-driver');
    const driverId = sel.value;
    const opt = sel.options[sel.selectedIndex];
    const driverName = opt ? (opt.getAttribute('data-name') || opt.textContent || '') : '';
    const payload = {
      infringementId: document.getElementById('infr-id').value,
      driverId, driverName,
      occurredAt: document.getElementById('infr-occurred').value,
      severity: document.getElementById('infr-severity').value,
      infringementType: document.getElementById('infr-type').value.trim(),
      description: document.getElementById('infr-desc').value.trim()
    };
    if (!payload.driverId || !payload.occurredAt || !payload.severity || !payload.infringementType || !payload.description) {
      this.toast('Driver, date/time, severity, type and description are required.', 'e'); return;
    }
    const btn = document.getElementById('save-infr-btn');
    this._runBtn(btn, 'Saving...', done => {
      google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(r => {
        done(r.ok);
        if (r.ok) { this.toast('Infringement saved.', 's'); this.closeModal('infringement-modal'); this.reloadInfringements(); }
        else this.toast('Error: ' + r.msg, 'e');
      }).withFailureHandler(e => { done(false); this.toast(e.message,'e'); }).saveDriverInfringement(payload);
    });
  },

  confirmDeleteInfringement() {
    const id = document.getElementById('infr-id').value;
    if (!id) return;
    document.getElementById('confirm-msg').textContent = 'Delete this infringement record?';
    document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn').textContent = 'Delete';
    document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn').className = 'btn btn-danger';
    document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn').onclick = () => {
      google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(r => {
        if (r.ok) { this.toast('Infringement deleted.', 'i'); this.closeModal('confirm-modal'); this.closeModal('infringement-modal'); this.reloadInfringements(); }
        else this.toast('Error: ' + r.msg, 'e');
      }).deleteDriverInfringement(id);
    };
    this.openModal('confirm-modal');
  },

  _showVehicleConflictAlert(plate, affected) {
    const total = affected.length;
    const drivers = Array.from(new Set(affected.map(a => a.driverName)));
    document.getElementById('conflict-intro').innerHTML =
      `Vehicle <strong>${this.esc(plate)}</strong> has been marked as unavailable, but is still assigned to `
      + `<strong>${total} shift${total!==1?'s':''}</strong> across <strong>${drivers.length} driver${drivers.length!==1?'s':''}</strong> in current or future rosters.`;

    // Group by week
    const byWeek = {};
    affected.forEach(a => {
      if (!byWeek[a.weekStart]) byWeek[a.weekStart] = [];
      byWeek[a.weekStart].push(a);
    });

    let html = '';
    Object.keys(byWeek).sort().forEach(wk => {
      const dates = this.getWeekDates(wk);
      html += `<div style="background:#fff;border:1px solid #fecaca;border-radius:8px;overflow:hidden">
        <div style="background:#fef2f2;padding:6px 12px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#dc2626;border-bottom:1px solid #fecaca">
          Week of ${this.fmtFull(dates[0])}
        </div>
        <div style="padding:6px 0">`;
      byWeek[wk].forEach(a => {
        html += `<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:5px 12px;font-size:13px">
          <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" style="width:14px;height:14px;stroke:#dc2626;fill:none;stroke-width:2;flex-shrink:0"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><line x1="12" y1="8" x2="12" y2="12"/><line x1="12" y1="16" x2="12.01" y2="16"/></svg>
          <strong>${this.esc(a.driverName)}</strong>
          <span style="color:#6b7280">—</span>
          <span style="background:#fee2e2;color:#991b1b;padding:1px 7px;border-radius:4px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700">${a.day}</span>
        </div>`;
      });
      html += `</div></div>`;
    });
    document.getElementById('conflict-list').innerHTML = html;
    this.openModal('vehicle-conflict-modal');
  },

  _reloadDrivers(cb) {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(all => {
      this.D.allDrivers = all;
      this.D.drivers    = all.filter(d => d.active !== false && !d.rosterHidden);
      this.renderDrivers();
      // Rebuild the roster grid so visibility changes (hide/show, (de)activate) show up at once
      if (this.roster && this.roster.weekStart) {
        this._applyRoster(this.roster, this.roster.weekStart);
      }
      if (typeof cb === 'function') cb();
    }).getAllDrivers();
  },

  editRouteStat(cell, routeNum, day, field, currentVal) {
    const unit = field === 'km' ? ' km' : 'h';
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type      = 'number';
    input.min       = '0';
    input.step      = field === 'hours' ? '0.5' : '1';
    input.value     = currentVal || '';
    input.className = 'rt-stat-input';
    input.placeholder = '0';
    cell.innerHTML = '';
    cell.appendChild(input);
    input.focus();
    input.select();
    let saved = false;

    const renderVal = val => (isNaN(val) || val === 0)
      ? '<span style="color:#94a3b8">—</span>'
      : val + unit;

    const save = () => {
      if (saved) return; saved = true;
      const val   = parseFloat(input.value);
      const stats = {};
      stats[field] = isNaN(val) ? 0 : val;
      input.disabled = true;
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(r => {
          if (r.ok) {
            // Update exact matching row in local data by route + day
            (this.D.routes||[]).forEach(rt => {
              if (rt.route === routeNum && (rt.day||'') === day) {
                rt[field] = isNaN(val) ? 0 : val;
              }
            });
            cell.innerHTML = renderVal(val);
          } else {
            cell.innerHTML = renderVal(parseFloat(currentVal));
            this.toast('Error: ' + r.msg, 'e');
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(e => {
          cell.innerHTML = renderVal(parseFloat(currentVal));
          this.toast(e.message, 'e');
        })
        .saveRouteStats(routeNum, day, stats);
    };

    const cancel = () => {
      if (saved) return; saved = true;
      cell.innerHTML = renderVal(parseFloat(currentVal));
    };

    input.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
      if (e.key === 'Enter')  { e.preventDefault(); save(); }
      if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.preventDefault(); cancel(); }
    });
    input.addEventListener('blur', save);
  },

  // Routes
  // Compatibility bridge: older callbacks still call renderRoutes(), but the
  // current Routes screen is rendered by the delivery planner (#route-planner).
  // Never write to the removed legacy table (#routes-tbody).
  renderRoutes(forceReload) {
    this._resetCustFilter();
    if (forceReload === false) {
      this._renderPlanner();
      return;
    }
    this.renderCustomers(true);
  },

  _nextExternalRouteNumber() {
    const used = new Set((this.D.routes||[]).map(r => String(r.route||'').toUpperCase()));
    let n = 1;
    while (used.has('EXT-' + String(n).padStart(3,'0'))) n++;
    return 'EXT-' + String(n).padStart(3,'0');
  },

  _routeDayNames(){ return ['Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday','Sunday']; },

  _getExternalRouteDays(){
    return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#rt-day-checks input[type="checkbox"]:checked')).map(x=>x.value);
  },

  _setExternalRouteDays(days){
    const wanted=new Set((days||[]).map(x=>String(x).toLowerCase()));
    document.querySelectorAll('#rt-day-checks input[type="checkbox"]').forEach(cb=>{ cb.checked=wanted.has(String(cb.value).toLowerCase()); });
    this.updateExternalRouteDayCount();
  },

  updateExternalRouteDayCount(){
    const n=this._getExternalRouteDays().length;
    const el=document.getElementById('rt-day-count');
    if(el) el.textContent=n+' day'+(n===1?'':'s')+' selected';
  },

  setExternalRouteDayPreset(preset){
    const map={MWF:['Monday','Wednesday','Friday'],TTH:['Tuesday','Thursday'],WEEKDAYS:['Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday'],ALL:this._routeDayNames(),CLEAR:[]};
    this._setExternalRouteDays(map[preset]||[]);
  },

  _routeColourOptions(){
    const seen={}, out=[];
    (this.D.routes||[]).forEach(r=>{
      const colour=String(r.colour||'').trim();
      if(!/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(colour)) return;
      const key=colour.toLowerCase();
      if(!seen[key]){ seen[key]={colour:colour,names:[]}; out.push(seen[key]); }
      const name=String(r.displayName||('Route '+r.route)).trim();
      if(name && seen[key].names.indexOf(name)<0) seen[key].names.push(name);
    });
    return out;
  },

  _syncRouteColourPreview(colour){
    const c=/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(String(colour||''))?String(colour):'#94a3b8';
    document.getElementById('rt-colour').value=c;
    const preview=document.getElementById('rt-colour-preview'); if(preview) preview.style.background=c;
  },

  _fillRouteColourOptions(selectedColour){
    const select=document.getElementById('rt-colour-choice'); if(!select) return;
    const options=this._routeColourOptions();
    let selected=String(selectedColour||'').trim();
    const found=options.find(x=>x.colour.toLowerCase()===selected.toLowerCase());
    let html=options.map(x=>{
      const names=x.names.slice(0,3).join(' / ')+(x.names.length>3?' +'+(x.names.length-3):'');
      return '<option value="'+this.esc(x.colour)+'">'+this.esc(names||'Existing route')+' — '+this.esc(x.colour.toUpperCase())+'</option>';
    }).join('');
    html+='<option value="__NEW__">＋ New colour…</option>';
    select.innerHTML=html;
    if(found){ select.value=found.colour; this._syncRouteColourPreview(found.colour); }
    else if(selected && /^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(selected)){
      select.value='__NEW__';
      document.getElementById('rt-colour-picker').value=selected;
      document.getElementById('rt-colour-new').value=selected;
      this._syncRouteColourPreview(selected);
    } else if(options.length){ select.value=options[0].colour; this._syncRouteColourPreview(options[0].colour); }
    else {
      const fallback='#7c3aed'; select.value='__NEW__';
      document.getElementById('rt-colour-picker').value=fallback; document.getElementById('rt-colour-new').value=fallback;
      this._syncRouteColourPreview(fallback);
    }
    this.onRouteColourChoice(true);
  },

  onRouteColourChoice(skipSync){
    const select=document.getElementById('rt-colour-choice');
    const wrap=document.getElementById('rt-colour-new-wrap');
    const isNew=select && select.value==='__NEW__';
    if(wrap) wrap.classList.toggle('open',!!isNew);
    if(!skipSync && select){
      if(isNew) this.onNewRouteColourInput(document.getElementById('rt-colour-new').value||document.getElementById('rt-colour-picker').value);
      else this._syncRouteColourPreview(select.value);
    }
  },

  onNewRouteColourInput(value){
    const raw=String(value||'').trim();
    if(/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(raw)){
      document.getElementById('rt-colour-picker').value=raw;
      document.getElementById('rt-colour-new').value=raw;
      this._syncRouteColourPreview(raw);
    }
  },

  onRouteIdentityInput(){
    if(document.getElementById('rt-type').value!=='EXTERNAL' || this._editingRoute) return;
    const route=document.getElementById('rt-route').value.trim();
    const code=document.getElementById('rt-code');
    const display=document.getElementById('rt-display');
    if(code && (!code.value || code.dataset.auto==='1')){ code.value=route; code.dataset.auto='1'; }
    if(display && (!display.value || display.dataset.auto==='1')){ display.value=route?'External Route '+route:''; display.dataset.auto='1'; }
  },

  onRouteTypeChange() {
    const external = document.getElementById('rt-type').value === 'EXTERNAL';
    const note = document.getElementById('rt-external-note');
    if (note) note.style.display = external ? '' : 'none';
    const std=document.getElementById('rt-standard-day-group'); if(std) std.style.display=external?'none':'';
    const ext=document.getElementById('rt-external-days-group'); if(ext) ext.style.display=external?'':'none';
    const lbl=document.getElementById('rt-route-label'); if(lbl) lbl.innerHTML=external?'Route ID <small>(shared across selected days)</small>':'Route No. <small>(unique)</small>';
    this.updateExternalRouteDayCount();
  },

  openRouteModal(rt, forcedType) {
    const routeType = rt ? (rt.routeType || 'STANDARD') : (forcedType || 'STANDARD');
    this._editingRoute=rt||null;
    const familyRows = rt && routeType==='EXTERNAL' ? (this.D.routes||[]).filter(x=>String(x.route)===String(rt.route) && String(x.routeType||'STANDARD').toUpperCase()==='EXTERNAL') : [];
    this._routeModalOriginalDays=familyRows.map(x=>x.day).filter(Boolean);
    document.getElementById('route-modal-title').textContent = rt ? (routeType==='EXTERNAL'?'Edit External Route':'Edit Route') : (routeType === 'EXTERNAL' ? 'New External Route' : 'New Route');
    const typeEl=document.getElementById('rt-type'); typeEl.value=routeType; typeEl.disabled=!!rt;
    document.getElementById('rt-active').value     = rt && rt.active === false ? 'no' : 'yes';
    document.getElementById('rt-route').value      = rt ? rt.route : (routeType === 'EXTERNAL' ? this._nextExternalRouteNumber() : '');
    document.getElementById('rt-route').readOnly   = !!rt;
    document.getElementById('rt-code').value       = rt ? rt.code : (routeType==='EXTERNAL'?document.getElementById('rt-route').value:'');
    document.getElementById('rt-display').value    = rt ? rt.displayName : (routeType==='EXTERNAL'?'External Route '+document.getElementById('rt-route').value:'');
    document.getElementById('rt-code').dataset.auto=rt?'0':'1';
    document.getElementById('rt-display').dataset.auto=rt?'0':'1';
    document.getElementById('rt-km').value         = rt ? rt.km : '';
    document.getElementById('rt-hours').value      = rt ? rt.hours : '';
    document.getElementById('rt-stops').value      = rt ? rt.stops : '';
    document.getElementById('rt-day').value        = rt ? rt.day : '';
    document.getElementById('rt-valid-from').value = rt ? (rt.validFrom||'') : '';
    document.getElementById('rt-valid-until').value= rt ? (rt.validUntil||'') : '';
    const selectedDays=routeType==='EXTERNAL' ? (familyRows.length?familyRows.map(x=>x.day):[]) : [];
    this._setExternalRouteDays(selectedDays);
    const addAfter=document.getElementById('rt-add-stops-after-wrap'); if(addAfter) addAfter.style.display=(!rt&&routeType==='EXTERNAL')?'flex':'none';
    const col = rt ? rt.colour : '';
    this._fillRouteColourOptions(col);
    this.onRouteTypeChange();
    this.openModal('route-modal');
  },

  saveRoute() {
    const routeType=document.getElementById('rt-type').value;
    const days=routeType==='EXTERNAL'?this._getExternalRouteDays():[];
    const r = {
      route:       document.getElementById('rt-route').value.trim(),
      code:        document.getElementById('rt-code').value.trim(),
      displayName: document.getElementById('rt-display').value.trim(),
      km:          parseFloat(document.getElementById('rt-km').value) || 0,
      hours:       parseFloat(document.getElementById('rt-hours').value) || 0,
      stops:       parseInt(document.getElementById('rt-stops').value) || 0,
      day:         document.getElementById('rt-day').value.trim(),
      days:        days,
      originalDays:this._routeModalOriginalDays||[],
      colour:      document.getElementById('rt-colour').value.trim() || '#94a3b8',
      routeType:   routeType,
      active:      document.getElementById('rt-active').value !== 'no',
      validFrom:   document.getElementById('rt-valid-from').value || '',
      validUntil:  document.getElementById('rt-valid-until').value || ''
    };
    if (!r.route) { this.toast('Route ID is required.', 'e'); return; }
    if (routeType==='EXTERNAL' && !days.length) { this.toast('Select at least one operating day.', 'e'); return; }
    if (!r.displayName) r.displayName = (r.routeType === 'EXTERNAL' ? 'External Route ' : 'Route ') + r.route;
    if (r.validFrom && r.validUntil && r.validFrom > r.validUntil) { this.toast('Valid From must be before Valid Until.', 'e'); return; }
    const wasNew=!this._editingRoute;
    const openStops=wasNew && routeType==='EXTERNAL' && document.getElementById('rt-add-stops-after').checked;
    const btn = document.getElementById('save-route-btn');
    this._runBtn(btn, days.length>1?'Creating '+days.length+' route days…':'Saving…', done => {
      const runner=google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(res => {
          done(res && res.ok);
          if (res && res.ok) {
            this.toast(routeType==='EXTERNAL' ? (days.length+' external route day'+(days.length===1?'':'s')+' saved.') : 'Route saved!', 's');
            this.closeModal('route-modal');
            google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(rts => {
              this.D.routes = rts; this.renderRoutes();
              if(openStops) this.openExternalDelivery({route:r.route,day:days[0],presetDays:days,routeType:'EXTERNAL',displayName:r.displayName,colour:r.colour});
            }).getRoutes();
          } else { this.toast('Error: ' + ((res && res.msg) || 'Could not save route'), 'e'); }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(e => { done(false); this.toast(e.message, 'e'); });
      if(routeType==='EXTERNAL') runner.saveExternalRouteDays(r); else runner.saveRoute(r);
    });
  },

  renderExternalRoutePanel() {
    // External routes now live directly in the main route planner.  Keep this
    // compatibility method so callbacks from older cached pages remain harmless.
    const host = document.getElementById('external-route-panel');
    if (host) { host.style.display='none'; host.innerHTML=''; }
  },

  toggleExternalRoute(route) {
    const next = Object.assign({}, route, { active: route.active === false });
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(res => {
      if (!res || !res.ok) { this.toast('Error: '+((res&&res.msg)||'Could not update route'),'e'); return; }
      this.toast(next.active ? 'External route reactivated.' : 'External route set inactive. Roster history and stops were preserved.','s');
      google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(rts => { this.D.routes=rts; this._renderPlanner(); }).getRoutes();
    }).withFailureHandler(e => this.toast(e.message,'e')).saveRoute(next);
  },

  confirmDeleteRoute(routeNum, displayName) {
    document.getElementById('confirm-msg').textContent =
      `Delete "${displayName || routeNum}" (Route ${routeNum})?`;
    document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn').onclick = () => {
      const okBtn = document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn');
      this._runBtn(okBtn, 'Deleting…', done => {
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(r => {
            done(r && r.ok);
            if (r && r.ok) {
              this.toast('Route deleted.', 'i'); this.closeModal('confirm-modal');
              google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(rts => { this.D.routes = rts; this.renderRoutes(); }).getRoutes();
            } else { this.toast('Error: ' + ((r && r.msg) || 'Could not delete route'), 'e'); }
          })
          .withFailureHandler(e => { done(false); this.toast(e.message, 'e'); })
          .deleteRoute(routeNum, displayName);
      });
    };
    this.openModal('confirm-modal');
  },

  // History — tab switching
  _histTab: 'rosters',

  switchHistTab(tab) {
    this._histTab = tab;
    document.getElementById('hist-rosters').style.display = tab==='rosters' ? '' : 'none';
    document.getElementById('hist-maint').style.display   = tab==='maint'   ? '' : 'none';
    document.getElementById('hist-infr').style.display    = tab==='infr'    ? '' : 'none';
    document.getElementById('add-maint-btn').style.display= tab==='maint'   ? '' : 'none';
    document.getElementById('add-infr-btn').style.display = tab==='infr'    ? '' : 'none';
    document.querySelectorAll('.hist-tab').forEach(b => {
      const isActive = b.id === 'tab-'+tab;
      b.style.background = isActive ? 'var(--blue)' : 'transparent';
      b.style.color = isActive ? '#fff' : 'var(--txt2)';
    });
    if (tab === 'maint') {
      this.loadMaintLogIfNeeded();
      this.renderMaintHistory();
    }
    if (tab === 'infr') {
      this.renderInfringements();
      this.ensureInfringementsLoaded(() => this.renderInfringements());
    }
  },

  renderHistory() {
    this._histTab = this._histTab || 'rosters';
    this.switchHistTab(this._histTab);
    const list = document.getElementById('hist-list');
    if (!this.D.weeks.length) {
      list.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state"><h3>No rosters saved yet</h3><p>Create and save a roster in the Weekly Roster section.</p></div>';
      return;
    }
    list.innerHTML = this.D.weeks.map(w => {
      const dates    = this.getWeekDates(w);
      const todayWk  = this.mondayOf(new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10));
      const today    = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
      // Actual hours editable for: past weeks, current week, and up to Friday after week close
      // i.e. show button if week ended AND today <= that week's Sunday + 4 days (Thursday)
      const weekSun  = this.getWeekDates(w)[6]; // Sunday of that week
      const deadline = new Date(weekSun + 'T12:00:00Z');
      deadline.setUTCDate(deadline.getUTCDate() + 4); // +4 days = Thursday after week ends
      const canEdit  = w < todayWk && today <= deadline.toISOString().slice(0,10);
      const isPast   = w < todayWk;
      const isFuture = w > todayWk;
      const futureChip = isFuture ? '<span class="chip" style="background:#eff6ff;color:#1d4ed8;margin-left:6px">future</span>' : '';
      const hoursBtn   = isPast
        ? '<button class="hist-hours-btn" id="hrs-btn-' + w + '" onclick="App.openActualHours(\'' + w + '\')"'
          + (canEdit ? '' : ' style="opacity:.5;cursor:default" title="Edit window closed (available until Thursday after week ends)"')
          + '>'
          + '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" style="width:12px;height:12px;stroke:currentColor;fill:none;stroke-width:2"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><polyline points="12 6 12 12 16 14"/></svg>'
          + (canEdit ? ' Actual Hours' : ' Actual Hours ✓')
          + '</button>'
        : '';
      const mileageBtn = !isFuture
        ? '<button class="hist-hours-btn" id="mileage-btn-' + w + '" onclick="App.openVehicleMileage(\'' + w + '\')" title="Record or review weekly vehicle mileage">🚚 Vehicle Mileage</button>'
        : '';
      return '<div class="hist-item">'
        + '<div>'
        + '<div class="hist-date">Week of ' + this.fmtFull(dates[0]) + futureChip + '</div>'
        + '<div class="hist-range">' + this.fmtFull(dates[0]) + ' \u2014 ' + this.fmtFull(dates[6]) + '</div>'
        + '</div>'
        + '<div style="display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap">'
        + hoursBtn
        + mileageBtn
        + '<button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onclick="App.nav(\'roster\');App.loadWeek(\'' + w + '\')">View</button>'
        + '<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" style="color:var(--red)" onclick="App.confirmDeleteWeek(\'' + w + '\')">Delete</button>'
        + '</div></div>';
    }).join('');
  },

  // Maintenance history

  loadMaintLogIfNeeded() {
    if (this.D.maintLog !== null || this._maintLoading) return;
    this._maintLoading = true;
    const tb = document.getElementById('maint-tbody');
    if (tb) tb.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8"><div class="empty-state"><p>Loading maintenance...</p></div></td></tr>';
    // Load BOTH sources: scheduled maintenance (MaintLog) + defects (all, open & closed)
    let pending = 2;
    const finish = () => { if (--pending <= 0) { this._maintLoading = false; if (this._histTab === 'maint') this.renderMaintHistory(); } };
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(log => { this.D.maintLog = log || []; finish(); })
      .withFailureHandler(e => { this.D.maintLog = []; finish(); })
      .getMaintLog();
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(res => { this.D.maintDefects = (res && res.rows) || []; finish(); })
      .withFailureHandler(e => { this.D.maintDefects = []; finish(); })
      .getVehicleDefects({});
  },

  // Build a single, unified list of fleet events: defects + scheduled maintenance.
  _unifiedMaintRows() {
    const rows = [];
    // a) Scheduled / logged maintenance from MaintLog
    (this.D.maintLog || []).forEach(e => {
      rows.push({
        kind: 'maintenance',
        plate: e.plate || '', type: e.type || '',
        status: e.status || 'maintenance',
        startDate: e.startDate || '', endDate: e.endDate || '',
        notes: e.notes || '',
        raw: e
      });
    });
    // b) Defects from VehicleDefects
    (this.D.maintDefects || []).forEach(d => {
      const isClosed = String(d.Status || '').toLowerCase() === 'closed';
      rows.push({
        kind: 'defect',
        plate: d.VehiclePlate || '', type: d.VehicleType || '',
        status: isClosed ? 'defect-closed' : 'defect-open',
        startDate: String(d.DateTime || d.CreatedAt || '').slice(0,10),
        endDate: isClosed ? String(d.ResolvedDate || d.ClosedDate || '').slice(0,10) : (String(d.ExpectedReturnDate || '').slice(0,10)),
        notes: [d.Severity, d.Category, d.Description].filter(Boolean).join(' · '),
        reportNumber: d.ReportNumber || '',
        roadworthy: d.VehicleStatus || '',
        raw: d
      });
    });
    // newest first by start date
    rows.sort((a,b) => String(b.startDate || '').localeCompare(String(a.startDate || '')));
    return rows;
  },

  renderMaintHistory() {
    const tb = document.getElementById('maint-tbody');
    if (this.D.maintLog === null && !this.D.maintDefects) {
      tb.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8"><div class="empty-state"><p>Loading...</p></div></td></tr>';
      return;
    }
    const all = this._unifiedMaintRows();
    this.populateMaintFilters(all);
    const plateFilter  = (document.getElementById('maint-filter-plate') || {}).value || '';
    const statusFilter = (document.getElementById('maint-filter-status') || {}).value || '';
    const list = all.filter(e => {
      if (plateFilter && String(e.plate || '') !== plateFilter) return false;
      if (statusFilter && String(e.status || '') !== statusFilter) return false;
      return true;
    });
    if (!all.length) {
      tb.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8"><div class="empty-state"><h3>No records yet</h3><p>Defects and scheduled maintenance appear here. Report one from <strong>Fleet → Report / Schedule</strong>.</p></div></td></tr>';
      return;
    }
    if (!list.length) {
      tb.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8"><div class="empty-state"><h3>No records match the filters</h3><p>Adjust Plate or Status.</p></div></td></tr>';
      return;
    }
    const stMap = { maintenance:'st-maint', scheduled:'st-sched', completed:'st-ok', 'defect-open':'st-defect-open', 'defect-closed':'st-defect-closed' };
    const stLabel = { maintenance:'🔧 Maintenance', scheduled:'📅 Scheduled', completed:'✅ Completed', 'defect-open':'🔴 Defect (open)', 'defect-closed':'✅ Defect (closed)' };
    tb.innerHTML = list.map(e => {
      const dur = this._maintDuration(e.startDate, e.endDate);
      let actions;
      if (e.kind === 'defect') {
        const rn = this.esc(e.reportNumber || '');
        const closeBtn = e.status === 'defect-open'
          ? `<button class="btn btn-success btn-sm" onclick="App.openCloseDefectModal('${rn}')">Close</button>` : '';
        actions = `<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="App.printDefectReport('${rn}')">Print</button>${closeBtn}`;
      } else {
        const safeE = JSON.stringify(e.raw).replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
        actions = `<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick='App.openMaintModal(${safeE})'>Edit</button>
          <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" style="color:var(--red)" onclick="App.confirmDeleteMaint('${e.raw.id}','${this.esc(e.plate)}')">Del</button>`;
      }
      const ref = e.kind === 'defect' && e.reportNumber ? `<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--txt3);font-family:monospace">${this.esc(e.reportNumber)}</div>` : '';
      return `<tr>
        <td><strong style="font-family:monospace;font-size:12px">${this.esc(e.plate)}</strong>${ref}</td>
        <td style="font-size:12px">${this.esc(e.type)}</td>
        <td><span class="st-badge ${stMap[e.status]||'st-ok'}">${stLabel[e.status]||e.status}</span></td>
        <td style="font-size:12px;white-space:nowrap">${e.startDate?this.fmtFull(e.startDate):'—'}</td>
        <td style="font-size:12px;white-space:nowrap">${e.endDate?this.fmtFull(e.endDate):'—'}</td>
        <td style="font-size:12px;color:var(--txt2)">${dur}</td>
        <td style="font-size:12px;color:var(--txt2);max-width:200px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap" title="${this.esc(e.notes)}">${this.esc(e.notes)}</td>
        <td style="white-space:nowrap">${actions}</td>
      </tr>`;
    }).join('');
  },

  populateMaintFilters(log) {
    const plateEl = document.getElementById('maint-filter-plate');
    if (!plateEl) return;
    const current = plateEl.value || '';
    const plates = Array.from(new Set((log || []).map(e => String(e.plate || '').trim()).filter(Boolean))).sort();
    plateEl.innerHTML = '<option value="">All plates</option>' + plates.map(p => `<option value="${this.esc(p)}">${this.esc(p)}</option>`).join('');
    if (plates.includes(current)) plateEl.value = current;
  },

  resetMaintFilters() {
    const p = document.getElementById('maint-filter-plate');
    const s = document.getElementById('maint-filter-status');
    if (p) p.value = '';
    if (s) s.value = '';
    this.renderMaintHistory();
  },

  _maintDuration(start, end) {
    if (!start && !end) return '—';
    if (!start || !end) return end ? 'Until '+this.fmtFull(end) : 'From '+this.fmtFull(start);
    const d = Math.round((new Date(end+' 12:00:00') - new Date(start+' 12:00:00')) / 86400000) + 1;
    return d > 0 ? d+' day'+(d!==1?'s':'') : '—';
  },

  openMaintModal(e) {
    document.getElementById('maint-modal-title').textContent = e ? 'Edit Record' : 'Log Maintenance';
    document.getElementById('ml-id').value     = e ? e.id : '';
    // Populate vehicle select
    var plateSel = document.getElementById('ml-plate');
    plateSel.innerHTML = (this.D.vehicles||[]).map(v =>
      '<option value="'+this.esc(v.plate)+'"'+(e&&e.plate===v.plate?' selected':'')+'>'+this.esc(v.plate)+' ('+this.esc(v.type)+')</option>'
    ).join('') || '<option value="">No vehicles registered</option>';
    document.getElementById('ml-status').value = e ? (e.status||'maintenance') : 'maintenance';
    document.getElementById('ml-start').value  = e ? (e.startDate||'') : new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
    document.getElementById('ml-end').value    = e ? (e.endDate||'') : '';
    document.getElementById('ml-notes').value  = e ? (e.notes||'') : '';
    this.openModal('maint-modal');
    setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('ml-notes').focus(), 100);
  },

  saveMaintEntry() {
    const idVal = document.getElementById('ml-id').value;
    const entry = {
      id:        idVal,
      plate:     document.getElementById('ml-plate').value,
      type:      ((this.D.vehicles||[]).find(v=>v.plate===document.getElementById('ml-plate').value)||{}).type||'',
      status:    document.getElementById('ml-status').value,
      startDate: document.getElementById('ml-start').value||'',
      endDate:   document.getElementById('ml-end').value||'',
      notes:     document.getElementById('ml-notes').value.trim()
    };
    if (!entry.plate) { this.toast('Select a vehicle.','e'); return; }
    const fn = idVal ? 'updateMaintLogEntry' : 'addMaintLogEntry';
    this._runBtn(document.getElementById('save-maint-btn'), 'Saving...', done => {
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(r => {
          done(r.ok);
          if (r.ok) {
            this.toast('Record saved!','s');
            this.closeModal('maint-modal');
            google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(log => { this.D.maintLog=log; this.renderMaintHistory(); }).getMaintLog();
          } else { this.toast('Error: '+r.msg,'e'); }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(e => { done(false); this.toast(e.message,'e'); })
        [fn](entry);
    });
  },

  confirmDeleteMaint(id, plate) {
    document.getElementById('confirm-msg').textContent = 'Delete maintenance record for "'+plate+'"?';
    document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn').textContent = 'Delete';
    document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn').className = 'btn btn-danger';
    document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn').onclick = () => {
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(r => {
          if (r.ok) {
            this.toast('Record deleted.','i');
            this.closeModal('confirm-modal');
            google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(log => { this.D.maintLog=log; this.renderMaintHistory(); }).getMaintLog();
          } else { this.toast('Error: '+r.msg,'e'); }
        }).deleteMaintLogEntry(id);
    };
    this.openModal('confirm-modal');
  },

  // Actual Hours
  _actualHoursWeek: null,

  openActualHours(weekStart) {
    this._actualHoursWeek = weekStart;
    const dates = this.getWeekDates(weekStart);
    document.getElementById('actual-hours-title').textContent =
      'Actual Hours — Week of ' + this.fmtFull(dates[0]);

    // Load roster for this week to get estimated hours and current actuals
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(r => {
        const estimated  = {}; // driverId → estimated hours
        const actualSaved = r.actualHours || {};
        const STATUS_V   = ['Off','Hols','Training','Absent','Sick'];

        // Calculate estimated hours per driver
        const allDrivers = this._rosterDrivers || this.D.drivers;
        allDrivers.forEach(drv => {
          let hrs = 0;
          const a = (r.assignments||[]).find(x => String(x.driverId) === String(drv.id));
          if (a) {
            this.DAYS.forEach(day => {
              const val = a[day]||'';
              const rt  = this.findRoute(val);
              if (rt) hrs += rt.hours;
              else if (val === 'Training') hrs += 8;
            });
          }
          estimated[drv.id] = hrs;
        });

        // Render the hours input table
        let h = '<div class="hours-row" style="padding-bottom:6px;margin-bottom:4px">';
        h += '<div class="hrs-col-hdr" style="text-align:left">Driver</div>';
        h += '<div class="hrs-col-hdr">Estimated</div>';
        h += '<div class="hrs-col-hdr">Actual</div>';
        h += '<div class="hrs-col-hdr">Diff</div>';
        h += '</div>';

        allDrivers.forEach(drv => {
          const est    = estimated[drv.id] || 0;
          const actual = actualSaved[String(drv.id)] || '';
          const diff   = actual !== '' ? (parseFloat(actual) - est).toFixed(1) : '';
          const diffCl = diff === '' ? '' : (parseFloat(diff) > 0 ? 'diff-pos' : parseFloat(diff) < 0 ? 'diff-neg' : 'diff-zero');
          const diffTxt = diff === '' ? '—' : (parseFloat(diff) > 0 ? '+'+diff+'h' : diff+'h');

          h += '<div class="hours-row" id="hrs-row-'+this.esc(drv.id)+'">';
          h += '<div style="font-weight:600;font-size:13px">' + this.esc(drv.name) + (drv._historical?'<span style="font-size:10px;color:#94a3b8;margin-left:4px">(inactive)</span>':'') + '</div>';
          h += '<div style="text-align:center;font-size:13px;color:var(--txt2)">' + (est?est+'h':'—') + '</div>';
          h += '<div><input type="number" min="0" max="80" step="0.5" placeholder="—" value="' + actual + '" data-driver-id="' + this.esc(drv.id) + '" data-est="' + est + '" oninput="App.updateHoursDiff(this)" style="width:100%;padding:6px 8px;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:6px;font-size:13px;text-align:center;font-family:inherit;background:var(--card);color:var(--text)"></div>';
          h += '<div class="' + diffCl + '" style="text-align:center;font-size:13px" id="hrs-diff-'+this.esc(drv.id)+'">' + diffTxt + '</div>';
          h += '</div>';
        });

        document.getElementById('actual-hours-list').innerHTML = h;
        this.openModal('actual-hours-modal');
      })
      .withFailureHandler(e => this.toast(e.message, 'e'))
      .getRosterData(weekStart);
  },

  updateHoursDiff(input) {
    const drvId = input.dataset.driverId;
    const est   = parseFloat(input.dataset.est) || 0;
    const actual = parseFloat(input.value);
    const diffEl = document.getElementById('hrs-diff-' + drvId);
    if (!diffEl) return;
    if (isNaN(actual) || input.value === '') {
      diffEl.textContent = '—'; diffEl.className = 'diff-zero'; return;
    }
    const diff = (actual - est).toFixed(1);
    diffEl.textContent = parseFloat(diff) > 0 ? '+'+diff+'h' : diff+'h';
    diffEl.className = parseFloat(diff) > 0 ? 'diff-pos' : parseFloat(diff) < 0 ? 'diff-neg' : 'diff-zero';
  },

  saveActualHours() {
    const weekStart = this._actualHoursWeek;
    if (!weekStart) return;
    const inputs  = document.querySelectorAll('#actual-hours-list input[data-driver-id]');
    const payload = {};
    inputs.forEach(inp => {
      const val = inp.value.trim();
      if (val !== '' && !isNaN(parseFloat(val))) {
        payload[inp.dataset.driverId] = parseFloat(val);
      }
    });
    const btn = document.getElementById('save-actual-hours-btn');
    this._runBtn(btn, 'Saving...', done => {
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(r => {
          done(r.ok);
          if (r.ok) {
            this.toast('Actual hours saved!', 's');
            this.closeModal('actual-hours-modal');
            // Update the button style in history to show data is present
            const hrsBtn = document.getElementById('hrs-btn-' + weekStart);
            if (hrsBtn) hrsBtn.className = 'hist-hours-btn has-data';
          } else { this.toast('Error: ' + r.msg, 'e'); }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(e => { done(false); this.toast(e.message, 'e'); })
        .saveActualHours(weekStart, payload);
    });
  },

  // Vehicle Mileage — one auditable record per vehicle and roster week
  _vehicleMileageWeek: null,

  openVehicleMileage(weekStart) {
    this._vehicleMileageWeek = weekStart;
    const dates = this.getWeekDates(weekStart);
    document.getElementById('vehicle-mileage-title').textContent =
      'Vehicle Mileage — Week of ' + this.fmtFull(dates[0]);
    document.getElementById('vehicle-mileage-list').innerHTML = '<div class="loading-wrap"><div class="spinner"></div>Loading mileage...</div>';
    this.openModal('vehicle-mileage-modal');

    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(res => {
        if (!res || res.ok === false) { this.toast((res && res.msg) || 'Could not load vehicle mileage.', 'e'); return; }
        const roster = res.roster || { assignments:[] };
        const savedRows = res.rows || [];
        const planned = {};
        (roster.assignments || []).forEach(a => {
          this.DAYS.forEach(day => {
            const parsed = this.parseCell(a[day] || '');
            if (!parsed.vehicle || !parsed.route || ['Off','Hols','Training','Absent','Sick'].includes(parsed.route)) return;
            const key = String(parsed.vehicle).replace(/\s+/g,'').toUpperCase();
            const rt = this.findRoute(a[day] || '');
            planned[key] = (planned[key] || 0) + (rt ? (parseFloat(rt.km)||0) : 0);
          });
        });
        const saved = {};
        savedRows.forEach(r => { saved[String(r.plate||r.Plate||'').replace(/\s+/g,'').toUpperCase()] = r; });
        const map = {};
        (this.D.vehicles || []).forEach(v => {
          const key = String(v.plate||'').replace(/\s+/g,'').toUpperCase();
          if (!key) return;
          if (String(v.status||'').toLowerCase() !== 'returned') map[key] = { plate:v.plate, type:v.type, status:v.status||'available' };
        });
        Object.keys(planned).forEach(key => {
          if (!map[key]) {
            const v=(this.D.vehicles||[]).find(x=>String(x.plate||'').replace(/\s+/g,'').toUpperCase()===key);
            map[key]={plate:v?v.plate:key,type:v?v.type:'',status:v?v.status:'historical'};
          }
        });
        Object.keys(saved).forEach(key => {
          if (!map[key]) map[key]={plate:saved[key].plate||saved[key].Plate||key,type:saved[key].vehicleType||saved[key].VehicleType||'',status:'returned'};
        });
        const vehicles = Object.values(map).sort((a,b) => {
          const ak=String(a.plate||'').replace(/\s+/g,'').toUpperCase(), bk=String(b.plate||'').replace(/\s+/g,'').toUpperCase();
          const ap=planned[ak]||0, bp=planned[bk]||0;
          return (bp>0)-(ap>0) || String(a.plate).localeCompare(String(b.plate));
        });
        if (!vehicles.length) {
          document.getElementById('vehicle-mileage-list').innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state"><h3>No active vehicles</h3><p>Returned vehicles are excluded from new weekly mileage entries.</p></div>';
          return;
        }
        let h='<div class="mileage-row" style="padding-bottom:6px;margin-bottom:4px">'
          +'<div class="hrs-col-hdr" style="text-align:left">Vehicle</div><div class="hrs-col-hdr">Type</div>'
          +'<div class="hrs-col-hdr">Planned</div><div class="hrs-col-hdr">Actual KM</div><div class="hrs-col-hdr">Variance</div></div>';
        vehicles.forEach(v => {
          const key=String(v.plate||'').replace(/\s+/g,'').toUpperCase();
          const rec=saved[key]||{};
          const plan=Math.round((planned[key]||0)*10)/10;
          const raw=rec.actualKm!==undefined?rec.actualKm:rec.ActualKm;
          const actual=(raw!==undefined&&raw!==null&&raw!=='')?Number(raw):'';
          const returned=String(v.status||'').toLowerCase()==='returned';
          const diff=actual!==''?(actual-plan):null;
          const diffTxt=diff===null?'—':((diff>0?'+':'')+diff.toFixed(1));
          const diffCl=diff===null?'':(diff>0?'diff-pos':diff<0?'diff-neg':'diff-zero');
          h+='<div class="mileage-row">'
            +'<div style="font-weight:700;font-size:13px">'+this.esc(v.plate)+(returned?'<span style="font-size:9px;color:#64748b;background:#e2e8f0;border-radius:999px;padding:2px 6px;margin-left:5px">history only</span>':'')+'</div>'
            +'<div style="text-align:center;font-size:12px;color:var(--txt2)">'+this.esc(v.type||'—')+'</div>'
            +'<div style="text-align:center;font-size:13px;color:var(--txt2)">'+(plan?plan.toFixed(1):'—')+'</div>'
            +'<div><input type="number" min="0" step="1" placeholder="—" value="'+(actual===''?'':actual)+'" data-mileage-plate="'+this.esc(v.plate)+'" data-type="'+this.esc(v.type||'')+'" data-planned="'+plan+'" oninput="App.updateMileageDiff(this)" '+(returned?'disabled title="Returned vehicle — historical record only"':'')+'></div>'
            +'<div class="'+diffCl+'" style="text-align:center;font-size:13px" id="mileage-diff-'+this.esc(key)+'">'+diffTxt+'</div>'
            +'</div>';
        });
        document.getElementById('vehicle-mileage-list').innerHTML=h;
      })
      .withFailureHandler(e => { this.toast(e.message,'e'); document.getElementById('vehicle-mileage-list').innerHTML='<div class="empty-state"><h3>Load error</h3><p>'+this.esc(e.message)+'</p></div>'; })
      .getVehicleMileageWeekData(weekStart);
  },

  updateMileageDiff(input) {
    const key=String(input.dataset.mileagePlate||'').replace(/\s+/g,'').toUpperCase();
    const plan=parseFloat(input.dataset.planned)||0;
    const el=document.getElementById('mileage-diff-'+key);
    if (!el) return;
    if (input.value==='') { el.textContent='—'; el.className=''; return; }
    const actual=parseFloat(input.value);
    if (isNaN(actual)) { el.textContent='—'; el.className=''; return; }
    const diff=actual-plan;
    el.textContent=(diff>0?'+':'')+diff.toFixed(1);
    el.className=diff>0?'diff-pos':diff<0?'diff-neg':'diff-zero';
  },

  saveVehicleMileage() {
    const weekStart=this._vehicleMileageWeek;
    if (!weekStart) return;
    const entries=[];
    document.querySelectorAll('#vehicle-mileage-list input[data-mileage-plate]:not(:disabled)').forEach(inp => {
      const val=inp.value.trim();
      if (val==='' || isNaN(parseFloat(val))) return;
      entries.push({
        plate:inp.dataset.mileagePlate,
        vehicleType:inp.dataset.type||'',
        plannedKm:parseFloat(inp.dataset.planned)||0,
        actualKm:parseFloat(val)
      });
    });
    if (!entries.length) { this.toast('Enter mileage for at least one vehicle.','e'); return; }
    const btn=document.getElementById('save-vehicle-mileage-btn');
    this._runBtn(btn,'Saving...',done => {
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(r => {
          done(r && r.ok);
          if (r && r.ok) {
            this.toast('Vehicle mileage saved for '+(r.saved||entries.length)+' vehicle'+((r.saved||entries.length)!==1?'s':'')+'.','s');
            this.closeModal('vehicle-mileage-modal');
            const b=document.getElementById('mileage-btn-'+weekStart); if (b) b.classList.add('has-data');
          } else this.toast('Error: '+((r&&r.msg)||'Could not save mileage'),'e');
        })
        .withFailureHandler(e => { done(false); this.toast(e.message,'e'); })
        .saveVehicleMileage(weekStart,entries);
    });
  },

  confirmDeleteWeek(w) {
    const dates = this.getWeekDates(w);
    document.getElementById('confirm-msg').textContent =
      `Delete the roster for week of ${this.fmtFull(dates[0])}?`;
    document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn').onclick = () => {
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(r => {
          if (r.ok) {
            this.toast('Week deleted.', 'i');
            this.D.weeks = this.D.weeks.filter(x => x !== w);
            this.closeModal('confirm-modal');
            this.renderHistory();
          } else { this.toast('Error: ' + r.msg, 'e'); }
        }).deleteWeek(w);
    };
    this.openModal('confirm-modal');
  },

  // Vehicles
  _vehFilter: 'all',

  filterVehicles(btn) {
    this._vehFilter = btn.dataset.f;
    document.querySelectorAll('.veh-filter').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
    btn.classList.add('active');
    this.renderVehicles();
  },

  // Strictly between start and end — start/end dates are borderline (warning only)
  vehIsUnavailable(v) {
    const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
    if (v.status === 'returned') return true;
    if (v.status === 'maintenance' || v.status === 'scheduled') {
      const afterStart = v.maintStart ? today > v.maintStart : true;   // strictly AFTER start
