      const beforeEnd  = v.maintEnd   ? today < v.maintEnd   : true;   // strictly BEFORE end
      return afterStart && beforeEnd;
    }
    return false;
  },

  // True if today is the start or end date — vehicle is available but with a caution note
  vehIsBorderline(v) {
    const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
    if (v.status !== 'maintenance' && v.status !== 'scheduled') return false;
    return (v.maintStart && today === v.maintStart) || (v.maintEnd && today === v.maintEnd);
  },

  vehBorderlineNote(v) {
    const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
    if (v.maintStart && today === v.maintStart) return 'Enters maintenance today';
    if (v.maintEnd   && today === v.maintEnd)   return 'Returns from maintenance today';
    return '';
  },

  vehStatusBadge(v) {
    const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
    const range = (v.maintStart || v.maintEnd)
      ? ` ${v.maintStart ? this.fmtFull(v.maintStart) : '?'} → ${v.maintEnd ? this.fmtFull(v.maintEnd) : '?'}`
      : '';

    if (v.status === 'returned') {
      return '<span class="st-badge" style="background:#e2e8f0;color:#475569;border:1px solid #cbd5e1">↩️ Returned / Inactive</span>';
    }

    if (v.status === 'maintenance' || v.status === 'scheduled') {
      if (v.maintEnd && today > v.maintEnd)
        return '<span class="st-badge st-ok">✅ Back in service</span>';
      if (v.maintStart && today === v.maintStart)
        return `<span class="st-badge" style="background:#fff7ed;color:#c2410c;border:1px solid #fed7aa">⚠️ Entering maint.${range}</span>`;
      if (v.maintEnd && today === v.maintEnd)
        return `<span class="st-badge" style="background:#fff7ed;color:#c2410c;border:1px solid #fed7aa">⚠️ Last day maint.${range}</span>`;
      if (this.vehIsUnavailable(v))
        return `<span class="st-badge ${v.status==='scheduled'?'st-sched':'st-maint'}">${v.status==='scheduled'?'📅 Scheduled':'🔧 Maintenance'}${range}</span>`;
      // scheduled but not yet started
      return `<span class="st-badge st-sched">📅 Scheduled${range}</span>`;
    }
    return '<span class="st-badge st-ok">✅ Available</span>';
  },


  vehicleCoreFields: [
    ['contractType','vh-contract-type'], ['vehicleDescription','vh-vehicle-description'],
    ['tachographType','vh-tachograph-type'], ['insurer','vh-insurer'], ['make','vh-make'], ['range','vh-range'],
    ['fuel','vh-fuel'], ['lastKmRecorded','vh-last-km-recorded'],
    ['contractStartDate','vh-contract-start-date'], ['contractEndDate','vh-contract-end-date'],
    ['registrationDate','vh-registration-date'], ['bodyDetail','vh-body-detail'],
    ['tailLiftDetail1','vh-tail-lift-detail1'], ['tailLiftDetail2','vh-tail-lift-detail2'],
    ['monthlyRentalExVat','vh-monthly-rental-ex-vat'], ['termMonths','vh-term-months']
  ],

  vehicleRequiredFutureFields: [
    ['Tacho','tachoCalibrationDate','vh-tacho-calibration-date'],
    ['CVRT/MOT','cvrtMotExpiryDate','vh-cvrt-mot-expiry-date'],
    ['Service','serviceNext','vh-service-next'],
    ['Road fund licence','roadFundLicenceNext','vh-road-fund-licence-next'],
    ['TL Weight Test','tlWeightTestNext','vh-tl-weight-test-next'],
    ['TL LOLER','tlLolerNext','vh-tl-loler-next']
  ],

  vehicleHistoryInputFields: [
    ['cvrtLast','vh-cvrt-last'], ['serviceLast','vh-service-last'], ['tachographLast','vh-tachograph-last'],
    ['tlWeightTestLast','vh-tl-weight-test-last'], ['tlLolerLast','vh-tl-loler-last']
  ],

  vehicleDueFields: [
    ['Service','serviceNext'], ['CVRT/MOT','cvrtMotExpiryDate'], ['Tacho','tachoCalibrationDate'],
    ['Road fund licence','roadFundLicenceNext'], ['TL Weight Test','tlWeightTestNext'], ['TL LOLER','tlLolerNext']
  ],

  setVehicleExtraFields(v) {
    const all = [].concat(this.vehicleCoreFields||[], this.vehicleRequiredFutureFields.map(x => [x[1], x[2]]), this.vehicleHistoryInputFields||[]);
    all.forEach(([key, id]) => {
      const el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (el) el.value = v ? (v[key] || '') : '';
    });
    const hb = document.getElementById('vh-history-btn');
    if (hb) hb.style.display = v && v.plate ? '' : 'none';
    this._currentVehiclePlate = v && v.plate ? v.plate : '';
    this.refreshVehicleDateRequirements();
  },

  readVehicleExtraFields(v) {
    const all = [].concat(this.vehicleCoreFields||[], this.vehicleRequiredFutureFields.map(x => [x[1], x[2]]), this.vehicleHistoryInputFields||[]);
    all.forEach(([key, id]) => {
      const el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (el) v[key] = el.value.trim();
    });
    return v;
  },

  vehicleDateIssues(v) {
    const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
    const missing = [], expired = [];
    const isVan = String(v.type || '').toUpperCase() === 'VAN';
    (this.vehicleRequiredFutureFields||[]).forEach(([label,key]) => {
      if (isVan && key === 'tachoCalibrationDate') return;
      const ds = v[key] || '';
      if (!ds) missing.push(label);
      else if (ds < today) expired.push(label + ' (' + this.fmtFull(ds) + ')');
    });
    return { missing, expired };
  },

  refreshVehicleDateRequirements() {
    const box = document.getElementById('vh-required-warning');
    if (!box) return true;
    const statusEl = document.getElementById('vh-status');
    if (statusEl && statusEl.value === 'returned') {
      document.querySelectorAll('.vh-required-date').forEach(el => { el.style.borderColor = ''; });
      box.style.display = 'none';
      box.innerHTML = '';
      return true;
    }
    const v = { type: (document.getElementById('vh-type')||{}).value || '' };
    const isVan = String(v.type).toUpperCase() === 'VAN';
    (this.vehicleRequiredFutureFields||[]).forEach(([label,key,id]) => {
      const el = document.getElementById(id);
      v[key] = el ? el.value : '';
      if (el) {
        const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
        const optional = isVan && key === 'tachoCalibrationDate';
        el.style.borderColor = (!optional && (!v[key] || v[key] < today)) ? '#ef4444' : '';
        el.disabled = false;
        el.title = optional ? 'Optional for VAN vehicles' : '';
      }
    });
    const issues = this.vehicleDateIssues(v);
    if (!issues.missing.length && !issues.expired.length) { box.style.display='none'; box.innerHTML=''; return true; }
    const parts = [];
    if (issues.missing.length) parts.push('<strong>Missing:</strong> ' + issues.missing.map(this.esc).join(', '));
    if (issues.expired.length) parts.push('<strong>Expired:</strong> ' + issues.expired.map(this.esc).join(', '));
    box.innerHTML = parts.join('<br>') + '<br>These fields must have valid current/future dates before saving.';
    box.style.display = '';
    return false;
  },

  getVehicleDueAlerts() {
    const today = new Date();
    today.setHours(0,0,0,0);
    const alerts = [];
    (this.D.vehicles||[]).forEach(v => {
      if (String(v.status || '').toLowerCase() === 'returned') return;
      (this.vehicleDueFields||[]).forEach(([label, key]) => {
        if (String(v.type||'').toUpperCase()==='VAN' && key==='tachoCalibrationDate') return;
        const ds = v[key];
        if (!ds) { alerts.push({ plate:v.plate, type:v.type, label, date:'', days:-9999, missing:true }); return; }
        const d = new Date(ds + 'T12:00:00Z');
        if (isNaN(d)) return;
        const diff = Math.ceil((d - today) / 86400000);
        if (diff < 0 || diff <= 30) alerts.push({ plate:v.plate, type:v.type, label, date:ds, days:diff });
      });
    });
    return alerts.sort((a,b) => a.days - b.days);
  },

  getVehicleAlertsForPlate(plate, onlyExpired) {
    const alerts = this.getVehicleDueAlerts().filter(a => String(a.plate) === String(plate));
    return onlyExpired ? alerts.filter(a => a.missing || a.days < 0) : alerts;
  },

  vehicleAlertBadges(plate) {
    const alerts = this.getVehicleAlertsForPlate(plate, false);
    if (!alerts.length) return '<span style="color:var(--txt2);font-size:12px">—</span>';
    const first = alerts.slice(0, 3).map(a => {
      const bad = a.missing || a.days < 0;
      const title = a.missing ? 'Missing required future date' : (bad ? ('Expired on ' + this.fmtFull(a.date)) : ('Due ' + this.fmtFull(a.date)));
      return `<span class="vehicle-mini-alert ${bad?'expired':''}" title="${this.esc(title)}">${bad?'🔴':'🟠'} ${this.esc(a.label)}</span>`;
    }).join('');
    return `<div class="vehicle-mini-alerts">${first}${alerts.length>3?`<span class="vehicle-mini-alert more">+${alerts.length-3}</span>`:''}</div>`;
  },

  toggleFleetComplianceAlerts(event) {
    if (event) {
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
    }
    this._fleetAlertsCollapsed = !this._fleetAlertsCollapsed;
    this.renderFleetComplianceAlerts();
  },

  renderFleetComplianceAlerts() {
    const box = document.getElementById('fleet-compliance-alerts');
    if (!box) return;
    const alerts = this.getVehicleDueAlerts();
    const expired = alerts.filter(a => a.missing || a.days < 0);
    const soon = alerts.filter(a => !a.missing && a.days >= 0 && a.days <= 30);
    const vehiclesTotal = (this.D.vehicles || []).length;
    const platesWithAlerts = new Set(alerts.map(a => a.plate));
    const okCount = Math.max(0, vehiclesTotal - platesWithAlerts.size);
    if (!expired.length && !soon.length) { box.style.display = 'none'; box.innerHTML = ''; return; }
    const top = expired.concat(soon).slice(0, 8);
    const collapsed = !!this._fleetAlertsCollapsed;
    box.style.display = '';
    box.innerHTML = `<div class="fleet-alert-card ${collapsed?'collapsed':''}">
      <div class="fleet-alert-head" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-expanded="${collapsed?'false':'true'}"
           title="${collapsed?'Show pending details':'Collapse pending details'}"
           onclick="App.toggleFleetComplianceAlerts(event)"
           onkeydown="if(event.target===this&&(event.key==='Enter'||event.key===' ')){App.toggleFleetComplianceAlerts(event)}">
        <div class="fleet-alert-title">🚨 Fleet compliance alerts</div>
        <div class="fleet-alert-summary">
          <div class="fleet-alert-counts">
            <span class="fleet-pill expired">${expired.length} expired/missing</span>
            <span class="fleet-pill soon">${soon.length} due in 30 days</span>
            <span class="fleet-pill ok">${okCount} OK</span>
          </div>
          <button type="button" class="fleet-alert-toggle" onclick="App.toggleFleetComplianceAlerts(event)">${collapsed?'▼ Show details':'▲ Collapse'}</button>
          <button type="button" class="fleet-alert-open" onclick="event.stopPropagation();App.nav('vehicles')">Vehicles</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="fleet-alert-body">
        ${top.map(a => {
          const bad = a.missing || a.days < 0;
          const when = a.missing ? 'required date missing'
            : (bad ? `${Math.abs(a.days)} day${Math.abs(a.days)!==1?'s':''} overdue`
                   : (a.days === 0 ? 'due today' : `due in ${a.days} day${a.days!==1?'s':''}`));
          return `<div class="fleet-alert-row ${bad?'expired':''}">
            <div><div class="fleet-alert-main">${bad?'🔴':'🟠'} ${this.esc(a.plate)} — ${this.esc(a.label)}</div>
            <div class="fleet-alert-sub">${a.missing?'No future date set':this.fmtFull(a.date)} · ${when}</div></div>
            <div class="fleet-alert-actions"><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="event.stopPropagation();App.openVehicleFromPlate('${this.esc(a.plate)}')">Edit</button></div>
          </div>`;
        }).join('')}
        ${alerts.length > top.length ? `<div class="fleet-alert-row"><div class="fleet-alert-main">+ ${alerts.length - top.length} more vehicle date alert(s)</div><div class="fleet-alert-actions"><button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onclick="event.stopPropagation();App.nav('vehicles')">View all</button></div></div>` : ''}
      </div>
    </div>`;
  },

  openVehicleFromPlate(plate) {
    const v = (this.D.vehicles || []).find(x => String(x.plate) === String(plate));
    if (!v) { this.nav('vehicles'); return; }
    this.openVehicleModal(v);
  },

  renderVehicleAlerts() {
    this.renderFleetComplianceAlerts();
    const box = document.getElementById('vehicle-alerts');
    if (!box) return;
    const alerts = this.getVehicleDueAlerts();
    if (!alerts.length) { box.style.display = 'none'; box.innerHTML = ''; return; }
    box.style.display = '';
    box.innerHTML = alerts.slice(0, 12).map(a => {
      const overdue = a.missing || a.days < 0;
      const when = a.missing ? 'missing date'
        : (overdue ? (Math.abs(a.days) + ' day' + (Math.abs(a.days)!==1?'s':'') + ' overdue')
                   : (a.days === 0 ? 'due today' : 'due in ' + a.days + ' day' + (a.days!==1?'s':'')));
      const dateTxt = a.missing ? 'not set' : this.fmtFull(a.date);
      return `<div class="vehicle-alert ${overdue?'overdue':''}"><strong>${this.esc(a.plate)}</strong> ${this.esc(a.type||'')} — ${this.esc(a.label)}: <strong>${dateTxt}</strong> (${when})</div>`;
    }).join('') + (alerts.length > 12 ? `<div class="vehicle-alert">+ ${alerts.length - 12} more upcoming/overdue vehicle date(s)</div>` : '');
  },

  onVehStatusChange() {
    const st = document.getElementById('vh-status').value;
    document.getElementById('vh-date-group').style.display =
      (st === 'maintenance' || st === 'scheduled') ? '' : 'none';
    const preserveNote = document.getElementById('vh-returned-preserve-note');
    if (preserveNote) preserveNote.style.display = st === 'returned' ? '' : 'none';
    this.onVehDateChange();
    this.refreshVehicleDateRequirements();
  },

  onVehDateChange() {
    const s = document.getElementById('vh-maint-start').value;
    const e = document.getElementById('vh-maint-end').value;
    const preview = document.getElementById('vh-date-preview');
    if (s || e) {
      const days = (s && e) ? Math.ceil((new Date(e) - new Date(s)) / 86400000) + 1 : null;
      var daysStr = days ? ' (' + days + ' day' + (days!==1?'s':'') + ')' : '';
      preview.textContent = 'Out of service: ' + (s ? this.fmtFull(s) : '?') + ' \u2192 ' + (e ? this.fmtFull(e) : '?') + daysStr;
      preview.style.display = '';
    } else {
      preview.style.display = 'none';
    }
  },

  openVehicleModal(v) {
    document.getElementById('vehicle-modal-title').textContent = v ? 'Edit Vehicle' : 'New Vehicle';
    document.getElementById('vh-orig-plate').value   = v ? v.plate : '';
    document.getElementById('vh-plate').value        = v ? v.plate : '';
    document.getElementById('vh-plate').readOnly     = !!v;
    document.getElementById('vh-type').value         = v && ['TRUCK','VAN'].includes(String(v.type||'').toUpperCase()) ? String(v.type).toUpperCase() : 'TRUCK';
    document.getElementById('vh-status').value       = v ? (v.status||'available') : 'available';
    document.getElementById('vh-maint-start').value  = v ? (v.maintStart||'') : '';
    document.getElementById('vh-maint-end').value    = v ? (v.maintEnd||'') : '';
    document.getElementById('vh-notes').value        = v ? v.notes : '';
    this.setVehicleExtraFields(v);
    this.onVehStatusChange();
    this.openModal('vehicle-modal');
    setTimeout(() => document.getElementById(v ? 'vh-type' : 'vh-plate').focus(), 100);
  },

  openVehicleHistoryModal(plate) {
    const p = plate || this._currentVehiclePlate || document.getElementById('vh-plate').value;
    if (!p) { this.toast('Select/save a vehicle first.', 'e'); return; }
    document.getElementById('vehicle-history-title').textContent = 'History — ' + p;
    const body = document.getElementById('vehicle-history-body');
    body.innerHTML = '<div class="loading-wrap"><div class="spinner"></div>Loading history…</div>';
    this.openModal('vehicle-history-modal');
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(res => {
      if (!res || !res.ok) { body.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state"><h3>Could not load history</h3><p>'+this.esc((res&&res.msg)||'')+'</p></div>'; return; }
      const rows = res.rows || [];
      if (!rows.length) { body.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state"><h3>No history yet</h3><p>Every monitored date entry, renewal, correction or clearing will appear here after saving.</p></div>'; return; }
      body.innerHTML = '<div style="overflow-x:auto"><table class="data-table" style="min-width:760px"><thead><tr><th>Date</th><th>Item</th><th>Change</th><th>Old</th><th>New</th><th>Status</th><th>Source</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + rows.map(r => {
        const change = String(r.ChangeType || (r.OldDate ? 'UPDATED' : 'INITIAL_SET')).replace(/_/g,' ');
        return `<tr><td style="white-space:nowrap;font-size:12px">${this.esc(String(r.ChangedAt||'').slice(0,19))}</td><td>${this.esc(r.FieldLabel||r.FieldKey||'')}</td><td style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700">${this.esc(change)}</td><td>${r.OldDate?this.fmtFull(r.OldDate):'—'}</td><td>${r.NewDate?this.fmtFull(r.NewDate):'—'}</td><td style="font-size:12px">${this.esc(r.VehicleStatus||'—')}</td><td style="font-size:12px;color:var(--txt2)">${this.esc(r.Source||'')}</td></tr>`;
      }).join('') + '</tbody></table></div>';
    }).withFailureHandler(e => { body.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state"><h3>Error</h3><p>'+this.esc(e.message)+'</p></div>'; }).getVehicleHistory(p);
  },

  saveVehicle() {
    const st = document.getElementById('vh-status').value;
    const v = {
      isNew:      !document.getElementById('vh-orig-plate').value,
      origPlate:  document.getElementById('vh-orig-plate').value,
      plate:      document.getElementById('vh-plate').value.trim(),
      type:       document.getElementById('vh-type').value.trim(),
      status:     st,
      maintStart: document.getElementById('vh-maint-start').value || '',
      maintEnd:   document.getElementById('vh-maint-end').value   || '',
      notes:      document.getElementById('vh-notes').value.trim()
    };
    this.readVehicleExtraFields(v);
    v.type = String(v.type || '').toUpperCase();
    v.contractType = String(v.contractType || '').trim();
    if (!v.plate) { this.toast('Plate is required.', 'e'); return; }
    if (!['TRUCK','VAN'].includes(v.type)) { this.toast('Type must be VAN or TRUCK.', 'e'); return; }
    if (!v.contractType) { this.toast('Contract Type is required.', 'e'); document.getElementById('vh-contract-type').focus(); return; }
    if ((st === 'maintenance' || st === 'scheduled') && !v.maintStart && !v.maintEnd) {
      this.toast('Please set at least a start or end date.', 'e'); return;
    }
    if (v.maintStart && v.maintEnd && v.maintStart > v.maintEnd) {
      this.toast('Start date must be before end date.', 'e'); return;
    }
    if (st !== 'returned') {
      const issues = this.vehicleDateIssues(v);
      if (issues.missing.length || issues.expired.length) {
        this.refreshVehicleDateRequirements();
        this.toast('Update the required vehicle dates before saving.', 'e'); return;
      }
    }
    // New vehicle → check for a duplicate plate first (alerts, does not block).
    if (v.isNew) {
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(res => {
          const dups = (res && res.duplicates) || [];
          if (dups.length) {
            const list = dups.map(d => d.plate + (d.type?(' · '+d.type):'')).join(', ');
            this._confirmDuplicate(
              'Possible duplicate vehicle',
              `A vehicle with plate <strong>${this.esc(v.plate)}</strong> already exists: ${this.esc(list)}.<br><br>Do you still want to add this as a new vehicle?`,
              () => this._doSaveVehicle(v)
            );
          } else {
            this._doSaveVehicle(v);
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(() => this._doSaveVehicle(v))
        .checkDuplicateVehicle(v.plate, v.origPlate || '');
    } else {
      this._doSaveVehicle(v);
    }
  },

  _prepareVehicleReturn(v) {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(res => {
      const rows=(res&&res.ok&&res.rows)||[];
      if(!rows.length){ this._doSaveVehicle(v,true); return; }
      this._pendingReturnedVehicle=v;
      this._returnDefectQueue=rows.slice();
      this.toast('Close the open defect report'+(rows.length===1?'':'s')+' before returning this vehicle.','i');
      this.openCloseDefectModal(rows[0].ReportNumber, rows[0]);
    }).withFailureHandler(e => this.toast('Could not verify open defects: '+e.message,'e')).getVehicleDefects({plate:v.origPlate||v.plate,openOnly:true,limit:500});
  },

  _doSaveVehicle(v, returnDefectsChecked) {
    if(v.status==='returned' && !returnDefectsChecked){ this._prepareVehicleReturn(v); return; }
    this._runBtn(document.getElementById('save-vehicle-btn'), 'Saving...', done => {
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(r => {
          done(r.ok);
          if (r.ok) {
            this.toast('Vehicle saved!', 's');
            this.closeModal('vehicle-modal');
            google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(vs => { this.D.vehicles = vs; this.renderVehicles(); this.renderFleetComplianceAlerts(); }).getVehicles();
            const isUnavail = v.status === 'maintenance' || v.status === 'scheduled' || v.status === 'returned';
            if (isUnavail) {
              google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(res => {
                if (res.ok && res.affected && res.affected.length > 0) this._showVehicleConflictAlert(v.plate, res.affected);
              }).checkVehicleInRosters(v.plate);
            }
          } else { this.toast('Error: ' + r.msg, 'e'); }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(e => { done(false); this.toast(e.message, 'e'); })
        .saveVehicle(v);
    });
  },

  renderVehicles() {
    this.renderVehicleAlerts();
    this.renderVehicleDefectDashboard();
    const tb = document.getElementById('vehicles-tbody');
    let vehicles = this.D.vehicles || [];
    if (this._vehFilter === 'available') {
      vehicles = vehicles.filter(v => !this.vehIsUnavailable(v));
    } else if (this._vehFilter === 'maintenance') {
      vehicles = vehicles.filter(v => v.status === 'maintenance' && this.vehIsUnavailable(v));
    } else if (this._vehFilter === 'scheduled') {
      vehicles = vehicles.filter(v => v.status === 'scheduled');
    } else if (this._vehFilter === 'returned') {
      vehicles = vehicles.filter(v => v.status === 'returned');
    } else if (this._vehFilter === 'defects') {
      vehicles = vehicles.filter(v => this.vehicleOpenDefects(v.plate).length);
    } else if (this._vehFilter === 'out_of_service') {
      vehicles = vehicles.filter(v => this.vehicleOpenDefects(v.plate).some(d => String(d.VehicleStatus||'') === 'Out Of Service'));
    }
    if (!vehicles.length) {
      tb.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="8"><div class="empty-state"><h3>No vehicles${this._vehFilter!=='all'?' in this category':''}</h3></div></td></tr>`;
      return;
    }
    tb.innerHTML = vehicles.map(v => {
      const unavail = this.vehIsUnavailable(v);
      const defects = this.vehicleOpenDefects(v.plate);
      const critical = defects.some(d => String(d.Severity||'').toLowerCase() === 'critical');
      const oos = defects.some(d => String(d.VehicleStatus||'') === 'Out Of Service');
      const alertRow = oos ? 'defect-row-oos' : (critical ? 'defect-row-critical' : (defects.length ? 'defect-row-alert' : ''));
      const safeV   = JSON.stringify(v).replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
      const range   = (v.maintStart||v.maintEnd)
        ? `${v.maintStart?this.fmtFull(v.maintStart):'?'} → ${v.maintEnd?this.fmtFull(v.maintEnd):'?'}`
        : '—';
      return `<tr class="${alertRow}" style="${unavail?'opacity:.65':''}">
        <td><strong style="font-family:monospace;letter-spacing:.5px">${this.esc(v.plate)}</strong></td>
        <td>${this.esc(v.type)}</td>
        <td>${this.vehStatusBadge(v)}</td>
        <td>${this.vehicleDefectBadges(v.plate)}</td>
        <td>${this.vehicleAlertBadges(v.plate)}</td>
        <td style="font-size:12px;color:var(--txt2);white-space:nowrap">${(v.status==='maintenance'||v.status==='scheduled')?range:'—'}</td>
        <td style="font-size:12px;color:var(--txt2)">${this.esc(v.notes)}</td>
        <td>
          <div style="display:flex;gap:5px;flex-wrap:wrap">
            <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onclick="App.openVehicleProfile('${this.esc(v.plate)}')">Profile</button>
            <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="App.openVehicleDefectModal(null,'${this.esc(v.plate)}')">+ Defect</button>
            <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick='App.openVehicleModal(${safeV})'>Edit</button>
            <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" style="color:var(--red)" onclick="App.confirmDeleteVehicle('${this.esc(v.plate)}')">Delete</button>
          </div>
        </td>
      </tr>`;
    }).join('');
  },

  // Force the History → Maintenance tab to reload (defects + maintenance) next time it's shown.
  _invalidateMaintHistory() {
    this.D.maintLog = null;
    this.D.maintDefects = null;
    if (this._histTab === 'maint') this.loadMaintLogIfNeeded();
  },

  refreshFleetMaintenance() {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(res => {
      if (res && res.ok) this.D.vehicleDefects = res.rows || [];
      google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(stats => {
        this.D.vehicleDefectStats = stats || {};
        google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(vs => {
          this.D.vehicles = vs || this.D.vehicles || [];
          this.renderVehicles();
          this.renderFleetComplianceAlerts();
          this.toast('Fleet maintenance refreshed.', 's');
        }).getVehicles();
      }).getVehicleDefectStats();
    }).withFailureHandler(e => this.toast(e.message, 'e')).getVehicleDefects({ openOnly:true });
  },

  vehicleOpenDefects(plate) {
    const p = String(plate || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    return (this.D.vehicleDefects || []).filter(d =>
      String(d.VehiclePlate || d.vehiclePlate || '').trim().toUpperCase() === p &&
      String(d.Status || d.status || '').toLowerCase() !== 'closed'
    );
  },

  vehicleDefectBadges(plate) {
    const defects = this.vehicleOpenDefects(plate);
    if (!defects.length) return '<span style="color:var(--txt2);font-size:12px">—</span>';
    const html = defects.slice(0,4).map(d => {
      const sev = String(d.Severity || '').toLowerCase();
      const vs  = String(d.VehicleStatus || '');
      const cls = vs === 'Out Of Service' ? 'defect-oos' : (sev === 'critical' ? 'defect-critical' : (sev === 'major' ? 'defect-major' : 'defect-minor'));
      const icon = vs === 'Out Of Service' ? '🚫' : (sev === 'critical' ? '🔴' : (sev === 'major' ? '🟠' : '🟡'));
      return `<span class="defect-badge ${cls}" title="${this.esc(d.Description||'')}">${icon} ${this.esc(d.ReportNumber||'DR')} ${this.esc(d.Severity||'')}</span>`;
    }).join('');
    return `<div class="defect-badges">${html}${defects.length>4?`<span class="defect-badge">+${defects.length-4}</span>`:''}</div>`;
  },

  renderVehicleDefectDashboard() {
    const box = document.getElementById('vehicle-defect-dashboard');
    if (!box) return;
    const stats = this.computeVehicleDefectStats();
    box.innerHTML = `
      <div class="vehicle-defect-card" onclick="App.filterVehicleDefectsFromDashboard('defects')">
        <div class="k">Vehicle Defects</div><div class="v">${stats.totalOpen}</div>
        <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--txt2);margin-top:5px">${stats.affectedVehicles} affected vehicle${stats.affectedVehicles!==1?'s':''}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="vehicle-defect-card vehicle-defect-critical" onclick="App.filterVehicleDefectsFromDashboard('defects')"><div class="k">Critical</div><div class="v">${stats.critical}</div></div>
      <div class="vehicle-defect-card vehicle-defect-major" onclick="App.filterVehicleDefectsFromDashboard('defects')"><div class="k">Major</div><div class="v">${stats.major}</div></div>
      <div class="vehicle-defect-card vehicle-defect-minor" onclick="App.filterVehicleDefectsFromDashboard('defects')"><div class="k">Minor</div><div class="v">${stats.minor}</div></div>
      <div class="vehicle-defect-card vehicle-defect-oos" onclick="App.filterVehicleDefectsFromDashboard('out_of_service')"><div class="k">Out Of Service</div><div class="v">${stats.outOfService}</div></div>
    `;
  },

  computeVehicleDefectStats() {
    const open = (this.D.vehicleDefects || []).filter(d => String(d.Status||'').toLowerCase() !== 'closed');
    const stats = { critical:0, major:0, minor:0, outOfService:0, totalOpen:open.length, affectedVehicles:0 };
    const plates = {};
    open.forEach(d => {
      const sev = String(d.Severity||'').toLowerCase();
      if (sev === 'critical') stats.critical++;
      else if (sev === 'major') stats.major++;
      else stats.minor++;
      if (String(d.VehicleStatus||'') === 'Out Of Service') stats.outOfService++;
      if (d.VehiclePlate) plates[String(d.VehiclePlate).toUpperCase()] = true;
    });
    stats.affectedVehicles = Object.keys(plates).length;
    return stats;
  },

  filterVehicleDefectsFromDashboard(filter) {
    const btn = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.veh-filter')).find(b => b.dataset.f === filter);
    if (btn) this.filterVehicles(btn);
    this.nav('vehicles');
  },

  populateDefectVehicles(selectedPlate) {
    const sel = document.getElementById('vd-vehicle');
    if (!sel) return;
    sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select vehicle</option>' + (this.D.vehicles||[]).map(v => `<option value="${this.esc(v.plate)}" data-type="${this.esc(v.type||'')}">${this.esc(v.plate)} — ${this.esc(v.type||'')}</option>`).join('');
    if (selectedPlate) sel.value = selectedPlate;
  },

  onDefectVehicleChange() {
    const sel = document.getElementById('vd-vehicle');
    const opt = sel && sel.options[sel.selectedIndex];
    this._defectVehicleType = opt ? (opt.getAttribute('data-type') || '') : '';
  },

  // Current event type in the unified modal: 'corrective' | 'scheduled'
  _vehicleEventKind: 'corrective',

  setVehicleEventKind(kind) {
    this._vehicleEventKind = kind;
    document.getElementById('ve-type-corrective').classList.toggle('active', kind === 'corrective');
    document.getElementById('ve-type-scheduled').classList.toggle('active', kind === 'scheduled');
    const corrective = kind === 'corrective';
    // Show/hide corrective-only fields
    document.querySelectorAll('.ve-corrective').forEach(el => { el.style.display = corrective ? '' : 'none'; });
    // Relabel shared fields for clarity
    document.getElementById('vehicle-defect-title').textContent = corrective ? 'Report Defect (Corrective)' : 'Schedule Maintenance';
    document.getElementById('vd-description-label').textContent = corrective ? 'Description' : 'Work to be done / reason';
    document.getElementById('vd-description').placeholder = corrective ? 'Describe the defect found during the walk-around check' : 'e.g. Annual service, CVRT test, brake inspection...';
    document.getElementById('save-defect-btn').textContent = corrective ? 'Save Defect' : 'Schedule Maintenance';
    if (corrective) {
      this.onDefectRoadworthyChange();
    } else {
      // Scheduled always books the truck out for the date window
      document.getElementById('vd-start-label').textContent = 'Maintenance from';
      document.getElementById('vd-end-label').textContent = 'Back in service on';
      document.getElementById('vd-block-warning').style.display = 'none';
      document.getElementById('vd-hint').textContent = 'Books the truck out of service for these dates and records it in maintenance history. The Roster will show it as unavailable.';
    }
  },

  onDefectRoadworthyChange() {
    const oos = document.getElementById('vd-vehicle-status').value.toLowerCase().indexOf('out of service') !== -1;
    document.getElementById('vd-block-warning').style.display = oos ? '' : 'none';
    document.getElementById('vd-start-label').textContent = oos ? 'Out of service from' : 'Date noticed';
    document.getElementById('vd-end-label').textContent = oos ? 'Expected return date' : 'Expected return date (optional)';
    document.getElementById('vd-hint').textContent = oos
      ? 'Logs the defect, creates the A4 report, and marks the truck UNAVAILABLE on the Roster for these dates.'
      : 'Logs the defect and creates the A4 report. The truck stays available on the Roster.';
  },

  openVehicleDefectModal(defect, plate) {
    this.populateDefectVehicles(plate || (defect && defect.VehiclePlate) || '');
    document.getElementById('vd-report-number').value = defect ? (defect.ReportNumber || '') : '';
    document.getElementById('vd-vehicle').value = defect ? (defect.VehiclePlate || '') : (plate || '');
    document.getElementById('vd-driver').value = defect ? (defect.DriverInspector || '') : '';
    document.getElementById('vd-severity').value = defect ? (defect.Severity || 'Minor') : 'Minor';
    document.getElementById('vd-category').value = defect ? (defect.Category || 'Other') : 'Other';
    document.getElementById('vd-vehicle-status').value = defect ? (defect.VehicleStatus || 'Roadworthy') : 'Roadworthy';
    document.getElementById('vd-description').value = defect ? (defect.Description || '') : '';
    document.getElementById('vd-start-date').value = '';
    document.getElementById('vd-expected-return').value = defect ? String(defect.ExpectedReturnDate || '').slice(0,10) : '';
    document.getElementById('vd-supervisor').value = defect ? (defect.SupervisorName || '') : '';
    this.onDefectVehicleChange();
    // Editing an existing defect → corrective; new → default corrective too.
    this.setVehicleEventKind('corrective');
    this.openModal('vehicle-defect-modal');
    setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('vd-vehicle').focus(), 100);
  },

  // Open the modal straight into Scheduled Maintenance mode.
  openScheduleMaintenance(plate) {
    this.openVehicleDefectModal(null, plate || '');
    this.setVehicleEventKind('scheduled');
    const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
    document.getElementById('vd-start-date').value = today;
  },

  saveVehicleEvent() {
    const kind = this._vehicleEventKind || 'corrective';
    const sel = document.getElementById('vd-vehicle');
    const opt = sel && sel.options[sel.selectedIndex];
    const plate = sel.value;
    const type = opt ? (opt.getAttribute('data-type') || '') : '';
    const startDate = document.getElementById('vd-start-date').value;
    const endDate = document.getElementById('vd-expected-return').value;
    const description = document.getElementById('vd-description').value.trim();

    if (!plate) { this.toast('Select a vehicle.', 'e'); return; }

    let payload;
    if (kind === 'corrective') {
      payload = {
        kind: 'corrective', plate: plate, type: type,
        reportNumber: document.getElementById('vd-report-number').value,
        driverInspector: document.getElementById('vd-driver').value.trim(),
        severity: document.getElementById('vd-severity').value,
        category: document.getElementById('vd-category').value,
        description: description,
        vehicleStatus: document.getElementById('vd-vehicle-status').value,
        startDate: startDate, endDate: endDate,
        supervisorName: document.getElementById('vd-supervisor').value.trim(),
        notes: description
      };
      if (!payload.driverInspector || !payload.description || !payload.supervisorName) {
        this.toast('Driver/Inspector, Description and Supervisor are required.', 'e'); return;
      }
    } else {
      payload = {
        kind: 'scheduled', plate: plate, type: type,
        startDate: startDate, endDate: endDate,
        notes: description, description: description
      };
      if (!payload.startDate) { this.toast('Enter the maintenance start date.', 'e'); return; }
    }

    this._runBtn(document.getElementById('save-defect-btn'), 'Saving...', done => {
      google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(r => {
        done(r && r.ok);
        if (r && r.ok) {
          const blockedMsg = r.blocked ? ' — truck marked unavailable on the Roster' : '';
          this.toast((kind === 'corrective' ? 'Defect saved' : 'Maintenance scheduled') + blockedMsg, 's');
          this.closeModal('vehicle-defect-modal');
          this.refreshFleetMaintenance();
          this._invalidateMaintHistory();
        } else this.toast('Error: ' + ((r && r.msg) || 'Could not save'), 'e');
      }).withFailureHandler(e => { done(false); this.toast(e.message, 'e'); }).recordVehicleEvent(payload);
    });
  },

  openCloseDefectModal(reportNumber, defectRecord) {
    const pool = [].concat(this.D.vehicleDefects || [], this.D.maintDefects || [], this._returnDefectQueue || []);
    const d = defectRecord || pool.find(x => String(x.ReportNumber||'') === String(reportNumber));
    if (!d) { this.toast('Defect not found in current list. Refresh and try again.', 'e'); return; }
    const inReturnFlow = !!this._pendingReturnedVehicle;
    document.getElementById('vehicle-defect-close-title').textContent = (inReturnFlow ? 'Close before vehicle return — ' : 'Close Defect Report ') + reportNumber;
    const note=document.getElementById('vdc-return-flow-note');
    if(note){ note.style.display=inReturnFlow?'':'none'; note.textContent=inReturnFlow ? ('This vehicle has '+(this._returnDefectQueue||[]).length+' open defect(s). Complete the repair/closure information before it can be set to Returned / Not Available.') : ''; }
    document.getElementById('vdc-report-number').value = reportNumber;
    document.getElementById('vdc-summary').innerHTML = `<strong>${this.esc(d.VehiclePlate||'')}</strong> · ${this.esc(d.Severity||'')} · ${this.esc(d.Category||'')}<br>${this.esc(d.Description||'')}`;
    document.getElementById('vdc-repair-date').value = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
    document.getElementById('vdc-workshop').value = '';
    document.getElementById('vdc-cost').value = '';
    document.getElementById('vdc-completed-by').value = '';
    document.getElementById('vdc-notes').value = '';
    this.openModal('vehicle-defect-close-modal');
  },

  cancelVehicleReturnDefectFlow() {
    const wasReturnFlow=!!this._pendingReturnedVehicle;
    this._pendingReturnedVehicle=null; this._returnDefectQueue=[];
    this.closeModal('vehicle-defect-close-modal');
    if(wasReturnFlow) this.toast('Vehicle return cancelled. Open defects were not bypassed.','i');
  },

  closeVehicleDefect() {
    const payload = {
      reportNumber: document.getElementById('vdc-report-number').value,
      repairDate: document.getElementById('vdc-repair-date').value,
      workshop: document.getElementById('vdc-workshop').value.trim(),
      cost: document.getElementById('vdc-cost').value,
      completedBy: document.getElementById('vdc-completed-by').value.trim(),
      notes: document.getElementById('vdc-notes').value.trim()
    };
    if (!payload.reportNumber || !payload.repairDate || !payload.completedBy) {
      this.toast('Repair Date and Completed By are required.', 'e'); return;
    }
    this._runBtn(document.getElementById('close-defect-btn'), 'Closing...', done => {
      google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(r => {
        done(r && r.ok);
        if (r && r.ok) {
          this.toast('Defect closed and sent to Vehicle Maintenance History.', 's');
          this.closeModal('vehicle-defect-close-modal');
          this.refreshFleetMaintenance();
          this._invalidateMaintHistory();
          if (this._pendingReturnedVehicle) {
            this._returnDefectQueue = (this._returnDefectQueue||[]).filter(d => String(d.ReportNumber||'') !== String(payload.reportNumber));
            if (this._returnDefectQueue.length) {
              setTimeout(() => this.openCloseDefectModal(this._returnDefectQueue[0].ReportNumber, this._returnDefectQueue[0]), 120);
            } else {
              const vehicle=this._pendingReturnedVehicle;
              this._pendingReturnedVehicle=null; this._returnDefectQueue=[];
              setTimeout(() => this._doSaveVehicle(vehicle, true), 120);
            }
          } else if (this._vehicleProfilePlate) this.openVehicleProfile(this._vehicleProfilePlate);
        } else this.toast('Error: ' + ((r && r.msg) || 'Could not close defect'), 'e');
      }).withFailureHandler(e => { done(false); this.toast(e.message, 'e'); }).closeVehicleDefect(payload);
    });
  },

  printDefectReport(reportNumber) {
    const d = (this.D.vehicleDefects || []).concat(this._profileDefects || []).find(x => String(x.ReportNumber||'') === String(reportNumber));
    if (!d) { this.toast('Defect not found in current list. Refresh and try again.', 'e'); return; }
    const html = `<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><title>${this.esc(d.ReportNumber||'Defect Report')}</title>
      <style>@page{size:A4;margin:18mm}body{font-family:Arial,sans-serif;color:#111827}h1{font-size:22px;margin:0 0 18px;text-align:center;letter-spacing:.5px}.box{border:1px solid #111827;padding:14px;margin-bottom:12px}.row{display:grid;grid-template-columns:190px 1fr;border-bottom:1px solid #e5e7eb;padding:8px 0}.row:last-child{border-bottom:none}.lbl{font-weight:700;color:#374151}.sig{height:44px;border-bottom:1px solid #111827;margin-top:12px}</style>
      </head><body>
      <h1>WALK AROUND CHECK &<br>DEFECT REPORT (HGV)</h1>
      <div class="box">
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Report No:</div><div>${this.esc(d.ReportNumber||'')}</div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Vehicle:</div><div>${this.esc(d.VehiclePlate||'')}</div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Driver:</div><div>${this.esc(d.DriverInspector||'')}</div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Date:</div><div>${this.esc(String(d.DateTime||d.CreatedAt||'').slice(0,16))}</div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Defect Category:</div><div>${this.esc(d.Category||'')}</div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Severity:</div><div>${this.esc(d.Severity||'')}</div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Description:</div><div>${this.esc(d.Description||'')}</div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Vehicle Status:</div><div>${this.esc(d.VehicleStatus||'')}</div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Expected Return:</div><div>${d.ExpectedReturnDate ? this.fmtFull(String(d.ExpectedReturnDate).slice(0,10)) : ''}</div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Supervisor:</div><div>${this.esc(d.SupervisorName||'')}</div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Signature:</div><div><div class="sig"></div></div></div>
      </div>
      </body></html>`;
    const w = window.open('', '_blank');
    if (!w) { this.toast('Popup blocked. Allow popups to print the report.', 'e'); return; }
    w.document.open();
    w.document.write(html);
    w.document.close();
    setTimeout(() => { try { w.focus(); w.print(); } catch (e) {} }, 250);
  },

  openVehicleProfile(plate) {
    this._vehicleProfilePlate = plate;
    this._vehicleProfileTab = 'compliance';
    document.getElementById('vehicle-profile-title').textContent = plate + ' — Fleet Maintenance & Defect Management';
    this.openModal('vehicle-profile-modal');
    this.switchVehicleProfileTab('compliance');
  },

  switchVehicleProfileTab(tab) {
    this._vehicleProfileTab = tab;
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-fleet-tab]').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.fleetTab === tab));
    const plate = this._vehicleProfilePlate;
    const body = document.getElementById('vehicle-profile-body');
    if (!plate || !body) return;
    const v = (this.D.vehicles||[]).find(x => String(x.plate) === String(plate)) || { plate };
    if (tab === 'compliance') {
      const due = this.getVehicleAlertsForPlate(plate,false);
      body.innerHTML = `<div class="card" style="padding:14px;margin-bottom:12px">
        <h3 style="font-size:16px;margin-bottom:8px">${this.esc(plate)}</h3>
        <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(180px,1fr));gap:10px">
          ${this.vehicleDueFields.map(([label,key]) => `<div class="vehicle-defect-card"><div class="k">${this.esc(label)}</div><div style="font-weight:900;margin-top:7px">${v[key] ? this.fmtFull(v[key]) : 'Not set'}</div></div>`).join('')}
        </div>
      </div>${due.length ? '<div class="vehicle-alert-wrap" style="display:flex">' + due.map(a=>`<div class="vehicle-alert ${(a.missing||a.days<0)?'overdue':''}"><strong>${this.esc(a.label)}</strong> — ${a.missing?'not set':this.fmtFull(a.date)}</div>`).join('') + '</div>' : ''}`;
    } else if (tab === 'defects') {
      body.innerHTML = '<div class="loading-wrap"><div class="spinner"></div>Loading defects…</div>';
      google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(res => {
        const rows = (res && res.rows) || [];
        this._profileDefects = rows;
        body.innerHTML = rows.length ? `<table class="data-table"><thead><tr><th>Report</th><th>Date</th><th>Severity</th><th>Category</th><th>Status</th><th>Description</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map(d=>`<tr><td><strong>${this.esc(d.ReportNumber||'')}</strong></td><td>${this.esc(String(d.DateTime||d.CreatedAt||'').slice(0,10))}</td><td>${this.esc(d.Severity||'')}</td><td>${this.esc(d.Category||'')}</td><td>${this.esc(d.Status||'')}</td><td>${this.esc(d.Description||'')}</td><td style="white-space:nowrap"><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="App.printDefectReport('${this.esc(d.ReportNumber||'')}')">Print</button>${String(d.Status||'').toLowerCase()==='closed'?'':`<button class="btn btn-success btn-sm" onclick="App.openCloseDefectModal('${this.esc(d.ReportNumber||'')}')">Close</button>`}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table>` : '<div class="empty-state"><h3>No defect reports for this vehicle</h3></div>';
      }).getVehicleDefects({ plate: plate, limit: 500 });
    } else if (tab === 'maintenance') {
      body.innerHTML = '<div class="loading-wrap"><div class="spinner"></div>Loading maintenance history…</div>';
      google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(res => {
        const rows = (res && res.rows) || [];
        body.innerHTML = rows.length ? `<table class="data-table"><thead><tr><th>Repair Date</th><th>Report</th><th>Defect</th><th>Workshop</th><th>Cost</th><th>Completed By</th><th>Notes</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map(r=>`<tr><td>${this.esc(String(r.RepairDate||'').slice(0,10))}</td><td>${this.esc(r.ReportNumber||'')}</td><td>${this.esc(r.Defect||'')}</td><td>${this.esc(r.Workshop||'')}</td><td>${this.esc(r.Cost||'')}</td><td>${this.esc(r.CompletedBy||'')}</td><td>${this.esc(r.Notes||'')}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table>` : '<div class="empty-state"><h3>No maintenance history yet</h3><p>Closed defect reports will appear here automatically.</p></div>';
      }).getVehicleMaintenanceHistory({ plate: plate, limit: 500 });
    } else if (tab === 'timeline') {
      body.innerHTML = '<div class="loading-wrap"><div class="spinner"></div>Loading timeline…</div>';
      google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(res => {
        const rows = (res && res.rows) || [];
        body.innerHTML = rows.length ? `<div class="timeline">${rows.map(t=>`<div class="timeline-item"><div class="timeline-date">${this.fmtFull(String(t.date||'').slice(0,10))}</div><div class="timeline-title">${this.esc(t.title||t.type||'')}</div><div class="timeline-detail">${this.esc(t.detail||'')}</div></div>`).join('')}</div>` : '<div class="empty-state"><h3>No timeline yet</h3></div>';
      }).getVehicleTimeline(plate);
    }
  },

  confirmDeleteVehicle(plate) {
    document.getElementById('confirm-msg').textContent = `Delete vehicle "${plate}"?`;
    document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn').onclick = () => {
      const okBtn = document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn');
      this._runBtn(okBtn, 'Deleting…', done => {
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(r => {
            done(r && r.ok);
            if (r && r.ok) {
              this.toast('Vehicle deleted.', 'i');
              this.closeModal('confirm-modal');
              google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(vs => { this.D.vehicles = vs; this.renderVehicles(); this.renderFleetComplianceAlerts(); }).getVehicles();
            } else { this.toast('Error: ' + ((r && r.msg) || 'Could not delete vehicle'), 'e'); }
          })
          .withFailureHandler(e => { done(false); this.toast(e.message, 'e'); })
          .deleteVehicle(plate);
      });
    };
    this.openModal('confirm-modal');
  },

  // Customers
  renderCustomers(forceReload) {
    var self = this;
    var custSel = document.getElementById('route-route-filter');
    var host = document.getElementById('route-planner');

    // PERFORMANCE: stale-while-revalidate.
    // If we already have planner data, paint it INSTANTLY from memory, then refresh
    // quietly in the background. Only show the "Loading…" state on the very first open.
    var haveCache = Array.isArray(this.PL) && this.PL.length > 0;
    var fresh = haveCache && !forceReload && this._plLoadedAt && (Date.now() - this._plLoadedAt < 60000);

    if (haveCache) {
      this._renderPlanner();        // instant
      if (fresh) return;            // cache is recent enough — skip the server call entirely
      if (this._plLoading && !forceReload) return; // avoid duplicate background refreshes
    } else if (host) {
      host.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state"><h3>Loading\u2026</h3></div>';
      if (this._plLoading && !forceReload) return;
    }

    var loadSeq = (this._plLoadSeq || 0) + 1;
    this._plLoadSeq = loadSeq;
    this._plLoading = true;
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){
      if (loadSeq !== self._plLoadSeq) return;
      self._plLoading = false;
      var rows = (res && res.customers) || [];
      self.PL = rows;
      self._overlays = (res && res.overlays) || [];
      self._plLoadedAt = Date.now();
      if (res && res.routes && res.routes.length) self.D.routes = res.routes;
      if (custSel) {
        var existing = Array.prototype.slice.call(custSel.options).map(function(o){return o.value;}).filter(Boolean);
        var nums = Object.keys((self.D.routes||[]).reduce(function(a,r){if(r.route)a[r.route]=1;return a;}, rows.reduce(function(a,c){a[c.route]=1;return a;},{}))).sort(function(a,b){return String(a).localeCompare(String(b),undefined,{numeric:true});});
        nums.forEach(function(r){ if(existing.indexOf(r)===-1){ var o=document.createElement('option'); o.value=r; o.textContent='Route '+r; custSel.appendChild(o);} });
      }
      self._renderPlanner();
    }).withFailureHandler(function(e){
      if (loadSeq !== self._plLoadSeq) return;
      self._plLoading = false;
      // On a background refresh failure, keep showing the cached view silently.
      if (!haveCache && host) host.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state"><h3>Could not load</h3><p>'+self.esc(e.message)+'</p></div>';
    }).getDeliveryPlanner();
  },

  _renderPlanner() {
    var self = this;
    var host = document.getElementById('route-planner'); if(!host) return;
    var rf = (document.getElementById('route-route-filter')||{}).value || '';
    var df = (document.getElementById('route-day-filter')||{}).value || '';
    var q  = ((document.getElementById('route-search')||{}).value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    var allRows = this.PL || [];
    var rows = allRows.filter(function(c){
      if (rf && String(c.route)!==String(rf)) return false;
      if (df && String(c.day).toLowerCase().trim()!==df.toLowerCase().trim()) return false;
      if (q) {
        var hay = [c.customer,c.route,c.displayName,c.eircode,c.time].join(' ').toLowerCase();
        if (hay.indexOf(q)<0) return false;
      }
      return true;
    });
    var DAYS=['monday','tuesday','wednesday','thursday','friday','saturday','sunday'];
    var groups={};
    rows.forEach(function(c){
      var k=String(c.day).toLowerCase()+'||'+c.route;
      (groups[k]=groups[k]||{day:c.day,route:c.route,rows:[],routeMeta:null}).rows.push(c);
    });

    // External routes must be visible as one normal route bar per selected day,
    // including route days that do not have any delivery stops yet.
    (this.D.routes||[]).forEach(function(r){
      if (String(r.routeType||'STANDARD').toUpperCase()!=='EXTERNAL') return;
      var route=String(r.route||'');
      var day=String(r.day||'');
      if (!route || !day) return;
      if (rf && route!==String(rf)) return;
      if (df && day.toLowerCase().trim()!==df.toLowerCase().trim()) return;
      var k=day.toLowerCase()+'||'+route;
      if (q && !groups[k]) {
        var routeHay=[route,r.displayName,r.code,day,'external'].join(' ').toLowerCase();
        if (routeHay.indexOf(q)<0) return;
      }
      groups[k]=groups[k]||{day:day,route:route,rows:[],routeMeta:r};
      groups[k].routeMeta=r;
    });

    var keys=Object.keys(groups).sort(function(a,b){
      var ga=groups[a],gb=groups[b];
      return DAYS.indexOf(String(ga.day).toLowerCase())-DAYS.indexOf(String(gb.day).toLowerCase())
        || String(ga.route).localeCompare(String(gb.route),undefined,{numeric:true});
    });
    if(!keys.length){
      host.innerHTML='<div class="empty-state"><h3>No routes or stops found</h3><p>Adjust the filters, use Add collection, or create an External Route.</p></div>';
      return;
    }

    var html='';
    keys.forEach(function(k){
      var g=groups[k];
      g.rows.sort(function(a,b){var ao=(a.order&&a.order>0)?a.order:9999;var bo=(b.order&&b.order>0)?b.order:9999;return ao-bo;});
      var dayKey = (self.DAY_FULL||[]).map(function(x){return String(x).toLowerCase();}).indexOf(String(g.day||'').toLowerCase());
      dayKey = dayKey >= 0 ? self.DAYS[dayKey] : String(g.day||'').slice(0,3).toLowerCase();
      var routeMeta=g.routeMeta
        || (self.D.routes||[]).find(function(r){return String(r.route)===String(g.route) && String(r.day||'').toLowerCase()===String(g.day||'').toLowerCase();})
        || (self.D.routes||[]).find(function(r){return String(r.route)===String(g.route) && self.routeMatchesDay(r, dayKey);})
        || (self.D.routes||[]).find(function(r){return String(r.route)===String(g.route);}) || {};
      var isExternal=String(routeMeta.routeType||'STANDARD').toUpperCase()==='EXTERNAL';
      var colour=(g.rows[0]&&g.rows[0].colour)||routeMeta.colour||'#94a3b8';
      var disp=(g.rows[0]&&g.rows[0].displayName)||routeMeta.displayName||('Route '+g.route);
      var kg=g.rows.reduce(function(s,c){return s+(parseFloat(c.kg)||0);},0);
      var collapsed = self.customerCollapsed[k] !== false;
      var kmVal = routeMeta.km !== undefined && routeMeta.km !== null && routeMeta.km !== '' ? routeMeta.km : '';
      var hrVal = routeMeta.hours !== undefined && routeMeta.hours !== null && routeMeta.hours !== '' ? routeMeta.hours : '';
      var groupHasOverlay = g.rows.some(function(c){ return c.overlay; });
      var cardClass='card cust-route-card'+(groupHasOverlay?' has-overlay':'')+(isExternal?' external-route-main':'')+(isExternal&&routeMeta.active===false?' inactive':'');
      var routeActions='';
      if(isExternal){
        var routePayload=encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify({
          route:routeMeta.route||g.route,
          day:routeMeta.day||g.day,
          routeType:'EXTERNAL',
          displayName:routeMeta.displayName||disp,
          code:routeMeta.code||routeMeta.route||g.route,
          colour:routeMeta.colour||colour,
          km:routeMeta.km||0,
          hours:routeMeta.hours||0,
          stops:routeMeta.stops||0,
          active:routeMeta.active!==false,
          validFrom:routeMeta.validFrom||'',
          validUntil:routeMeta.validUntil||''
        })).replace(/'/g,'%27');
        var inactive=routeMeta.active===false;
        routeActions='<div class="cust-route-actions" onclick="event.stopPropagation()">'
          +'<button class="route-head-btn add-stop" '+(inactive?'disabled title="Reactivate this route before adding stops"':'title="Add a delivery-only stop to this route day" onclick="App.openExternalDelivery(JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(\''+routePayload+'\')))"')+'>＋ Add delivery stop</button>'
          +'<button class="route-head-btn" title="Edit this External Route family" onclick="App.openRouteModal(JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(\''+routePayload+'\')))">Edit route</button>'
          +'<button class="route-head-btn" title="'+(inactive?'Make this route available in the Roster':'Keep history and stops, but remove this route from new Roster selection')+'" onclick="App.toggleExternalRoute(JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(\''+routePayload+'\')))">'+(inactive?'Reactivate':'Set inactive')+'</button>'
          +'</div>';
      }
      html+='<div class="'+cardClass+'" style="margin-bottom:16px;overflow:hidden">'
        +'<div class="cust-route-head '+(collapsed?'collapsed':'')+'" data-key="'+self.esc(k)+'" onclick="App.toggleCustomerRoute(this.dataset.key)">'
        +'<span class="cust-route-chevron '+(collapsed?'closed':'')+'">▼</span>'
        +'<span class="cust-route-swatch" style="background:'+self.esc(colour)+'"></span>'
        +'<div class="cust-route-title"><div class="cust-route-title-main">'+self.esc(disp)+(isExternal?'<span class="external-route-tag">External</span>':'')+'</div><div class="cust-route-title-sub">Route '+self.esc(g.route)+' · '+self.esc(g.day)+(isExternal&&routeMeta.active===false?' · Inactive':'')+'</div></div>'
        +routeActions
        +'<div class="cust-route-metrics">'
          +'<div class="cust-metric"><span class="v">'+g.rows.length+'</span><span class="l">Stops</span></div>'
          +'<div class="cust-metric editable" title="Click to edit KM" data-route="'+self.esc(g.route)+'" data-day="'+self.esc(g.day)+'" data-key="'+self.esc(k)+'" data-field="km" data-value="'+self.esc(kmVal)+'" onclick="event.stopPropagation();App.editInlineRouteStat(this)"><span class="v">'+(kmVal!==''?self.esc(kmVal):'—')+'</span><span class="l">KM</span></div>'
          +'<div class="cust-metric editable" title="Click to edit Hours" data-route="'+self.esc(g.route)+'" data-day="'+self.esc(g.day)+'" data-key="'+self.esc(k)+'" data-field="hours" data-value="'+self.esc(hrVal)+'" onclick="event.stopPropagation();App.editInlineRouteStat(this)"><span class="v">'+(hrVal!==''?self.esc(hrVal):'—')+'</span><span class="l">Hours</span></div>'
          +'<div class="cust-metric"><span class="v">'+Math.round(kg)+'</span><span class="l">KG</span></div>'
        +'</div></div>'
        +'<div class="cust-route-body" style="display:'+(collapsed?'none':'block')+'">'
        +'<table class="data-table"><thead><tr><th style="width:44px">#</th><th>Customer / delivery stop</th><th>Time</th><th>Clothes</th><th>MOP</th><th>Eircode</th><th style="text-align:right">KG</th><th style="width:230px"></th></tr></thead><tbody>';

      if(!g.rows.length && isExternal){
        html+='<tr><td colspan="8" class="external-empty-stop"><strong>No delivery stops on '+self.esc(g.day)+'</strong>Use the Add delivery stop button in the route bar to add the first customer or delivery location.</td></tr>';
      }

      g.rows.forEach(function(c,i){
        var cj=JSON.stringify(c).replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
        var ov = c.overlay || null;
        var rowCls = '', badge = '', meta = '';
        if (ov && ov.kind === 'EXTERNAL_DELIVERY') {
          rowCls = ' class="ovl-row-external"';
          badge = '<span class="ovl-badge ovl-external" title="Delivery-only stop on an External Route. It is never added to Customers or production."><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M3 6h13v11H3z"/><path d="M16 9h4l2 3v5h-2"/><circle cx="7" cy="18" r="2"/><circle cx="18" cy="18" r="2"/></svg>External</span>';
          if (ov.expiresOn) meta += '<div class="ovl-meta">Remove after <span class="ovl-expires">'+self.esc(ov.expiresOn)+'</span></div>';
          if (ov.reason) meta += '<div class="ovl-meta">'+self.esc(ov.reason)+'</div>';
        } else if (ov && (ov.kind === 'COLLECTION' || ov.kind === 'TEMP_STOP')) {
          rowCls = ' class="ovl-row-collection"';
          badge = '<span class="ovl-badge ovl-collection" title="Collection-only stop. Visible in Routes and to the driver; never added to Customers or production."><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M3 6h13v11H3z"/><path d="M16 9h4l2 3v5h-2"/><circle cx="7" cy="18" r="2"/><circle cx="18" cy="18" r="2"/></svg>Collection</span>';
          if (ov.expiresOn) meta += '<div class="ovl-meta">Remove after <span class="ovl-expires">'+self.esc(ov.expiresOn)+'</span></div>';
          if (ov.reason)    meta += '<div class="ovl-meta">'+self.esc(ov.reason)+'</div>';
        } else if (ov && ov.kind === 'TEMP_MOVE') {
          rowCls = ' class="ovl-row-temp"';
          badge = '<span class="ovl-badge ovl-temp-move" title="Temporarily moved here for a delivery-time test. Production unchanged."><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><polyline points="17 1 21 5 17 9"/><path d="M3 11V9a4 4 0 0 1 4-4h14"/><polyline points="7 23 3 19 7 15"/><path d="M21 13v2a4 4 0 0 1-4 4H3"/></svg>Test move</span>';
          meta += '<div class="ovl-meta">From Route '+self.esc(ov.fromRoute||'?')+(ov.fromDay?(' · '+self.esc(ov.fromDay)):'')+'</div>';
          if (ov.expiresOn) meta += '<div class="ovl-meta">Expires <span class="ovl-expires">'+self.esc(ov.expiresOn)+'</span></div>';
          if (ov.reason)    meta += '<div class="ovl-meta">'+self.esc(ov.reason)+'</div>';
        }
        var actions = '';
        if (ov && ov.kind === 'EXTERNAL_DELIVERY') {
          actions =
             '<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" title="Move delivery up" onclick="App.moveDelivery(\''+self.esc(c.rowIndex)+'\',-1)"'+(i===0?' disabled':'')+'>&uarr;</button>'
            +'<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" title="Move delivery down" onclick="App.moveDelivery(\''+self.esc(c.rowIndex)+'\',1)"'+(i===g.rows.length-1?' disabled':'')+'>&darr;</button>'
            +'<button class="ovl-act-btn ovl-act-external" title="Edit external delivery stop" onclick=\'App.openExternalDelivery('+cj+')\'>Edit</button>'
            +'<button class="ovl-act-btn ovl-act-cancel" title="Remove external delivery stop" onclick="App.cancelOverlay(\''+self.esc(ov.overlayId)+'\')">✕ Remove</button>';
        } else if (ov && (ov.kind === 'COLLECTION' || ov.kind === 'TEMP_STOP')) {
          actions =
             '<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" title="Move collection up" onclick="App.moveDelivery(\''+self.esc(c.rowIndex)+'\',-1)"'+(i===0?' disabled':'')+'>&uarr;</button>'
            +'<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" title="Move collection down" onclick="App.moveDelivery(\''+self.esc(c.rowIndex)+'\',1)"'+(i===g.rows.length-1?' disabled':'')+'>&darr;</button>'
            +'<button class="ovl-act-btn ovl-act-edit" title="Edit collection" onclick=\'App.openCollection('+cj+')\'>Edit</button>'
            +'<button class="ovl-act-btn ovl-act-cancel" title="Remove collection from this route" onclick="App.cancelOverlay(\''+self.esc(ov.overlayId)+'\')">✕ Remove</button>';
        } else if (ov && ov.kind === 'TEMP_MOVE') {
          actions = '<button class="ovl-act-btn ovl-act-cancel" title="Cancel this temporary move" onclick="App.cancelOverlay(\''+self.esc(ov.overlayId)+'\')">✕ Cancel</button>';
        } else {
          actions =
             '<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" title="Move up" onclick="App.moveDelivery(\''+c.rowIndex+'\',-1)"'+(i===0?' disabled':'')+'>&uarr;</button>'
            +'<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" title="Move down" onclick="App.moveDelivery(\''+c.rowIndex+'\',1)"'+(i===g.rows.length-1?' disabled':'')+'>&darr;</button>'
            +'<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick=\'App.openCustomerModal('+cj+')\'>Edit</button>'
            +'<button class="ovl-act-btn ovl-act-move" title="Temporarily move to another route (delivery test). Production stays the same." onclick=\'App.openTempMove('+cj+')\'>⇄ Test</button>'
            +'<button class="ovl-act-btn ovl-act-hide" title="Hide from this route/day (delivery only)" onclick=\'App.hideStop('+cj+')\'>⦸ Hide</button>'
            +'<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" style="color:var(--red)" onclick="App.confirmDeleteCustomer(\''+self.esc(c.route)+'\',\''+self.esc(c.day)+'\','+(c.order||0)+',\''+self.esc(String(c.customer).replace(/'/g,"\\'"))+'\')">Del</button>';
        }
        html+='<tr'+rowCls+'>'
          +'<td style="text-align:center;font-weight:700;color:var(--txt2)">'+(c.order||(i+1))+'</td>'
          +'<td><strong>'+self.esc(c.customer)+'</strong>'+badge+meta+'</td>'
          +'<td style="font-size:12px;white-space:nowrap">'+self.esc(c.time||'')+'</td>'
          +'<td style="font-size:12px">'+self.esc(c.trolleyClothes||'')+'</td>'
          +'<td style="font-size:12px">'+self.esc(c.trolleyMop||'')+'</td>'
          +'<td style="font-size:12px">'+self.esc(c.eircode||'')+'</td>'
          +'<td style="text-align:right;font-size:12px">'+(c.kg?Math.round(c.kg):'')+'</td>'
          +'<td style="white-space:nowrap">'+actions+'</td></tr>';
      });
      var hiddenForGroup = (self._overlays||[]).filter(function(o){
        return o.Kind==='HIDE'
          && String(self._cleanRoute(o.FromRoute||o.Route)).toLowerCase()===String(g.route).toLowerCase()
          && String((o.FromDay||'')).toLowerCase().slice(0,3)===String(g.day||'').toLowerCase().slice(0,3);
      });
      hiddenForGroup.forEach(function(o){
        html+='<tr class="ovl-row-hidden">'
          +'<td style="text-align:center">—</td>'
          +'<td><strong>'+self.esc(o.CustomerName||o.CustomerID)+'</strong>'
            +'<span class="ovl-badge ovl-hidden" title="Hidden from this route/day (delivery only)"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M17.94 17.94A10.07 10.07 0 0 1 12 20c-7 0-11-8-11-8a18.45 18.45 0 0 1 5.06-5.94M9.9 4.24A9.12 9.12 0 0 1 12 4c7 0 11 8 11 8a18.5 18.5 0 0 1-2.16 3.19m-6.72-1.07a3 3 0 1 1-4.24-4.24"/><line x1="1" y1="1" x2="23" y2="23"/></svg>Hidden</span>'
            +(o.Reason?('<div class="ovl-meta">'+self.esc(o.Reason)+'</div>'):'')
            +(o.ExpiresOn?('<div class="ovl-meta">Expires <span class="ovl-expires">'+self.esc(o.ExpiresOn)+'</span></div>'):'')
          +'</td>'
          +'<td colspan="4" style="font-size:12px;color:var(--txt2)">Hidden from delivery</td>'
          +'<td></td>'
          +'<td style="white-space:nowrap"><button class="ovl-act-btn ovl-act-unhide" onclick="App.cancelOverlay(\''+self.esc(o.OverlayID)+'\')">↺ Unhide</button></td>'
          +'</tr>';
      });
      html+='</tbody></table></div></div>';
    });
    host.innerHTML=html;
  },

  toggleCustomerRoute(key) {
    this.customerCollapsed[key] = this.customerCollapsed[key] === false ? true : false;
    this._renderPlanner();
  },

  editInlineRouteStat(el) {
    if (!el || el.querySelector('input')) return;
    var field = el.getAttribute('data-field');
    var value = el.getAttribute('data-value') || '';
    el.setAttribute('data-original', value);
    el.innerHTML = '<input class="cust-metric-input" type="number" min="0" step="'+(field==='hours'?'0.25':'0.1')+'" value="'+this.esc(value)+'" onblur="App.commitInlineRouteStat(this)" onkeydown="App.keyInlineRouteStat(event,this)"><span class="l">'+(field==='hours'?'Hours':'KM')+'</span>';
    var input = el.querySelector('input');
    setTimeout(function(){ input.focus(); input.select(); }, 0);
  },

  keyInlineRouteStat(ev, input) {
    if (ev.key === 'Enter') { ev.preventDefault(); input.blur(); }
    if (ev.key === 'Escape') {
      ev.preventDefault();
      var box = input.closest('.cust-metric');
      var old = box ? (box.getAttribute('data-original') || '') : '';
      input.value = old;
      input.blur();
    }
  },

  commitInlineRouteStat(input) {
    var self=this;
    var box = input && input.closest('.cust-metric');
    if (!box) return;
    var route = box.getAttribute('data-route');
    var day   = box.getAttribute('data-day');
    var key   = box.getAttribute('data-key');
    var field = box.getAttribute('data-field');
    var original = box.getAttribute('data-original') || '';
    var raw = String(input.value || '').trim();
    var next = raw === '' ? 0 : parseFloat(raw);
    if (isNaN(next)) next = 0;
    if (String(next) === String(original) || (raw === '' && original === '')) {
      box.setAttribute('data-value', raw === '' ? '' : next);
      box.innerHTML = '<span class="v">'+(raw === '' ? '—' : self.esc(next))+'</span><span class="l">'+(field==='hours'?'Hours':'KM')+'</span>';
      return;
    }
    var current = (self.D.routes||[]).find(function(rt){
      return String(rt.route)===String(route) && String(rt.day||'').toLowerCase()===String(day||'').toLowerCase();
    }) || {};
    var stats = {
      km: field === 'km' ? next : (parseFloat(current.km) || 0),
      hours: field === 'hours' ? next : (parseFloat(current.hours) || 0)
    };
    box.classList.add('saving');
    box.innerHTML = '<span class="v">Saving…</span><span class="l">'+(field==='hours'?'Hours':'KM')+'</span>';
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(function(r){
        if(!r || !r.ok){
          box.classList.remove('saving');
          box.setAttribute('data-value', original);
          box.innerHTML = '<span class="v">'+(original === '' ? '—' : self.esc(original))+'</span><span class="l">'+(field==='hours'?'Hours':'KM')+'</span>';
          self.toast('Error: ' + ((r&&r.msg)||'Could not save'), 'e');
          return;
        }
        (self.D.routes||[]).forEach(function(rt){
          if(String(rt.route)===String(route) && String(rt.day||'').toLowerCase()===String(day||'').toLowerCase()){
            rt.km = stats.km; rt.hours = stats.hours;
          }
        });
        self.customerCollapsed[key] = true;
        self.toast((field==='hours'?'Hours':'KM') + ' saved.', 's');
        self._renderPlanner();
      })
      .withFailureHandler(function(e){
        box.classList.remove('saving');
        box.setAttribute('data-value', original);
        box.innerHTML = '<span class="v">'+(original === '' ? '—' : self.esc(original))+'</span><span class="l">'+(field==='hours'?'Hours':'KM')+'</span>';
        self.toast(e.message, 'e');
      })
      .saveRouteStats(route, day, stats);
  },

  saveCustomerRouteStats(btn) {
    // Backward-compatible wrapper kept in case an older cached DOM still calls this.
    return;
  },

  moveDelivery(rowIndex, dir) {
    var self=this;
    var c=(this.PL||[]).find(function(x){return String(x.rowIndex)===String(rowIndex);});
    if(!c) return;

    // Use the exact same missing-order rule as the route renderer: blank/zero
    // orders sit at the end until the first reorder normalises every stop to 1..N.
    var peers=(this.PL||[]).filter(function(x){
      return String(x.route)===String(c.route) && String(x.day).toLowerCase()===String(c.day).toLowerCase();
    }).sort(function(a,b){
      var ao=(parseInt(a.order,10)>0)?parseInt(a.order,10):9999;
      var bo=(parseInt(b.order,10)>0)?parseInt(b.order,10):9999;
      if(ao!==bo) return ao-bo;
      return String(a.customer||'').localeCompare(String(b.customer||''));
    });

    var i=peers.indexOf(c), j=i+dir;
    if(j<0||j>=peers.length) return;

    // Keep a rollback snapshot because the UI updates before the server call.
    var previous=peers.map(function(p){return {rowIndex:String(p.rowIndex),order:p.order};});
    var moved=peers.splice(i,1)[0];
    peers.splice(j,0,moved);
    peers.forEach(function(p,idx){p.order=idx+1;});
    this._renderPlanner();

    var items=peers.map(function(p){
      var ov=p.overlay||null;
      return {
        rowIndex:       p.rowIndex,
        order:          p.order,
        overlayId:      ov&&ov.overlayId||'',
        overlayKind:    ov&&ov.kind||'',
        customerId:     p.customerId||'',
        customerName:   p.customer||'',
        fromRoute:      ov&&ov.fromRoute||'',
        fromDay:        ov&&ov.fromDay||'',
        route:          p.route||'',
        deliveryDay:    p.day||'',
        deliveryTime:   p.time||'',
        routeColor:     p.colour||'',
        displayName:    p.displayName||'',
        eircode:        p.eircode||'',
        kgEstimated:    p.kg||'',
        qtyClothes:     p.trolleyClothes||'',
        trolleyMop:     p.trolleyMop||'',
        notes:          p.notes||'',
        expiresOn:      ov&&ov.expiresOn||'',
        reason:         ov&&ov.reason||''
      };
    });

    function rollback(msg){
      previous.forEach(function(old){
        var row=(self.PL||[]).find(function(x){return String(x.rowIndex)===old.rowIndex;});
        if(row) row.order=old.order;
      });
      self._renderPlanner();
      self.toast(msg||'Could not save order','e');
    }

    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){
      if(!r||!r.ok){ rollback((r&&r.msg)||'Could not save order'); return; }
      self.toast('Order saved','s');
      if (typeof window.invalidateDistributionCustomerCache === 'function') window.invalidateDistributionCustomerCache();
    }).withFailureHandler(function(e){ rollback(e.message); }).saveDeliveryRouteOrder(items);
  },

  /* ============================================================
   *  DELIVERY OVERLAY — distribution-only changes (supervisor)
   *  COLLECTION · TEMP_MOVE · HIDE / UNHIDE
   *  Collection rows never enter the Customers/production planner. All changes are audited.
   * ============================================================ */
  _cleanRoute(v){ return String(v==null?'':v).trim().replace(/\.0+$/,''); },

  _fillOverlayRouteSelect(selId, mode, selectedRoute){
    var self=this, seen={}, html='';
    var externalOnly=String(mode||'').toUpperCase()==='EXTERNAL_DELIVERY';
    (this.D.routes||[]).forEach(function(r){
      if(!r.route) return;
      if(externalOnly && String(r.routeType||'STANDARD').toUpperCase()!=='EXTERNAL') return;
      if(externalOnly && r.active===false && String(r.route)!==String(selectedRoute||'')) return;
      var key=String(r.route);
      if(!seen[key]){seen[key]=1; html+='<option value="'+self.esc(r.route)+'">'+self.esc(r.displayName||('Route '+r.route))+(externalOnly?' — External':'')+'</option>';}
    });
    var el=document.getElementById(selId); if(el) el.innerHTML='<option value="" disabled selected>— Select route —</option>'+html;
  },

  _collectionRouteColours(){
    var seen={}, list=[];
    (this.D.routes||[]).forEach(function(r){
      var colour=String(r.colour||'').trim();
      if(!/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(colour)) return;
      var key=colour.toLowerCase();
      var routeName=String(r.displayName||('Route '+(r.route||''))).trim();
      if(!seen[key]){
        seen[key]={ colour:colour, routes:[] };
        list.push(seen[key]);
      }
      if(routeName && seen[key].routes.indexOf(routeName)<0) seen[key].routes.push(routeName);
    });
    return list;
  },

  _routeColourForCollection(route){
    route=this._cleanRoute(route);
    var match=(this.D.routes||[]).find(function(r){
      return String(r.route||'').trim().replace(/\.0+$/,'')===route && /^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(String(r.colour||'').trim());
    });
    return match ? String(match.colour).trim() : '';
  },

  _fillCollectionColourSelect(selectedColour, route){
    var el=document.getElementById('col-colour');
    if(!el) return;
    var self=this, colours=this._collectionRouteColours();
    if(!colours.length) colours=[{colour:'#94a3b8',routes:['Default route colour']}];
    var routeColour=this._routeColourForCollection(route||'');
    var selected=String(selectedColour||'').trim().toLowerCase();
    var selectedEntry=colours.find(function(x){return x.colour.toLowerCase()===selected;});
    var routeEntry=colours.find(function(x){return x.colour.toLowerCase()===String(routeColour||'').toLowerCase();});
    var preferred=selectedEntry ? selectedEntry.colour : (routeEntry ? routeEntry.colour : colours[0].colour);
    el.innerHTML=colours.map(function(x){
      var names=x.routes.slice(0,3).join(' / ')+(x.routes.length>3?' +'+(x.routes.length-3):'');
      return '<option value="'+self.esc(x.colour)+'">'+self.esc(names)+' — '+self.esc(x.colour.toUpperCase())+'</option>';
    }).join('');
    el.value=preferred;
    if(!el.value && colours.length) el.value=colours[0].colour;
    this.updateCollectionColourPreview();
  },

  onCollectionRouteChange(){
    var route=(document.getElementById('col-route')||{}).value||'';
    this._fillCollectionColourSelect('', route);
    if((this._overlayStopMode||'')==='EXTERNAL_DELIVERY' && !this._editingCollection) this._renderExternalStopDays(route,[]);
  },

  updateCollectionColourPreview(){
    var el=document.getElementById('col-colour');
    var preview=document.getElementById('col-colour-preview');
    if(preview) preview.style.background=(el&&el.value)||'#94a3b8';
  },

  _loadCollectionCustomerOptions(){
    var self=this;
    var status=document.getElementById('col-customer-status');
    var results=document.getElementById('col-customer-results');
    self._collectionCustomerOptions=[];
    if(status) status.textContent='Loading existing customers…';
    if(results){ results.innerHTML=''; results.classList.remove('open'); }

    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){
      var rows=(res&&res.ok&&Array.isArray(res.customers))?res.customers:[];
      var seen={};
      self._collectionCustomerOptions=rows.filter(function(c){
        var key=String(c.name||'').trim().toLowerCase();
        if(!key||seen[key]) return false;
        seen[key]=1;
        return true;
      }).sort(function(a,b){ return String(a.name||'').localeCompare(String(b.name||'')); });

      if(status){
        status.textContent=self._collectionCustomerOptions.length
          ? self._collectionCustomerOptions.length+' existing customers available. Type to filter, or enter a new location.'
          : 'No existing customers were returned. You can still type a new collection location.';
      }
      var input=document.getElementById('col-name');
      if(input===document.activeElement) self._renderCollectionCustomerSuggestions(input.value||'');
    }).withFailureHandler(function(e){
      self._collectionCustomerOptions=[];
      if(status) status.textContent='Could not load the customer list. You can still type a new collection location.';
      if(results){ results.innerHTML=''; results.classList.remove('open'); }
      self.toast('Customer list error: '+String(e&&e.message||e||'Unknown error'),'e');
    }).getCollectionCustomerOptions();
  },

  _renderCollectionCustomerSuggestions(query){
    var host=document.getElementById('col-customer-results');
    if(!host) return;
    var self=this;
    var q=String(query||'').trim().toLowerCase();
    var rows=(this._collectionCustomerOptions||[]).map(function(c,index){ return {c:c,index:index}; });
    if(q){
      rows=rows.filter(function(x){
        var name=String(x.c.name||'').toLowerCase();
        var eir=String(x.c.eircode||'').toLowerCase();
        return name.indexOf(q)>=0 || eir.indexOf(q)>=0;
      }).sort(function(a,b){
        var an=String(a.c.name||'').toLowerCase(), bn=String(b.c.name||'').toLowerCase();
        var ap=an.indexOf(q)===0?0:1, bp=bn.indexOf(q)===0?0:1;
        return ap-bp || an.localeCompare(bn);
      });
    }
    rows=rows.slice(0,15);

    if(!(this._collectionCustomerOptions||[]).length){
      host.innerHTML='<div class="col-customer-empty">Customer list is still loading or no customers were returned. You may type a new name.</div>';
      host.classList.add('open');
      return;
    }
    if(!rows.length){
      host.innerHTML='<div class="col-customer-empty">No existing customer matches “'+this.esc(query||'')+'”. Keep this text to create a one-off collection.</div>';
      host.classList.add('open');
      return;
    }
    host.innerHTML=rows.map(function(x){
      var c=x.c;
      return '<button type="button" class="col-customer-option" data-index="'+x.index+'" onmousedown="event.preventDefault()" onclick="App.selectCollectionCustomer('+x.index+')">'
        +'<span class="col-customer-option-name">'+self.esc(c.name||'')+'</span>'
        +(c.eircode?'<span class="col-customer-option-eir">'+self.esc(c.eircode)+'</span>':'')
        +'</button>';
    }).join('');
