    host.classList.add('open');
  },

  showCollectionCustomerSuggestions(){
    var input=document.getElementById('col-name');
    this._renderCollectionCustomerSuggestions(input?input.value:'');
  },

  hideCollectionCustomerSuggestions(delay){
    var host=document.getElementById('col-customer-results');
    if(!host) return;
    window.clearTimeout(this._collectionHideTimer);
    this._collectionHideTimer=window.setTimeout(function(){ host.classList.remove('open'); }, Number(delay)||0);
  },

  selectCollectionCustomer(index){
    var c=(this._collectionCustomerOptions||[])[Number(index)];
    if(!c) return;
    var input=document.getElementById('col-name');
    var eir=document.getElementById('col-eircode');
    if(input) input.value=String(c.name||'');
    this._selectedCollectionCustomerId=String(c.customerId||'');
    if(eir && !String(eir.value||'').trim() && c.eircode) eir.value=String(c.eircode);
    this.hideCollectionCustomerSuggestions(0);
    if(input) input.focus();
  },

  onCollectionNameInput(){
    var input=document.getElementById('col-name');
    var raw=String((input||{}).value||'').trim();
    var name=raw.toLowerCase();
    var match=(this._collectionCustomerOptions||[]).find(function(c){ return String(c.name||'').trim().toLowerCase()===name; });
    this._selectedCollectionCustomerId=match ? String(match.customerId||'') : '';
    var eir=document.getElementById('col-eircode');
    if(match&&eir&&!String(eir.value||'').trim()&&match.eircode) eir.value=match.eircode;
    this._renderCollectionCustomerSuggestions(raw);
  },

  onCollectionNameKeydown(ev){
    if(!ev) return;
    if(ev.key==='Escape') this.hideCollectionCustomerSuggestions(0);
    if(ev.key==='ArrowDown'){
      var first=document.querySelector('#col-customer-results .col-customer-option');
      if(first){ ev.preventDefault(); first.focus(); }
    }
  },


  _externalRouteDays(route){
    const wanted=String(route||'');
    const order=this._routeDayNames();
    const seen={};
    (this.D.routes||[]).forEach(r=>{
      if(String(r.route)===wanted && String(r.routeType||'STANDARD').toUpperCase()==='EXTERNAL' && r.day) seen[String(r.day).toLowerCase()]=String(r.day);
    });
    return order.filter(d=>seen[d.toLowerCase()]);
  },

  _renderExternalStopDays(route, selectedDays){
    const host=document.getElementById('col-ext-day-checks'); if(!host) return;
    const available=this._externalRouteDays(route);
    const selected=new Set((selectedDays&&selectedDays.length?selectedDays:available).map(x=>String(x).toLowerCase()));
    host.innerHTML=available.map(d=>'<label class="cust-day-chip"><input type="checkbox" value="'+this.esc(d)+'" '+(selected.has(d.toLowerCase())?'checked':'')+' onchange="App.updateExternalStopDayCount()">'+this.esc(d.slice(0,3))+'</label>').join('');
    this.updateExternalStopDayCount();
  },

  _getExternalStopDays(){ return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#col-ext-day-checks input:checked')).map(x=>x.value); },

  updateExternalStopDayCount(){
    const n=this._getExternalStopDays().length, el=document.getElementById('col-day-count');
    if(el) el.textContent=n+' selected';
  },

  setExternalStopDayPreset(preset){
    document.querySelectorAll('#col-ext-day-checks input').forEach(cb=>{cb.checked=preset==='ALL';});
    this.updateExternalStopDayCount();
  },

  // ── Distribution-only stop: Collection or External Delivery ──
  _configureDistributionStopModal(mode, editing){
    const external = mode === 'EXTERNAL_DELIVERY';
    document.getElementById('collection-modal-title').textContent = editing ? (external?'Edit external delivery':'Edit collection') : (external?'Add external delivery':'Add collection');
    document.getElementById('collection-modal-subtitle').textContent = external ? '— External route, outside production' : '— Routes and driver only';
    const info=document.getElementById('collection-modal-info');
    info.style.background=external?'#f5f3ff':'#fff7ed'; info.style.borderColor=external?'#c4b5fd':'#fdba74'; info.style.color=external?'#5b21b6':'#9a3412';
    info.textContent=external
      ? 'This is a delivery stop on an External Route. It can use an existing customer or a new location, appears in the Roster/driver route, and never enters production.'
      : 'Use this when the driver must collect from a location without making a delivery. It appears in Routes and in the driver route, but is never added to Customers or production.';
    document.getElementById('col-name-label').textContent=external?'Customer / delivery location':'Customer / collection name';
    document.getElementById('col-day-label').textContent=external?'Delivery day':'Collection day';
    document.getElementById('col-notes-label').textContent=external?'Delivery notes':'Driver notes';
    const multi=external&&!editing;
    document.getElementById('col-single-day-group').style.display=multi?'none':'';
    document.getElementById('col-multi-days-group').style.display=multi?'':'none';
    document.getElementById('col-save-btn').textContent=editing ? (external?'Save delivery stop':'Save collection') : (external?'Add delivery stop(s)':'Add collection');
  },

  openCollection(cust, forcedMode, presetRoute){
    var c = cust ? ((typeof cust === 'string') ? JSON.parse(cust) : cust) : null;
    var detected = c && c.overlay && c.overlay.kind === 'EXTERNAL_DELIVERY' ? 'EXTERNAL_DELIVERY' : 'COLLECTION';
    var mode = forcedMode || detected;
    var editing = !!(c && c.overlay && c.overlay.overlayId);
    this._editingCollection = editing ? c : null;
    this._overlayStopMode = mode;
    this._selectedCollectionCustomerId = editing ? String(c.customerId||'') : '';
    this._fillOverlayRouteSelect('col-route', mode, editing ? c.route : presetRoute);
    this._loadCollectionCustomerOptions();

    ['col-name','col-eircode','col-time','col-kg','col-order','col-clothes','col-mop','col-notes','col-expires'].forEach(function(id){
      var e=document.getElementById(id); if(e) e.value='';
    });
    this._configureDistributionStopModal(mode, editing);

    if(editing){
      document.getElementById('col-name').value = c.customer || '';
      document.getElementById('col-route').value = c.route || '';
      document.getElementById('col-day').value = c.day || 'Monday';
      document.getElementById('col-eircode').value = c.eircode || '';
      document.getElementById('col-time').value = c.time || '';
      document.getElementById('col-kg').value = c.kg || '';
      document.getElementById('col-order').value = c.order || '';
      document.getElementById('col-clothes').value = c.trolleyClothes || '';
      document.getElementById('col-mop').value = c.trolleyMop || '';
      document.getElementById('col-notes').value = c.notes || '';
      document.getElementById('col-expires').value = (c.overlay && c.overlay.expiresOn) || '';
      this._fillCollectionColourSelect(c.colour || '', c.route || '');
    } else {
      var routeFilter=presetRoute || (document.getElementById('route-route-filter')||{}).value || '';
      var dayFilter=(document.getElementById('route-day-filter')||{}).value || '';
      if(routeFilter) document.getElementById('col-route').value=routeFilter;
      if(dayFilter) document.getElementById('col-day').value=dayFilter;
      this._fillCollectionColourSelect('', routeFilter || '');
      if(mode==='EXTERNAL_DELIVERY') this._renderExternalStopDays(routeFilter||'', this._pendingExternalStopDays||[]);
    }
    this._pendingExternalStopDays=[];
    this.openModal('collection-modal');
  },

  openExternalDelivery(routeOrStop){
    var x=routeOrStop ? ((typeof routeOrStop==='string')?JSON.parse(routeOrStop):routeOrStop) : null;
    if(x && x.overlay) return this.openCollection(x,'EXTERNAL_DELIVERY',x.route||'');
    this._pendingExternalStopDays=(x&&Array.isArray(x.presetDays)&&x.presetDays.length)?x.presetDays.slice():(x&&x.day?[x.day]:[]);
    return this.openCollection(null,'EXTERNAL_DELIVERY',x && x.route ? x.route : String(x||''));
  },

  saveCollection(){
    var self=this, current=this._editingCollection;
    var mode=this._overlayStopMode||'COLLECTION';
    var route=document.getElementById('col-route').value;
    var routeMeta=(this.D.routes||[]).find(r=>String(r.route)===String(route))||{};
    var payload={
      overlayId: current && current.overlay ? current.overlay.overlayId : '',
      customerId:this._selectedCollectionCustomerId||'',
      name:       (document.getElementById('col-name').value||'').trim(),
      route:      route,
      day:        document.getElementById('col-day').value,
      days:       (mode==='EXTERNAL_DELIVERY' && !current) ? this._getExternalStopDays() : [],
      eircode:    (document.getElementById('col-eircode').value||'').trim(),
      time:       (document.getElementById('col-time').value||'').trim(),
      kg:         (document.getElementById('col-kg').value||'').trim(),
      order:      parseInt(document.getElementById('col-order').value)||'',
      trolleyClothes:(document.getElementById('col-clothes').value||'').trim(),
      trolleyMop: (document.getElementById('col-mop').value||'').trim(),
      colour:     document.getElementById('col-colour').value,
      displayName:routeMeta.displayName||'',
      notes:      (document.getElementById('col-notes').value||'').trim(),
      expiresOn:  (document.getElementById('col-expires').value||'').trim()
    };
    if(!payload.name){ this.toast('Enter the customer / location name','e'); return; }
    if(!payload.route){ this.toast('Route is required','e'); return; }
    if(mode==='EXTERNAL_DELIVERY' && String(routeMeta.routeType||'STANDARD')!=='EXTERNAL'){ this.toast('External delivery stops must use an External Route.','e'); return; }
    if(mode==='EXTERNAL_DELIVERY' && !current && !payload.days.length){ this.toast('Select at least one delivery day.','e'); return; }
    this._runBtn(document.getElementById('col-save-btn'),'Saving…',function(done){
      var runner=google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){ done(r&&r.ok);
        if(r&&r.ok){
          const createdCount=(r&&r.createdCount)||((payload.days&&payload.days.length)||1);
          self.toast(payload.overlayId ? (mode==='EXTERNAL_DELIVERY'?'Delivery stop updated':'Collection updated') : (mode==='EXTERNAL_DELIVERY'?(createdCount+' external delivery stop'+(createdCount===1?'':'s')+' added'):'Collection added'),'s');
          self.closeModal('collection-modal');
          self._editingCollection=null; self._overlayStopMode='COLLECTION';
          self.renderCustomers(true);
        } else self.toast('Error: '+((r&&r.msg)||''),'e');
      }).withFailureHandler(function(e){ done(false); self.toast(e.message,'e'); });
      if(mode==='EXTERNAL_DELIVERY' && !payload.overlayId) runner.addExternalDeliveryStopsBatch(payload);
      else if(mode==='EXTERNAL_DELIVERY') runner.addExternalDeliveryStop(payload);
      else runner.addCollection(payload);
    });
  },

  // ── Temporarily move a customer to another route (delivery test) ──
  openTempMove(cust){
    var c = (typeof cust === 'string') ? JSON.parse(cust) : cust;
    this._tempMoveCust = c;
    this._fillOverlayRouteSelect('tm-route');
    document.getElementById('tm-who').textContent = c.customer + ' · currently Route '+c.route+' · '+c.day;
    document.getElementById('tm-day').value = c.day || 'Monday';
    ['tm-time','tm-order','tm-expires','tm-reason'].forEach(function(id){ var e=document.getElementById(id); if(e) e.value=''; });
    document.getElementById('tm-time').value = c.time || '';
    this.openModal('tempmove-modal');
  },
  saveTempMove(){
    var self=this, c=this._tempMoveCust||{};
    var payload={
      customerId:   c.customerId,
      customerName: c.customer,
      fromRoute:    c.route,
      fromDay:      c.day,
      route:        document.getElementById('tm-route').value,
      day:          document.getElementById('tm-day').value,
      time:         (document.getElementById('tm-time').value||'').trim(),
      order:        parseInt(document.getElementById('tm-order').value)||'',
      expiresOn:    (document.getElementById('tm-expires').value||'').trim(),
      reason:       (document.getElementById('tm-reason').value||'').trim()
    };
    if(!c.customerId){ this.toast('This stop has no customer id and cannot be moved','e'); return; }
    if(!payload.route){ this.toast('Pick a target route','e'); return; }
    if(String(payload.route)===String(c.route) && String(payload.day).toLowerCase()===String(c.day).toLowerCase()){
      this.toast('Target is the same as the current route/day','e'); return;
    }
    this._runBtn(document.getElementById('tm-save-btn'),'Saving…',function(done){
      google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){ done(r&&r.ok);
        if(r&&r.ok){ self.toast('Customer temporarily moved','s'); self.closeModal('tempmove-modal'); self.renderCustomers(true); }
        else self.toast('Error: '+((r&&r.msg)||''),'e');
      }).withFailureHandler(function(e){ done(false); self.toast(e.message,'e'); }).moveCustomerTemporarily(payload);
    });
  },

  // ── Hide a customer on a route/day (delivery only) ──
  hideStop(cust){
    var self=this;
    var c = (typeof cust === 'string') ? JSON.parse(cust) : cust;
    if(!c.customerId){ this.toast('This stop has no customer id and cannot be hidden','e'); return; }
    var reason = window.prompt('Hide "'+c.customer+'" from Route '+c.route+' on '+c.day+' (delivery only).\n\nOptional note/reason:','');
    if(reason===null) return; // cancelled
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){
      if(r&&r.ok){ self.toast('Customer hidden from delivery','s'); self.renderCustomers(true); }
      else self.toast('Error: '+((r&&r.msg)||''),'e');
    }).withFailureHandler(function(e){ self.toast(e.message,'e'); })
      .hideCustomerOnDay({ customerId:c.customerId, customerName:c.customer, fromRoute:c.route, fromDay:c.day, reason:reason });
  },

  // ── Cancel any overlay (unhide / remove collection / cancel temp move) ──
  cancelOverlay(overlayId){
    var self=this;
    if(!overlayId) return;
    if(!window.confirm('Remove this temporary change? The delivery view returns to normal.')) return;
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){
      if(r&&r.ok){ self.toast('Temporary change removed','s'); self.renderCustomers(true); }
      else self.toast('Error: '+((r&&r.msg)||''),'e');
    }).withFailureHandler(function(e){ self.toast(e.message,'e'); })
      .removeDeliveryOverlay(overlayId, false);
  },

  openCustomerModal(cust) {
    document.getElementById('cust-modal-title').textContent = cust ? 'Edit Stop' : 'New Stop';
    document.getElementById('cm-isnew').value     = cust ? '' : '1';
    document.getElementById('cm-orig-route').value = cust ? cust.route : '';
    document.getElementById('cm-orig-day').value   = cust ? cust.day   : '';
    document.getElementById('cm-orig-order').value = cust ? cust.order : '';

    // ── Unique routes for dropdown (deduplicated by route number) ──
    const seenRoutes = {};
    (this.D.routes||[]).forEach(r => {
      if (!seenRoutes[r.route]) seenRoutes[r.route] = r;
    });
    const uniqueRoutes = Object.values(seenRoutes);
    const cmSel = document.getElementById('cm-route');
    // For new stops: start with empty placeholder so user must choose
    const placeholder = cust ? '' : '<option value="" disabled selected>— Select a route —</option>';
    cmSel.innerHTML = placeholder + uniqueRoutes.map(r => {
      const sel = cust && cust.route === r.route ? ' selected' : '';
      return '<option value="' + this.esc(r.route) + '"' + sel + '>' + this.esc(r.displayName || r.route) + '</option>';
    }).join('');

    // ── Auto-suggest next order number ──
    const selRoute = cmSel.value;
    const selDay   = cust ? cust.day : 'Monday';
    if (!cust) {
      document.getElementById('cm-order').value = '';  // blank until route chosen
    } else {
      document.getElementById('cm-order').value = cust.order;
    }

    document.getElementById('cm-day').value        = cust ? cust.day : 'Monday';
    document.getElementById('cm-time').value       = cust ? cust.time : '';
    document.getElementById('cm-customer').value   = cust ? cust.customer : '';
    document.getElementById('cm-stops').value      = cust ? cust.stops : '1';
    document.getElementById('cm-eircode').value    = cust ? cust.eircode : '';
    document.getElementById('cm-kg').value         = cust ? cust.kg : '';
    document.getElementById('cm-tr-clothes').value = cust ? cust.trolleyClothes : '';
    document.getElementById('cm-tr-mop').value     = cust ? cust.trolleyMop : '';
    document.getElementById('cm-notes').value      = cust ? cust.notes : '';

    // ── Colour: use existing route colour, or customer colour ──
    let colVal = '#94a3b8';
    if (cust && cust.colour) {
      colVal = cust.colour;
    } else if (selRoute) {
      const routeObj = uniqueRoutes.find(r => r.route === selRoute);
      if (routeObj && routeObj.colour) colVal = routeObj.colour;
    }
    document.getElementById('cm-colour').value = colVal;
    document.getElementById('cm-colour-picker').value = /^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(colVal) ? colVal : '#94a3b8';

    // ── Display name: auto-fill from route (edit only if cust; blank for new) ──
    const routeObj2 = selRoute ? uniqueRoutes.find(r => r.route === selRoute) : null;
    document.getElementById('cm-display-name').value = cust
      ? (cust.displayName || (routeObj2 ? routeObj2.displayName : ''))
      : '';

    // ── Colour swatches from existing routes ──
    this._renderColourSwatches();

    this.openModal('customer-modal');
    setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('cm-customer').focus(), 100);
  },

  // Called when route dropdown changes in customer modal
  onCustRouteChange() {
    const sel = document.getElementById('cm-route').value;
    const seenRoutes = {};
    (this.D.routes||[]).forEach(r => { if (!seenRoutes[r.route]) seenRoutes[r.route] = r; });
    const routeObj = seenRoutes[sel];

    // Auto-fill colour
    if (routeObj && routeObj.colour) {
      document.getElementById('cm-colour').value = routeObj.colour;
      document.getElementById('cm-colour-picker').value = /^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(routeObj.colour) ? routeObj.colour : '#94a3b8';
    }
    // Auto-fill display name
    if (routeObj && routeObj.displayName) {
      document.getElementById('cm-display-name').value = routeObj.displayName;
    }
    // Update suggested order
    const day = document.getElementById('cm-day').value;
    if (document.getElementById('cm-isnew').value) {
      const maxOrder = ((this.D.customers&&this.D.customers.length?this.D.customers:this.PL)||[])
        .filter(c => c.route === sel && c.day === day)
        .reduce((m, c) => Math.max(m, c.order||0), 0);
      document.getElementById('cm-order').value = maxOrder + 1;
    }
  },

  onCustDayChange() {
    if (!document.getElementById('cm-isnew').value) return;
    const route = document.getElementById('cm-route').value;
    const day   = document.getElementById('cm-day').value;
    const maxOrder = ((this.D.customers&&this.D.customers.length?this.D.customers:this.PL)||[])
      .filter(c => c.route === route && c.day === day)
      .reduce((m, c) => Math.max(m, c.order||0), 0);
    document.getElementById('cm-order').value = maxOrder + 1;
  },

  _renderColourSwatches() {
    const seen = {};
    (this.D.routes||[]).forEach(r => {
      if (r.colour && r.colour !== '#94a3b8') seen[r.colour] = r.displayName || r.route;
    });
    ((this.D.customers&&this.D.customers.length?this.D.customers:this.PL)||[]).forEach(c => {
      if (c.colour && c.colour !== '#94a3b8') seen[c.colour] = c.displayName || c.route;
    });
    const swatchEl = document.getElementById('cm-colour-swatches');
    if (!swatchEl) return;
    const entries = Object.entries(seen).slice(0,12);
    swatchEl.innerHTML = entries.map(([col, name]) => {
      const border = this.bgLum(col) > 0.88 ? '1px solid #cbd5e1' : '1px solid transparent';
      return '<button type="button" onclick="App.setCustColour(\'' + col + '\')" '
        + 'title="' + this.esc(name) + ' (' + col + ')" '
        + 'style="width:22px;height:22px;border-radius:5px;background:' + col + ';border:' + border + ';cursor:pointer;flex-shrink:0"></button>';
    }).join('') + (entries.length ? '<span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--txt3);margin-left:2px;align-self:center">existing</span>' : '');
  },

  setCustColour(hex) {
    document.getElementById('cm-colour').value = hex;
    document.getElementById('cm-colour-picker').value = /^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(hex) ? hex : '#94a3b8';
  },

  saveCustomer() {
    const c = {
      isNew:     !!document.getElementById('cm-isnew').value,
      origRoute: document.getElementById('cm-orig-route').value,
      origDay:   document.getElementById('cm-orig-day').value,
      origOrder: parseInt(document.getElementById('cm-orig-order').value)||0,
      order:     parseInt(document.getElementById('cm-order').value)||0,
      route:     document.getElementById('cm-route').value,
      day:       document.getElementById('cm-day').value,
      time:      document.getElementById('cm-time').value.trim(),
      customer:  document.getElementById('cm-customer').value.trim(),
      stops:     parseInt(document.getElementById('cm-stops').value)||1,
      eircode:   document.getElementById('cm-eircode').value.trim(),
      kg:        parseFloat(document.getElementById('cm-kg').value)||0,
      trolleyClothes: document.getElementById('cm-tr-clothes').value.trim(),
      trolleyMop:     document.getElementById('cm-tr-mop').value.trim(),
      colour:      document.getElementById('cm-colour').value.trim(),
      displayName: document.getElementById('cm-display-name').value.trim(),
      notes:     document.getElementById('cm-notes').value.trim()
    };
    if (!c.customer) { this.toast('Customer name is required.','e'); return; }
    if (!c.order)    { this.toast('Delivery order # is required.','e'); return; }
    this._runBtn(document.getElementById('save-customer-btn'), 'Saving...', done => {
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(r => {
          done(r.ok);
          if (r.ok) {
            this.toast('Stop saved!','s');
            this.closeModal('customer-modal');
            if (typeof window.invalidateDistributionCustomerCache === 'function') window.invalidateDistributionCustomerCache();
            // One refresh call now returns customers + routes + overlays in parallel.
            // Current filter values stay in the DOM and are therefore preserved.
            this._plLoadedAt = 0;
            this.renderCustomers(true);
          } else { this.toast('Error: '+r.msg,'e'); }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(e => { done(false); this.toast(e.message,'e'); })
        .saveCustomer(c);
    });
  },

  printCustomers() {
    const rf = (document.getElementById('route-route-filter')||{}).value || '';
    const df = (document.getElementById('route-day-filter')||{}).value   || '';
    // Use the data actually loaded on the planner screen (this.PL); fall back to D.customers.
    let custs = (this.PL && this.PL.length) ? this.PL : (this.D.customers || []);
    if (!custs.length) {
      this.toast('No stops to print yet. Open the Routes screen so the data loads, then try again.', 'e');
      return;
    }
    if (rf) custs = custs.filter(c => String(c.route) === String(rf));
    if (df) custs = custs.filter(c => String(c.day).toLowerCase().trim() === String(df).toLowerCase().trim());
    if (!custs.length) {
      this.toast('No stops match the current filters.', 'e');
      return;
    }

    const title = 'Route Stops' + (rf ? ' — Route '+rf : '') + (df ? ' — '+df : '');
    let rows = '';

    // Print order is operational, not alphabetical:
    //   Route ascending → Monday..Saturday → DeliveryOrder ascending.
    // This keeps every route together for the full working week before moving
    // to the next route number (e.g. 610 Mon..Sat, then 611 Mon..Sat, etc.).
    const PRINT_DAY_ORDER = ['Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday'];
    const PRINT_DAY_RANK = PRINT_DAY_ORDER.reduce((m, day, idx) => {
      m[String(day).toLowerCase()] = idx;
      return m;
    }, {});
    const routePrintCompare = (a, b) => {
      const ar = String(a.route == null ? '' : a.route).trim();
      const br = String(b.route == null ? '' : b.route).trim();
      const an = Number(ar), bn = Number(br);
      const aNumeric = ar !== '' && Number.isFinite(an);
      const bNumeric = br !== '' && Number.isFinite(bn);
      if (aNumeric && bNumeric && an !== bn) return an - bn;
      if (aNumeric !== bNumeric) return aNumeric ? -1 : 1;
      return ar.localeCompare(br, 'en', { numeric:true, sensitivity:'base' });
    };
    const dayPrintRank = day => {
      const key = String(day || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(PRINT_DAY_RANK, key) ? PRINT_DAY_RANK[key] : 999;
    };

    // Group by route+day.
    const groups = {};
    custs.forEach(c => {
      const k = c.route + '|' + c.day;
      if (!groups[k]) groups[k] = { route: c.route, day: c.day, colour: c.colour, displayName: c.displayName, stops: [] };
      groups[k].stops.push(c);
    });
    Object.values(groups).sort((a,b) => {
      const routeCmp = routePrintCompare(a, b);
      if (routeCmp) return routeCmp;
      const dayCmp = dayPrintRank(a.day) - dayPrintRank(b.day);
      if (dayCmp) return dayCmp;
      return String(a.day || '').localeCompare(String(b.day || ''), 'en', { sensitivity:'base' });
    }).forEach(g => {
      g.stops.sort((a,b) => { const ao=Number(a.order)>0?Number(a.order):9999; const bo=Number(b.order)>0?Number(b.order):9999; return ao-bo; });
      const bg = g.colour || '#94a3b8';
      const isLight = this.bgLum(bg) > 0.88;
      const border = isLight ? ';border:1px solid #94a3b8' : '';
      rows += '<tr><td colspan="7" style="background:'+bg+';color:'+this.textColor(bg)+border+';font-weight:800;padding:5px 8px;font-size:11px">'
        + '#'+g.route+' &mdash; '+(g.displayName||g.route)+' &mdash; '+g.day+'</td></tr>';
      g.stops.forEach((s,si) => {
        rows += '<tr><td style="text-align:center;color:#64748b;font-size:11px">'+(s.order||si+1)+'</td>'
          + '<td style="font-weight:600">'+this.esc(s.customer)+((s.overlay&&s.overlay.kind==='EXTERNAL_DELIVERY')?'<span class="print-collection" style="background:#ede9fe;color:#6d28d9">EXTERNAL DELIVERY</span>':((s.overlay&&(s.overlay.kind==='COLLECTION'||s.overlay.kind==='TEMP_STOP'))?'<span class="print-collection">COLLECTION</span>':''))+'</td>'
          + '<td>'+this.esc(s.time||'')+'</td>'
          + '<td>'+this.esc(s.eircode||'')+'</td>'
          + '<td>'+this.esc(s.kg?s.kg+' kg':'')+'</td>'
          + '<td>'+this.esc(s.trolleyClothes||'')+'</td>'
          + '<td>'+this.esc(s.trolleyMop||'')+'</td></tr>';
      });
    });

    const html = '<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="UTF-8"><title>'+title+'</title>'
      + '<style>@page{size:A4;margin:12mm}body{font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:11px;color:#0f172a}'
      + 'h2{font-size:14px;font-weight:800;margin:0 0 4px}.sub{font-size:10px;color:#64748b;margin-bottom:12px}'
      + 'table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%}th{background:#1e293b;color:#fff;padding:5px 6px;font-size:10px;text-align:left;border:1px solid #334155}'
      + 'td{padding:4px 6px;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;vertical-align:top}.print-collection{display:inline-block;margin-left:6px;padding:1px 5px;border-radius:999px;background:#ffedd5;color:#c2410c;border:1px solid #fdba74;font-size:8px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:.3px}'
      + 'tr:nth-child(even) td{background:#f8fafc}'
      + '@media print{body{-webkit-print-color-adjust:exact;print-color-adjust:exact}}</style>'
      + '</head><body>'
      + '<h2>'+title+'</h2>'
      + '<div class="sub">Printed '+new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-IE')+'</div>'
      + '<table><thead><tr><th style="width:28px">#</th><th>Customer</th><th style="width:55px">Time</th>'
      + '<th style="width:80px">Eircode</th><th style="width:55px">KG</th><th style="width:60px">Clothes</th><th style="width:60px">MOP</th></tr></thead>'
      + '<tbody>'+rows+'</tbody></table>'
      + '</body></html>';

    const w = window.open('', '_blank', 'width=900,height=700');
    if (!w) { this.toast('Pop-up blocked. Allow pop-ups for this site to print.', 'e'); return; }
    w.document.open();
    w.document.write(html);
    w.document.close();
    setTimeout(() => { try { w.focus(); w.print(); } catch (e) {} }, 250);
  },

  confirmDeleteCustomer(route, day, order, name) {
    document.getElementById('confirm-msg').textContent =
      'Delete stop for "' + name + '" (Route ' + route + ', ' + day + ', #' + order + ')?';
    document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn').onclick = () => {
      const okBtn = document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn');
      this._runBtn(okBtn, 'Deleting…', done => {
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(r => {
            done(r && r.ok);
            if (r && r.ok) {
              this.toast('Stop deleted.','i');
              this.closeModal('confirm-modal');
              if (typeof window.invalidateDistributionCustomerCache === 'function') window.invalidateDistributionCustomerCache();
              this._plLoadedAt = 0;
              this.renderCustomers(true);
            } else { this.toast('Error: '+((r && r.msg) || 'Could not delete stop'),'e'); }
          })
          .withFailureHandler(e => { done(false); this.toast(e.message, 'e'); })
          .deleteCustomer(route, day, order);
      });
    };
    this.openModal('confirm-modal');
  },

  _resetRouteFilter() {
    // Legacy alias retained for backward-compatible callbacks.
    this._resetCustFilter();
  },

  _resetCustFilter() {
    var csel = document.getElementById('route-route-filter');
    if (csel) while(csel.options.length > 1) csel.remove(csel.options.length - 1);
  },

  // Print
  printRoster() {
    const ws        = this.roster.weekStart;
    const dates     = this.getWeekDates(ws);
    const asgns     = this.roster.assignments || [];
    const viewerUrl = this.D.viewerUrl || '';
    const STATUS = ['Off','Hols','Training','Absent','Sick'];

    // Build badge HTML for print (inline styles, no JS)
    const badgePrint = (val, day, driverId) => {
      if (!val) return '<span style="color:#ccc">—</span>';
      if (val === 'Off')      return '<span style="background:#e2e8f0;color:#475569;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:4px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700">Off</span>';
      if (val === 'Hols')     return '<span style="background:#fce7f3;color:#be185d;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:4px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700">Hols</span>';
      if (val === 'Training') return '<span style="background:#dbeafe;color:#1d4ed8;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:4px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700">Training</span>';
      if (val === 'Absent')   return '<span style="background:#fef3c7;color:#92400e;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:4px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700">Absent</span>';
      if (val === 'Sick')     return '<span style="background:#fee2e2;color:#b91c1c;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:4px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700">Sick</span>';
      const { route, vehicle } = this.parseCell(val);
      const rt  = this.findRoute(val);
      const bg  = rt ? rt.colour : '#94a3b8';
      const tx  = this.textColor(bg);
      const lum = this.bgLum(bg);
      const brd = lum > 0.88 ? `border:1.5px solid #94a3b8;` : '';
      const num = rt ? rt.route : route;
      const rtBadge = `<span style="background:${bg};color:${tx};${brd}padding:2px 10px;border-radius:4px;font-size:12px;font-weight:800;display:inline-block">${this.esc(num)}</span>`;
      const secondTurn = vehicle ? this._vehicleSecondTurn(day, driverId, vehicle) : false;
      const vLabel  = vehicle ? `<div style="font-size:9px;color:#475569;font-weight:700;margin-top:1px;letter-spacing:.2px">${this.esc(vehicle)}${secondTurn?' <span style="display:inline-block;background:#dbeafe;color:#1d4ed8;border:1px solid #93c5fd;border-radius:999px;padding:0 4px;font-size:8px">2T</span>':''}</div>` : '';
      return `<div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center">${rtBadge}${vLabel}</div>`;
    };

    // Routes planned per day
    const planned = this.DAYS.map(d =>
      asgns.filter(a => {
        const v = a[d]; if (!v) return false;
        const {route} = this.parseCell(v);
        return route && !STATUS.includes(route);
      }).length
    );
    const totalDays = asgns.reduce((sum, a) =>
      sum + this.DAYS.filter(d => {
        const v = a[d]; if (!v) return false;
        const {route} = this.parseCell(v);
        return route && !STATUS.includes(route);
      }).length, 0);

    let rows = '';
    const drivers = this._rosterDrivers || this.D.drivers;
    drivers.forEach((drv, idx) => {
      const a = asgns.find(x => String(x.driverId).trim() === String(drv.id).trim())
              || {mon:'',tue:'',wed:'',thu:'',fri:'',sat:'',sun:''};
      const daysW = this.DAYS.filter(d => {
        const v = a[d]; if (!v) return false;
        const {route} = this.parseCell(v);
        if (route === 'Training') return true; // Training counts as working day
        return route && !STATUS.includes(route);
      }).length;
      rows += '<tr>'
        + '<td class="dn">' + this.esc(drv.name) + (drv._historical?'<span style="font-size:8px;color:#94a3b8;margin-left:3px">(removed)</span>':'') + '</td>'
        + this.DAYS.map(function(d) { return '<td>' + badgePrint(a[d]||'', d, drv.id) + '</td>'; }).join('')
        + '<td style="text-align:center;font-weight:800">' + (daysW||'') + '</td>'
        + '</tr>';
    });


    const html = '<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="UTF-8">'
      + '<title>Roster — Week of ' + this.fmtFull(dates[0]) + '</title>'
      + '<style>'
      + '@page { size: A4 landscape; margin: 0; }'
      + '* { box-sizing: border-box; }'
      + 'html, body { margin: 0; padding: 0; background: #fff; }'
      + '#scale-wrap { width: 277mm; transform-origin: top left; padding: 10mm 12mm; font-family: Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 11px; color: #0f172a; }'
      + '.page-hdr { display: flex; align-items: flex-start; justify-content: space-between; margin-bottom: 10px; }'
      + '.hdr-left { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; }'
      + '.hdr-left img { height: 26px; }'
      + 'h1 { font-size: 13px; font-weight: 800; margin: 0 0 2px; }'
      + '.sub { font-size: 9px; color: #64748b; }'
      + '.qr-block { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius: 6px; padding: 5px 9px; background: #f8fafc; }'
      + '.qr-label { font-size: 9px; color: #64748b; line-height: 1.4; max-width: 85px; }'
      + '.qr-label strong { display: block; font-size: 10px; color: #1e293b; margin-bottom: 2px; }'
      + 'table { border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; table-layout: fixed; }'
      + 'th { background: #1e293b; color: #fff; padding: 4px 2px; font-size: 9.5px; font-weight: 700; border: 1px solid #334155; text-align: center; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; }'
      + 'th.dc { text-align: left; padding-left: 6px; width: 95px; }'
      + 'td { padding: 2px 2px; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0; text-align: center; vertical-align: middle; }'
      + 'td.dn { text-align: left; padding-left: 6px; font-weight: 600; font-size: 9.5px; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; }'
      + '.tfoot td { background: #f1f5f9; font-weight: 700; font-size: 9.5px; }'
      + '.tfoot td.lbl { text-align: left; padding-left: 6px; color: #64748b; }'
      + '@media print { html, body { -webkit-print-color-adjust: exact; print-color-adjust: exact; } }'
      + '</style>'
      + '</head><body>'
      + '<div id="scale-wrap">'
      + '<div class="page-hdr">'
      + '<div class="hdr-left">'
      + '<img src="https://ie.elis.com/themes/custom/elis/img/logo.svg" alt="Elis">'
      + '<div><h1>Driver Roster &mdash; Week of ' + this.fmtFull(dates[0]) + '</h1>'
      + '<div class="sub">' + this.fmtFull(dates[0]) + ' &mdash; ' + this.fmtFull(dates[6])
      + ' &nbsp;&middot;&nbsp; Printed ' + new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-IE') + '</div></div>'
      + '</div>'
      + (viewerUrl ? '<div class="qr-block"><div id="qr-canvas"></div><div class="qr-label"><strong>Live Roster</strong>Scan to view on your phone.</div></div>' : '')
      + '</div>'
      + '<table>'
      + '<thead><tr>'
      + '<th class="dc">Driver</th>'
      + this.DAY_LABELS.map(function(l,i){ return '<th>'+l+'<br><span style="font-weight:400;font-size:8px">'+this.fmtShort(dates[i])+'</span></th>'; }, this).join('')
      + '<th style="width:30px">Days</th>'
      + '</tr></thead>'
      + '<tbody>' + rows + '</tbody>'
      + '<tfoot><tr class="tfoot">'
      + '<td class="lbl">Routes Planned</td>'
      + planned.map(function(c){ return '<td>'+c+'</td>'; }).join('')
      + '<td style="font-weight:800">' + totalDays + '</td>'
      + '</tr></tfoot>'
      + '</table>'
      + '</div>'
      + '</body></html>';

    const w = window.open('', '_blank', 'width=1100,height=750');
    if (!w) { this.toast('Pop-up blocked. Allow pop-ups for this site to print.', 'e'); return; }
    w.document.open();
    w.document.write(html);
    w.document.close();

    const finishRosterPrint = () => {
      try {
        if (viewerUrl && w.QRCode && w.document.getElementById('qr-canvas')) {
          new w.QRCode(w.document.getElementById('qr-canvas'), {
            text: viewerUrl,
            width: 64,
            height: 64,
            colorDark: '#1e293b',
            colorLight: '#ffffff',
            correctLevel: w.QRCode.CorrectLevel.M
          });
        }
        const wrap = w.document.getElementById('scale-wrap');
        if (wrap) {
          const maxPxH = 190 * 96 / 25.4;
          const actualH = wrap.scrollHeight;
          if (actualH > maxPxH) {
            const scale = maxPxH / actualH;
            wrap.style.transform = 'scale(' + scale + ')';
            wrap.style.transformOrigin = 'top left';
            w.document.body.style.height = (actualH * scale) + 'px';
          }
        }
        w.focus();
        w.print();
      } catch (e) {
        try { w.print(); } catch (ignored) {}
      }
    };

    if (viewerUrl) {
      const qrLoader = w.document.createElement('script');
      qrLoader.src = 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/qrcodejs/1.0.0/qrcode.min.js';
      qrLoader.onload = () => setTimeout(finishRosterPrint, 80);
      qrLoader.onerror = () => setTimeout(finishRosterPrint, 80);
      w.document.head.appendChild(qrLoader);
    } else {
      setTimeout(finishRosterPrint, 250);
    }
  },
  loadLeaveSupervisorEmail() {
    const input = document.getElementById('leave-supervisor-email');
    if (!input || input.dataset.loaded) return;
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(email => { input.value = email || ''; input.dataset.loaded = '1'; })
      .withFailureHandler(e => { const h=document.getElementById('leave-email-hint'); if(h) h.textContent='Could not load email: '+e.message; })
      .getLeaveSupervisorEmailSetting();
  },

  saveLeaveSupervisorEmail(ev) {
    const input = document.getElementById('leave-supervisor-email');
    const email = input ? input.value.trim() : '';
    if (!email || email.indexOf('@') === -1) { this.toast('Enter a valid supervisor email.', 'e'); return; }
    this.runBusy(ev, 'Saving…', run => run.setLeaveSupervisorEmail(email),
      () => { this.toast('Supervisor email saved.', 's'); if (input) input.dataset.loaded='1'; });
  },

  copyUrl() {
    const url = document.getElementById('viewer-url-text').textContent;
    if (navigator.clipboard) {
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(url).then(() => this.toast('Link copied!', 's'));
    } else {
      const el = document.createElement('textarea'); el.value = url;
      document.body.appendChild(el); el.select(); document.execCommand('copy');
      document.body.removeChild(el); this.toast('Link copied!', 's');
    }
  },

  openModal(id)  { document.getElementById(id).classList.add('open'); },
  closeModal(id) { document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('open'); },

  // Returns #000 for light backgrounds, #fff for dark — ensures readable text on any route colour
  textColor(hex) {
    return this.bgLum(hex) > 0.55 ? '#000000' : '#ffffff';
  },

  bgLum(hex) {
    try {
      const h=(hex||'').replace('#','');
      const r=parseInt(h.slice(0,2),16),g=parseInt(h.slice(2,4),16),b=parseInt(h.slice(4,6),16);
      return (0.299*r+0.587*g+0.114*b)/255;
    } catch(e){ return 0; }
  },

  esc(s) {
    return String(s||'').replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
  },

  openHelp() { this.openModal('help-modal'); },

  toast(msg, type='i') {
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'toast ' + type;
    el.textContent = msg;
    document.getElementById('toasts').appendChild(el);
    setTimeout(() => { el.style.transition = 'opacity .3s'; el.style.opacity = '0'; }, 3000);
    setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 3400);
  },

  // ── Button "processing" feedback ──────────────────────────────
  // Resolve a button from an event/element/id.
  _resolveBtn(target) {
    if (!target) return null;
    if (target.currentTarget) return target.currentTarget;
    if (target.tagName) return target;
    if (typeof target === 'string') return document.getElementById(target);
    return null;
  },

  // Show a spinner + disable a button while a google.script.run call is in flight.
  // Builds on the existing _runBtn so the look is identical everywhere.
  // success/error are your callbacks; the button is ALWAYS restored.
  // build(run) must attach exactly one server method, e.g. run.setDriverRosterHidden(id,h)
  runBusy(target, busyLabel, build, success, error) {
    const btn = this._resolveBtn(target);
    this._runBtn(btn, busyLabel || 'Working…', done => {
      const run = google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(r => { done(true); if (success) success(r); })
        .withFailureHandler(e => { done(false); if (error) error(e); else this.toast((e && e.message) || 'Something went wrong', 'e'); });
      build(run);
    });
  }
};


document.querySelectorAll('.modal-overlay').forEach(ov => {
  ov.addEventListener('click', e => { if (e.target === ov) ov.classList.remove('open'); });
});

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => App.init());
</script>
